4|135|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo koonoo qawwameena bilqisti shuhadaa lillahi walaw ‘ala anfusikum awi alwalidayni walaqrabeena in yakun ghaniyyan aw faqeeran fallahu awla bihima fala tattabi‘oo alhawa an ta‘diloo wain talwoo aw tu‘ridoo fainna Allaha kana bima ta‘maloona khabeera
4|136|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo aminoo billahi warasoolihi walkitabi allathee nazzala ‘ala rasoolihi walkitabi allathee anzala min qablu waman yakfur billahi wamalaikatihi wakutubihi warusulihi walyawmi alakhiri faqad dalla dalalan ba‘eeda
4|137|Inna allatheena amanoo thumma kafaroo thumma amanoo thumma kafaroo thumma izdadoo kufran lam yakuni Allahu liyaghfira lahum wala liyahdiyahum sabeela
4|138|Bashshiri almunafiqeena bianna lahum ‘athaban aleema
4|139|Allatheena yattakhithoona alkafireena awliyaa min dooni almumineena ayabtaghoona ‘indahumu al‘izzata fainna al‘izzata lillahi jamee‘a
4|140|Waqad nazzala ‘alaykum fee alkitabi an itha sami‘tum ayati Allahi yukfaru biha wayustahzao biha fala taq‘udoo ma‘ahum hatta yakhoodoo fee hadeethin ghayrihi innakum ithan mithluhum inna Allaha jami‘u almunafiqeena walkafireena fee jahannama jamee‘a
4|141|Allatheena yatarabbasoona bikum fain kana lakum fathun mina Allahi qaloo alam nakun ma‘akum wain kana lilkafireena naseebun qaloo alam nastahwith ‘alaykum wanamna‘kum mina almumineena fallahu yahkumu baynakum yawma alqiyamati walan yaj‘ala Allahu lilkafireena ‘ala almumineena sabeela
4|142|Inna almunafiqeena yukhadi‘oona Allaha wahuwa khadi‘uhum waitha qamoo ila assalati qamoo kusala yuraoona annasa wala yathkuroona Allaha illa qaleela
4|143|Muthabthabeena bayna thalika la ila haolai wala ila haolai waman yudlili Allahu falan tajida lahu sabeela
4|144|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo la tattakhithoo alkafireena awliyaa min dooni almumineena atureedoona an taj‘aloo lillahi ‘alaykum sultanan mubeena
4|145|Inna almunafiqeena fee addarki alasfali mina annari walan tajida lahum naseera
4|146|Illa allatheena taboo waaslahoo wa‘tasamoo billahi waakhlasoo deenahum lillahi faolaika ma‘a almumineena wasawfa yuti Allahu almumineena ajran ‘atheema
4|147|Ma yaf‘alu Allahu bi‘athabikum in shakartum waamantum wakana Allahu shakiran ‘aleema
4|148|La yuhibbu Allahu aljahra bissooi mina alqawli illa man thulima wakana Allahu samee‘an ‘aleema
4|149|In tubdoo khayran aw tukhfoohu aw ta‘foo ‘an sooin fainna Allaha kana ‘afuwwan qadeera
4|150|Inna allatheena yakfuroona billahi warusulihi wayureedoona an yufarriqoo bayna Allahi warusulihi wayaqooloona numinu biba‘din wanakfuru biba‘din wayureedoona an yattakhithoo bayna thalika sabeela
4|151|Olaika humu alkafiroona haqqan waa‘tadna lilkafireena ‘athaban muheena
4|152|Wallatheena amanoo billahi warusulihi walam yufarriqoo bayna ahadin minhum olaika sawfa yuteehim ojoorahum wakana Allahu ghafooran raheema
4|153|Yasaluka ahlu alkitabi an tunazzila ‘alayhim kitaban mina assamai faqad saaloo moosa akbara min thalika faqaloo arina Allaha jahratan faakhathathumu assa‘iqatu bithulmihim thumma ittakhathoo al‘ijla min ba‘di ma jaathumu albayyinatu fa‘afawna ‘an thalika waatayna moosa sultanan mubeena
4|154|Warafa‘na fawqahumu attoora bimeethaqihim waqulna lahumu odkhuloo albaba sujjadan waqulna lahum la ta‘doo fee assabti waakhathna minhum meethaqan ghaleetha
4|155|Fabima naqdihim meethaqahum wakufrihim biayati Allahi waqatlihimu alanbiyaa bighayri haqqin waqawlihim quloobuna ghulfun bal taba‘a Allahu ‘alayha bikufrihim fala yuminoona illa qaleela
4|156|Wabikufrihim waqawlihim ‘ala maryama buhtanan ‘atheema
4|157|Waqawlihim inna qatalna almaseeha ‘eesa ibna maryama rasoola Allahi wama qataloohu wama salaboohu walakin shubbiha lahum wainna allatheena ikhtalafoo feehi lafee shakkin minhu ma lahum bihi min ‘ilmin illa ittiba‘a aththanni wama qataloohu yaqeena
4|158|Bal rafa‘ahu Allahu ilayhi wakana Allahu ‘azeezan hakeema
4|159|Wain min ahli alkitabi illa layuminanna bihi qabla mawtihi wayawma alqiyamati yakoonu ‘alayhim shaheeda
4|160|Fabithulmin mina allatheena hadoo harramna ‘alayhim tayyibatin ohillat lahum wabisaddihim ‘an sabeeli Allahi katheera
4|161|Waakhthihimu arriba waqad nuhoo ‘anhu waaklihim amwala annasi bilbatili waa‘tadna lilkafireena minhum ‘athaban aleema
4|162|Lakini arrasikhoona fee al‘ilmi minhum walmuminoona yuminoona bima onzila ilayka wama onzila min qablika walmuqeemeena assalata walmutoona azzakata walmuminoona billahi walyawmi alakhiri olaika sanuteehim ajran ‘atheema
4|163|Inna awhayna ilayka kama awhayna ila noohin wannabiyyeena min ba‘dihi waawhayna ila ibraheema waisma‘eela waishaqa waya‘qooba walasbati wa‘eesa waayyooba wayoonusa waharoona wasulaymana waatayna dawooda zaboora
4|164|Warusulan qad qasasnahum ‘alayka min qablu warusulan lam naqsushum ‘alayka wakallama Allahu moosa takleema
4|165|Rusulan mubashshireena wamunthireena lialla yakoona linnasi ‘ala Allahi hujjatun ba‘da arrusuli wakana Allahu ‘azeezan hakeema
4|166|Lakini Allahu yashhadu bima anzala ilayka anzalahu bi‘ilmihi walmalaikatu yashhadoona wakafa billahi shaheeda
4|167|Inna allatheena kafaroo wasaddoo ‘an sabeeli Allahi qad dalloo dalalan ba‘eeda
4|168|Inna allatheena kafaroo wathalamoo lam yakuni Allahu liyaghfira lahum wala liyahdiyahum tareeqa
4|169|Illa tareeqa jahannama khalideena feeha abadan wakana thalika ‘ala Allahi yaseera
4|170|Ya ayyuha annasu qad jaakumu arrasoolu bilhaqqi min rabbikum faaminoo khayran lakum wain takfuroo fainna lillahi ma fee assamawati walardi wakana Allahu ‘aleeman hakeema
4|171|Ya ahla alkitabi la taghloo fee deenikum wala taqooloo ‘ala Allahi illa alhaqqa innama almaseehu ‘eesa ibnu maryama rasoolu Allahi wakalimatuhu alqaha ila maryama waroohun minhu faaminoo billahi warusulihi wala taqooloo thalathatun intahoo khayran lakum innama Allahu ilahun wahidun subhanahu an yakoona lahu waladun lahu ma fee assamawati wama fee alardi wakafa billahi wakeela
4|172|Lan yastankifa almaseehu an yakoona ‘abdan lillahi wala almalaikatu almuqarraboona waman yastankif ‘an ‘ibadatihi wayastakbir fasayahshuruhum ilayhi jamee‘a
4|173|Faamma allatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati fayuwaffeehim ojoorahum wayazeeduhum min fadlihi waamma allatheena istankafoo wastakbaroo fayu‘aththibuhum ‘athaban aleeman wala yajidoona lahum min dooni Allahi waliyyan wala naseera
4|174|Ya ayyuha annasu qad jaakum burhanun min rabbikum waanzalna ilaykum nooran mubeena
4|175|Faamma allatheena amanoo billahi wa‘tasamoo bihi fasayudkhiluhum fee rahmatin minhu wafadlin wayahdeehim ilayhi siratan mustaqeema
4|176|Yastaftoonaka quli Allahu yufteekum fee alkalalati ini imruon halaka laysa lahu waladun walahu okhtun falaha nisfu ma taraka wahuwa yarithuha in lam yakun laha waladun fain kanata ithnatayni falahuma aththuluthani mimma taraka wain kanoo ikhwatan rijalan wanisaan faliththakari mithlu haththi alonthayayni yubayyinu Allahu lakum an tadilloo wallahu bikulli shayin ‘aleem
5|1|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo awfoo bil‘uqoodi ohillat lakum baheematu alan‘ami illa ma yutla ‘alaykum ghayra muhillee assaydi waantum hurumun inna Allaha yahkumu ma yureed
5|2|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo la tuhilloo sha‘aira Allahi wala ashshahra alharama wala alhadya wala alqalaida wala ammeena albayta alharama yabtaghoona fadlan min rabbihim waridwanan waitha halaltum fastadoo wala yajrimannakum shanaanu qawmin an saddookum ‘ani almasjidi alharami an ta‘tadoo wata‘awanoo ‘ala albirri wattaqwa wala ta‘awanoo ‘ala alithmi wal‘udwani wattaqoo Allaha inna Allaha shadeedu al‘iqab
5|3|Hurrimat ‘alaykumu almaytatu waddamu walahmu alkhinzeeri wama ohilla lighayri Allahi bihi walmunkhaniqatu walmawqoothatu walmutaraddiyatu wannateehatu wama akala assabu‘u illa ma thakkaytum wama thubiha ‘ala annusubi waan tastaqsimoo bilazlami thalikum fisqun alyawma yaisa allatheena kafaroo min deenikum fala takhshawhum wakhshawni alyawma akmaltu lakum deenakum waatmamtu ‘alaykum ni‘matee waradeetu lakumu alislama deenan famani idturra fee makhmasatin ghayra mutajanifin liithmin fainna Allaha ghafoorun raheem
5|4|Yasaloonaka matha ohilla lahum qul ohilla lakumu attayyibatu wama ‘allamtum mina aljawarihi mukallibeena tu‘allimoonahunna mimma ‘allamakumu Allahu fakuloo mimma amsakna ‘alaykum wathkuroo isma Allahi ‘alayhi wattaqoo Allaha inna Allaha saree‘u alhisab
5|5|Alyawma ohilla lakumu attayyibatu wata‘amu allatheena ootoo alkitaba hillun lakum wata‘amukum hillun lahum walmuhsanatu mina almuminati walmuhsanatu mina allatheena ootoo alkitaba min qablikum itha ataytumoohunna ojoorahunna muhsineena ghayra musafiheena wala muttakhithee akhdanin waman yakfur bileemani faqad habita ‘amaluhu wahuwa fee alakhirati mina alkhasireen
5|6|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo itha qumtum ila assalati faghsiloo wujoohakum waaydiyakum ila almarafiqi wamsahoo biruoosikum waarjulakum ila alka‘bayni wain kuntum junuban fattahharoo wain kuntum marda aw ‘ala safarin aw jaa ahadun minkum mina alghaiti aw lamastumu annisaa falam tajidoo maan fatayammamoo sa‘eedan tayyiban famsahoo biwujoohikum waaydeekum minhu ma yureedu Allahu liyaj‘ala ‘alaykum min harajin walakin yureedu liyutahhirakum waliyutimma ni‘matahu ‘alaykum la‘allakum tashkuroon
5|7|Wathkuroo ni‘mata Allahi ‘alaykum wameethaqahu allathee wathaqakum bihi ith qultum sami‘na waata‘na wattaqoo Allaha inna Allaha ‘aleemun bithati assudoor
5|8|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo koonoo qawwameena lillahi shuhadaa bilqisti wala yajrimannakum shanaanu qawmin ‘ala alla ta‘diloo i‘diloo huwa aqrabu littaqwa wattaqoo Allaha inna Allaha khabeerun bima ta‘maloon
5|9|Wa‘ada Allahu allatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati lahum maghfiratun waajrun ‘atheem
5|10|Wallatheena kafaroo wakaththaboo biayatina olaika ashabu aljaheem
5|11|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo othkuroo ni‘mata Allahi ‘alaykum ith hamma qawmun an yabsutoo ilaykum aydiyahum fakaffa aydiyahum ‘ankum wattaqoo Allaha wa‘ala Allahi falyatawakkali almuminoon
5|12|Walaqad akhatha Allahu meethaqa banee israeela waba‘athna minhumu ithnay ‘ashara naqeeban waqala Allahu innee ma‘akum lain aqamtumu assalata waataytumu azzakata waamantum birusulee wa‘azzartumoohum waaqradtumu Allaha qardan hasanan laokaffiranna ‘ankum sayyiatikum walaodkhilannakum jannatin tajree min tahtiha alanharu faman kafara ba‘da thalika minkum faqad dalla sawaa assabeel
5|13|Fabima naqdihim meethaqahum la‘annahum waja‘alna quloobahum qasiyatan yuharrifoona alkalima ‘an mawadi‘ihi wanasoo haththan mimma thukkiroo bihi wala tazalu tattali‘u ‘ala khainatin minhum illa qaleelan minhum fa‘fu ‘anhum wasfah inna Allaha yuhibbu almuhsineen
5|14|Wamina allatheena qaloo inna nasara akhathna meethaqahum fanasoo haththan mimma thukkiroo bihi faaghrayna baynahumu al‘adawata walbaghdaa ila yawmi alqiyamati wasawfa yunabbiohumu Allahu bima kanoo yasna‘oon
5|15|Ya ahla alkitabi qad jaakum rasooluna yubayyinu lakum katheeran mimma kuntum tukhfoona mina alkitabi waya‘foo ‘an katheerin qad jaakum mina Allahi noorun wakitabun mubeen
5|16|Yahdee bihi Allahu mani ittaba‘a ridwanahu subula assalami wayukhrijuhum mina aththulumati ila annoori biithnihi wayahdeehim ila siratin mustaqeem
5|17|Laqad kafara allatheena qaloo inna Allaha huwa almaseehu ibnu maryama qul faman yamliku mina Allahi shayan in arada an yuhlika almaseeha ibna maryama waommahu waman fee alardi jamee‘an walillahi mulku assamawati walardi wama baynahuma yakhluqu ma yashao wallahu ‘ala kulli shayin qadeer
5|18|Waqalati alyahoodu wannasara nahnu abnao Allahi waahibbaohu qul falima yu‘aththibukum bithunoobikum bal antum basharun mimman khalaqa yaghfiru liman yashao wayu‘aththibu man yashao walillahi mulku assamawati walardi wama baynahuma wailayhi almaseer
5|19|Ya ahla alkitabi qad jaakum rasooluna yubayyinu lakum ‘ala fatratin mina arrusuli an taqooloo ma jaana min basheerin wala natheerin faqad jaakum basheerun wanatheerun wallahu ‘ala kulli shayin qadeer
5|20|Waith qala moosa liqawmihi ya qawmi othkuroo ni‘mata Allahi ‘alaykum ith ja‘ala feekum anbiyaa waja‘alakum mulookan waatakum ma lam yuti ahadan mina al‘alameen
5|21|Ya qawmi odkhuloo alarda almuqaddasata allatee kataba Allahu lakum wala tartaddoo ‘ala adbarikum fatanqaliboo khasireen
5|22|Qaloo ya moosa inna feeha qawman jabbareena wainna lan nadkhulaha hatta yakhrujoo minha fain yakhrujoo minha fainna dakhiloon
5|23|Qala rajulani mina allatheena yakhafoona an‘ama Allahu ‘alayhima odkhuloo ‘alayhimu albaba faitha dakhaltumoohu fainnakum ghaliboona wa‘ala Allahi fatawakkaloo in kuntum mumineen
5|24|Qaloo ya moosa inna lan nadkhulaha abadan ma damoo feeha faithhab anta warabbuka faqatila inna hahuna qa‘idoon
5|25|Qala rabbi innee la amliku illa nafsee waakhee fafruq baynana wabayna alqawmi alfasiqeen
5|26|Qala fainnaha muharramatun ‘alayhim arba‘eena sanatan yateehoona fee alardi fala tasa ‘ala alqawmi alfasiqeen
5|27|Watlu ‘alayhim nabaa ibnay adama bilhaqqi ith qarraba qurbanan fatuqubbila min ahadihima walam yutaqabbal mina alakhari qala laaqtulannaka qala innama yataqabbalu Allahu mina almuttaqeen
5|28|Lain basatta ilayya yadaka litaqtulanee ma ana bibasitin yadiya ilayka liaqtulaka innee akhafu Allaha rabba al‘alameen
5|29|Innee oreedu an tabooa biithmee waithmika fatakoona min ashabi annari wathalika jazao aththalimeen
5|30|Fatawwa‘at lahu nafsuhu qatla akheehi faqatalahu faasbaha mina alkhasireen
5|31|Faba‘atha Allahu ghuraban yabhathu fee alardi liyuriyahu kayfa yuwaree sawata akheehi qala ya waylata a‘ajaztu an akoona mithla hatha alghurabi faowariya sawata akhee faasbaha mina annadimeen
5|32|Min ajli thalika katabna ‘ala banee israeela annahu man qatala nafsan bighayri nafsin aw fasadin fee alardi fakaannama qatala annasa jamee‘an waman ahyaha fakaannama ahya annasa jamee‘an walaqad jaa thum rusuluna bilbayyinati thumma inna katheeran minhum ba‘da thalika fee alardi lamusrifoon
5|33|Innama jazao allatheena yuhariboona Allaha warasoolahu wayas‘awna fee alardi fasadan an yuqattaloo aw yusallaboo aw tuqatta‘a aydeehim waarjuluhum min khilafin aw yunfaw mina alardi thalika lahum khizyun fee addunya walahum fee alakhirati ‘athabun ‘atheem
5|34|Illa allatheena taboo min qabli an taqdiroo ‘alayhim fa‘lamoo anna Allaha ghafoorun raheem
5|35|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo ittaqoo Allaha wabtaghoo ilayhi alwaseelata wajahidoo fee sabeelihi la‘allakum tuflihoon
5|36|Inna allatheena kafaroo law anna lahum ma fee alardi jamee‘an wamithlahu ma‘ahu liyaftadoo bihi min ‘athabi yawmi alqiyamati ma tuqubbila minhum walahum ‘athabun aleem
5|37|Yureedoona an yakhrujoo mina annari wama hum bikharijeena minha walahum ‘athabun muqeem
5|38|Wassariqu wassariqatu faqta‘oo aydiyahuma jazaan bima kasaba nakalan mina Allahi wallahu ‘azeezun hakeem
5|39|Faman taba min ba‘di thulmihi waaslaha fainna Allaha yatoobu ‘alayhi inna Allaha ghafoorun raheem
5|40|Alam ta‘lam anna Allaha lahu mulku assamawati walardi yu‘aththibu man yashao wayaghfiru liman yashao wallahu ‘ala kulli shayin qadeer
5|41|Ya ayyuha arrasoolu la yahzunka allatheena yusari‘oona fee alkufri mina allatheena qaloo amanna biafwahihim walam tumin quloobuhum wamina allatheena hadoo samma‘oona lilkathibi samma‘oona liqawmin akhareena lam yatooka yuharrifoona alkalima min ba‘di mawadi‘ihi yaqooloona in ooteetum hatha fakhuthoohu wain lam tutawhu fahtharoo waman yuridi Allahu fitnatahu falan tamlika lahu mina Allahi shayan olaika allatheena lam yuridi Allahu an yutahhira quloobahum lahum fee addunya khizyun walahum fee alakhirati ‘athabun ‘atheem
5|42|Samma‘oona lilkathibi akkaloona lissuhti fain jaooka fahkum baynahum aw a‘rid ‘anhum wain tu‘rid ‘anhum falan yadurrooka shayan wain hakamta fahkum baynahum bilqisti inna Allaha yuhibbu almuqsiteen
5|43|Wakayfa yuhakkimoonaka wa‘indahumu attawratu feeha hukmu Allahi thumma yatawallawna min ba‘di thalika wama olaika bilmumineen
5|44|Inna anzalna attawrata feeha hudan wanoorun yahkumu biha annabiyyoona allatheena aslamoo lillatheena hadoo warrabbaniyyoona walahbaru bima istuhfithoo min kitabi Allahi wakanoo ‘alayhi shuhadaa fala takhshawoo annasa wakhshawni wala tashtaroo biayatee thamanan qaleelan waman lam yahkum bima anzala Allahu faolaika humu alkafiroon
5|45|Wakatabna ‘alayhim feeha anna annafsa binnafsi wal‘ayna bil‘ayni walanfa bilanfi walothuna bilothuni wassinna bissinni waljurooha qisasun faman tasaddaqa bihi fahuwa kaffaratun lahu waman lam yahkum bima anzala Allahu faolaika humu aththalimoon
5|46|Waqaffayna ‘ala atharihim bi‘eesa ibni maryama musaddiqan lima bayna yadayhi mina attawrati waataynahu alinjeela feehi hudan wanoorun wamusaddiqan lima bayna yadayhi mina attawrati wahudan wamaw‘ithatan lilmuttaqeen
5|47|Walyahkum ahlu alinjeeli bima anzala Allahu feehi waman lam yahkum bima anzala Allahu faolaika humu alfasiqoon
5|48|Waanzalna ilayka alkitaba bilhaqqi musaddiqan lima bayna yadayhi mina alkitabi wamuhayminan ‘alayhi fahkum baynahum bima anzala Allahu wala tattabi‘ ahwaahum ‘amma jaaka mina alhaqqi likullin ja‘alna minkum shir‘atan waminhajan walaw shaa Allahu laja‘alakum ommatan wahidatan walakin liyabluwakum fee ma atakum fastabiqoo alkhayrati ila Allahi marji‘ukum jamee‘an fayunabbiokum bima kuntum feehi takhtalifoon
5|49|Waani ohkum baynahum bima anzala Allahu wala tattabi‘ ahwaahum wahtharhum an yaftinooka ‘an ba‘di ma anzala Allahu ilayka fain tawallaw fa‘lam annama yureedu Allahu an yuseebahum biba‘di thunoobihim wainna katheeran mina annasi lafasiqoon
5|50|Afahukma aljahiliyyati yabghoona waman ahsanu mina Allahu hukman liqawmin yooqinoon
5|51|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo la tattakhithoo alyahooda wannasara awliyaa ba‘duhum awliyao ba‘din waman yatawallahum minkum fainnahu minhum inna Allaha la yahdee alqawma aththalimeen
5|52|Fatara allatheena fee quloobihim maradun yusari‘oona feehim yaqooloona nakhsha an tuseebana dairatun fa‘asa Allahu an yatiya bilfathi aw amrin min ‘indihi fayusbihoo ‘ala ma asarroo fee anfusihim nadimeen
5|53|Wayaqoolu allatheena amanoo ahaolai allatheena aqsamoo billahi jahda aymanihim innahum lama‘akum habitat a‘maluhum faasbahoo khasireen
5|54|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo man yartadda minkum ‘an deenihi fasawfa yatee Allahu biqawmin yuhibbuhum wayuhibboonahu athillatin ‘ala almumineena a‘izzatin ‘ala alkafireena yujahidoona fee sabeeli Allahi wala yakhafoona lawmata laimin thalika fadlu Allahi yuteehi man yashao wallahu wasi‘un ‘aleem
5|55|Innama waliyyukumu Allahu warasooluhu wallatheena amanoo allatheena yuqeemoona assalata wayutoona azzakata wahum raki‘oon
5|56|Waman yatawalla Allaha warasoolahu wallatheena amanoo fainna hizba Allahi humu alghaliboon
5|57|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo la tattakhithoo allatheena ittakhathoo deenakum huzuwan wala‘iban mina allatheena ootoo alkitaba min qablikum walkuffara awliyaa wattaqoo Allaha in kuntum mumineen
5|58|Waitha nadaytum ila assalati ittakhathooha huzuwan wala‘iban thalika biannahum qawmun la ya‘qiloon
5|59|Qul ya ahla alkitabi hal tanqimoona minna illa an amanna billahi wama onzila ilayna wama onzila min qablu waanna aktharakum fasiqoon
5|60|Qul hal onabbiokum bisharrin min thalika mathoobatan ‘inda Allahi man la‘anahu Allahu waghadiba ‘alayhi waja‘ala minhumu alqiradata walkhanazeera wa‘abada attaghooti olaika sharrun makanan waadallu ‘an sawai assabeel
5|61|Waitha jaookum qaloo amanna waqad dakhaloo bilkufri wahum qad kharajoo bihi wallahu a‘lamu bima kanoo yaktumoon
5|62|Watara katheeran minhum yusari‘oona fee alithmi wal‘udwani waaklihimu assuhta labisa ma kanoo ya‘maloon
5|63|Lawla yanhahumu arrabbaniyyoona walahbaru ‘an qawlihimu alithma waaklihimu assuhta labisa ma kanoo yasna‘oon
5|64|Waqalati alyahoodu yadu Allahi maghloolatun ghullat aydeehim walu‘inoo bima qaloo bal yadahu mabsootatani yunfiqu kayfa yashao walayazeedanna katheeran minhum ma onzila ilayka min rabbika tughyanan wakufran waalqayna baynahumu al‘adawata walbaghdaa ila yawmi alqiyamati kullama awqadoo naran lilharbi atfaaha Allahu wayas‘awna fee alardi fasadan wallahu la yuhibbu almufsideen
5|65|Walaw anna ahla alkitabi amanoo wattaqaw lakaffarna ‘anhum sayyiatihim walaadkhalnahum jannati anna‘eem
5|66|Walaw annahum aqamoo attawrata walinjeela wama onzila ilayhim min rabbihim laakaloo min fawqihim wamin tahti arjulihim minhum ommatun muqtasidatun wakatheerun minhum saa ma ya‘maloon
5|67|Ya ayyuha arrasoolu balligh ma onzila ilayka min rabbika wain lam taf‘al fama ballaghta risalatahu wallahu ya‘simuka mina annasi inna Allaha la yahdee alqawma alkafireen
5|68|Qul ya ahla alkitabi lastum ‘ala shayin hatta tuqeemoo attawrata walinjeela wama onzila ilaykum min rabbikum walayazeedanna katheeran minhum ma onzila ilayka min rabbika tughyanan wakufran fala tasa ‘ala alqawmi alkafireen
5|69|Inna allatheena amanoo wallatheena hadoo wassabioona wannasara man amana billahi walyawmi alakhiri wa‘amila salihan fala khawfun ‘alayhim wala hum yahzanoon
5|70|Laqad akhathna meethaqa banee israeela waarsalna ilayhim rusulan kullama jaahum rasoolun bima la tahwa anfusuhum fareeqan kaththaboo wafareeqan yaqtuloon
5|71|Wahasiboo alla takoona fitnatun fa‘amoo wasammoo thumma taba Allahu ‘alayhim thumma ‘amoo wasammoo katheerun minhum wallahu baseerun bima ya‘maloon
5|72|Laqad kafara allatheena qaloo inna Allaha huwa almaseehu ibnu maryama waqala almaseehu ya banee israeela o‘budoo Allaha rabbee warabbakum innahu man yushrik billahi faqad harrama Allahu ‘alayhi aljannata wamawahu annaru wama liththalimeena min ansar
5|73|Laqad kafara allatheena qaloo inna Allaha thalithu thalathatin wama min ilahin illa ilahun wahidun wain lam yantahoo ‘amma yaqooloona layamassanna allatheena kafaroo minhum ‘athabun aleem
5|74|Afala yatooboona ila Allahi wayastaghfiroonahu wallahu ghafoorun raheem
5|75|Ma almaseehu ibnu maryama illa rasoolun qad khalat min qablihi arrusulu waommuhu siddeeqatun kana yakulani atta‘ama onthur kayfa nubayyinu lahumu alayati thumma onthur anna yufakoon
5|76|Qul ata‘budoona min dooni Allahi ma la yamliku lakum darran wala naf‘an wallahu huwa assamee‘u al‘aleem
5|77|Qul ya ahla alkitabi la taghloo fee deenikum ghayra alhaqqi wala tattabi‘oo ahwaa qawmin qad dalloo min qablu waadalloo katheeran wadalloo ‘an sawai assabeel
5|78|Lu‘ina allatheena kafaroo min banee israeela ‘ala lisani dawooda wa‘eesa ibni maryama thalika bima ‘asaw wakanoo ya‘tadoon
5|79|Kanoo la yatanahawna ‘an munkarin fa‘aloohu labisa ma kanoo yaf‘aloon
5|80|Tara katheeran minhum yatawallawna allatheena kafaroo labisa ma qaddamat lahum anfusuhum an sakhita Allahu ‘alayhim wafee al‘athabi hum khalidoon
5|81|Walaw kanoo yuminoona billahi wannabiyyi wama onzila ilayhi ma ittakhathoohum awliyaa walakinna katheeran minhum fasiqoon
5|82|Latajidanna ashadda annasi ‘adawatan lillatheena amanoo alyahooda wallatheena ashrakoo walatajidanna aqrabahum mawaddatan lillatheena amanoo allatheena qaloo inna nasara thalika bianna minhum qisseeseena waruhbanan waannahum la yastakbiroon
5|83|Waitha sami‘oo ma onzila ila arrasooli tara a‘yunahum tafeedu mina addam‘i mimma ‘arafoo mina alhaqqi yaqooloona rabbana amanna faktubna ma‘a ashshahideen
5|84|Wama lana la numinu billahi wama jaana mina alhaqqi wanatma‘u an yudkhilana rabbuna ma‘a alqawmi assaliheen
5|85|Faathabahumu Allahu bima qaloo jannatin tajree min tahtiha alanharu khalideena feeha wathalika jazao almuhsineen
5|86|Wallatheena kafaroo wakaththaboo biayatina olaika ashabu aljaheem
5|87|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo la tuharrimoo tayyibati ma ahalla Allahu lakum wala ta‘tadoo inna Allaha la yuhibbu almu‘tadeen
5|88|Wakuloo mimma razaqakumu Allahu halalan tayyiban wattaqoo Allaha allathee antum bihi muminoon
5|89|La yuakhithukumu Allahu billaghwi fee aymanikum walakin yuakhithukum bima ‘aqqadtumu alaymana fakaffaratuhu it‘amu ‘asharati masakeena min awsati ma tut‘imoona ahleekum aw kiswatuhum aw tahreeru raqabatin faman lam yajid fasiyamu thalathati ayyamin thalika kaffaratu aymanikum itha halaftum wahfathoo aymanakum kathalika yubayyinu Allahu lakum ayatihi la‘allakum tashkuroon
5|90|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo innama alkhamru walmaysiru walansabu walazlamu rijsun min ‘amali ashshaytani fajtaniboohu la‘allakum tuflihoon
5|91|Innama yureedu ashshaytanu an yooqi‘a baynakumu al‘adawata walbaghdaa fee alkhamri walmaysiri wayasuddakum ‘an thikri Allahi wa‘ani assalati fahal antum muntahoon
5|92|Waatee‘oo Allaha waatee‘oo arrasoola wahtharoo fain tawallaytum fa‘lamoo annama ‘ala rasoolina albalaghu almubeen
5|93|Laysa ‘ala allatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati junahun feema ta‘imoo itha ma ittaqaw waamanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati thumma ittaqaw waamanoo thumma ittaqaw waahsanoo wallahu yuhibbu almuhsineen
5|94|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo layabluwannakumu Allahu bishayin mina assaydi tanaluhu aydeekum warimahukum liya‘lama Allahu man yakhafuhu bilghaybi famani i‘tada ba‘da thalika falahu ‘athabun aleem
5|95|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo la taqtuloo assayda waantum hurumun waman qatalahu minkum muta‘ammidan fajazaon mithlu ma qatala mina anna‘ami yahkumu bihi thawa ‘adlin minkum hadyan baligha alka‘bati aw kaffaratun ta‘amu masakeena aw ‘adlu thalika siyaman liyathooqa wabala amrihi ‘afa Allahu ‘amma salafa waman ‘ada fayantaqimu Allahu minhu wallahu ‘azeezun thoo intiqam
5|96|Ohilla lakum saydu albahri wata‘amuhu mata‘an lakum walissayyarati wahurrima ‘alaykum saydu albarri ma dumtum huruman wattaqoo Allaha allathee ilayhi tuhsharoon
5|97|Ja‘ala Allahu alka‘bata albayta alharama qiyaman linnasi washshahra alharama walhadya walqalaida thalika lita‘lamoo anna Allaha ya‘lamu ma fee assamawati wama fee alardi waanna Allaha bikulli shayin ‘aleem
5|98|I‘lamoo anna Allaha shadeedu al‘iqabi waanna Allaha ghafoorun raheem
5|99|Ma ‘ala arrasooli illa albalaghu wallahu ya‘lamu ma tubdoona wama taktumoon
5|100|Qul la yastawee alkhabeethu wattayyibu walaw a‘jabaka kathratu alkhabeethi fattaqoo Allaha ya olee alalbabi la‘allakum tuflihoon
5|101|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo la tasaloo ‘an ashyaa in tubda lakum tasukum wain tasaloo ‘anha heena yunazzalu alquranu tubda lakum ‘afa Allahu ‘anha wallahu ghafoorun haleem
5|102|Qad saalaha qawmun min qablikum thumma asbahoo biha kafireen
5|103|Ma ja‘ala Allahu min baheeratin wala saibatin wala waseelatin wala hamin walakinna allatheena kafaroo yaftaroona ‘ala Allahi alkathiba waaktharuhum la ya‘qiloon
5|104|Waitha qeela lahum ta‘alaw ila ma anzala Allahu waila arrasooli qaloo hasbuna ma wajadna ‘alayhi abaana awalaw kana abaohum la ya‘lamoona shayan wala yahtadoon
5|105|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo ‘alaykum anfusakum la yadurrukum man dalla itha ihtadaytum ila Allahi marji‘ukum jamee‘an fayunabbiokum bima kuntum ta‘maloon
5|106|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo shahadatu baynikum itha hadara ahadakumu almawtu heena alwasiyyati ithnani thawa ‘adlin minkum aw akharani min ghayrikum in antum darabtum fee alardi faasabatkum museebatu almawti tahbisoonahuma min ba‘di assalati fayuqsimani billahi ini irtabtum la nashtaree bihi thamanan walaw kana tha qurba wala naktumu shahadata Allahi inna ithan lamina alathimeen
5|107|Fain ‘uthira ‘ala annahuma istahaqqa ithman faakharani yaqoomani maqamahuma mina allatheena istahaqqa ‘alayhimu alawlayani fayuqsimani billahi lashahadatuna ahaqqu min shahadatihima wama i‘tadayna inna ithan lamina aththalimeen
5|108|Thalika adna an yatoo bishshahadati ‘ala wajhiha aw yakhafoo an turadda aymanun ba‘da aymanihim wattaqoo Allaha wasma‘oo wallahu la yahdee alqawma alfasiqeen
5|109|Yawma yajma‘u Allahu arrusula fayaqoolu matha ojibtum qaloo la ‘ilma lana innaka anta ‘allamu alghuyoob
5|110|Ith qala Allahu ya ‘eesa ibna maryama othkur ni‘matee ‘alayka wa‘ala walidatika ith ayyadtuka biroohi alqudusi tukallimu annasa fee almahdi wakahlan waith ‘allamtuka alkitaba walhikmata wattawrata walinjeela waith takhluqu mina atteeni kahayati attayri biithnee fatanfukhu feeha fatakoonu tayran biithnee watubrio alakmaha walabrasa biithnee waith tukhriju almawta biithnee waith kafaftu banee israeela ‘anka ith jitahum bilbayyinati faqala allatheena kafaroo minhum in hatha illa sihrun mubeen
5|111|Waith awhaytu ila alhawariyyeena an aminoo bee wabirasoolee qaloo amanna washhad biannana muslimoon
5|112|Ith qala alhawariyyoona ya ‘eesa ibna maryama hal yastatee‘u rabbuka an yunazzila ‘alayna maidatan mina assamai qala ittaqoo Allaha in kuntum mumineen
5|113|Qaloo nureedu an nakula minha watatmainna quloobuna wana‘lama an qad sadaqtana wanakoona ‘alayha mina ashshahideen
5|114|Qala ‘eesa ibnu maryama allahumma rabbana anzil ‘alayna maidatan mina assamai takoonu lana ‘eedan liawwalina waakhirina waayatan minka warzuqna waanta khayru arraziqeen
5|115|Qala Allahu innee munazziluha ‘alaykum faman yakfur ba‘du minkum fainnee o‘aththibuhu ‘athaban la o‘aththibuhu ahadan mina al‘alameen
5|116|Waith qala Allahu ya ‘eesa ibna maryama aanta qulta linnasi ittakhithoonee waommiya ilahayni min dooni Allahi qala subhanaka ma yakoonu lee an aqoola ma laysa lee bihaqqin in kuntu qultuhu faqad ‘alimtahu ta‘lamu ma fee nafsee wala a‘lamu ma fee nafsika innaka anta ‘allamu alghuyoob
5|117|Ma qultu lahum illa ma amartanee bihi ani o‘budoo Allaha rabbee warabbakum wakuntu ‘alayhim shaheedan ma dumtu feehim falamma tawaffaytanee kunta anta arraqeeba ‘alayhim waanta ‘ala kulli shayin shaheed
5|118|In tu‘aththibhum fainnahum ‘ibaduka wain taghfir lahum fainnaka anta al‘azeezu alhakeem
5|119|Qala Allahu hatha yawmu yanfa‘u assadiqeena sidquhum lahum jannatun tajree min tahtiha alanharu khalideena feeha abadan radiya Allahu ‘anhum waradoo ‘anhu thalika alfawzu al‘atheem
5|120|Lillahi mulku assamawati walardi wama feehinna wahuwa ‘ala kulli shayin qadeer
6|1|Alhamdu lillahi allathee khalaqa assamawati walarda waja‘ala aththulumati wannoora thumma allatheena kafaroo birabbihim ya‘diloon
6|2|Huwa allathee khalaqakum min teenin thumma qada ajalan waajalun musamman ‘indahu thumma antum tamtaroon
6|3|Wahuwa Allahu fee assamawati wafee alardi ya‘lamu sirrakum wajahrakum waya‘lamu ma taksiboon
6|4|Wama tateehim min ayatin min ayati rabbihim illa kanoo ‘anha mu‘rideen
6|5|Faqad kaththaboo bilhaqqi lamma jaahum fasawfa yateehim anbao ma kanoo bihi yastahzioon
6|6|Alam yaraw kam ahlakna min qablihim min qarnin makkannahum fee alardi ma lam numakkin lakum waarsalna assamaa ‘alayhim midraran waja‘alna alanhara tajree min tahtihim faahlaknahum bithunoobihim waanshana min ba‘dihim qarnan akhareen
6|7|Walaw nazzalna ‘alayka kitaban fee qirtasin falamasoohu biaydeehim laqala allatheena kafaroo in hatha illa sihrun mubeen
6|8|Waqaloo lawla onzila ‘alayhi malakun walaw anzalna malakan laqudiya alamru thumma la yuntharoon
6|9|Walaw ja‘alnahu malakan laja‘alnahu rajulan walalabasna ‘alayhim ma yalbisoon
6|10|Walaqadi istuhzia birusulin min qablika fahaqa billatheena sakhiroo minhum ma kanoo bihi yastahzioon
6|11|Qul seeroo fee alardi thumma onthuroo kayfa kana ‘aqibatu almukaththibeen
6|12|Qul liman ma fee assamawati walardi qul lillahi kataba ‘ala nafsihi arrahmata layajma‘annakum ila yawmi alqiyamati la rayba feehi allatheena khasiroo anfusahum fahum la yuminoon
6|13|Walahu ma sakana fee allayli wannahari wahuwa assamee‘u al‘aleem
6|14|Qul aghayra Allahi attakhithu waliyyan fatiri assamawati walardi wahuwa yut‘imu wala yut‘amu qul innee omirtu an akoona awwala man aslama wala takoonanna mina almushrikeen
6|15|Qul innee akhafu in ‘asaytu rabbee ‘athaba yawmin ‘atheem
6|16|Man yusraf ‘anhu yawmaithin faqad rahimahu wathalika alfawzu almubeen
6|17|Wain yamsaska Allahu bidurrin fala kashifa lahu illa huwa wain yamsaska bikhayrin fahuwa ‘ala kulli shayin qadeer
6|18|Wahuwa alqahiru fawqa ‘ibadihi wahuwa alhakeemu alkhabeer
6|19|Qul ayyu shayin akbaru shahadatan quli Allahu shaheedun baynee wabaynakum waoohiya ilayya hatha alquranu lionthirakum bihi waman balagha ainnakum latashhadoona anna ma‘a Allahi alihatan okhra qul la ashhadu qul innama huwa ilahun wahidun wainnanee bareeon mimma tushrikoon
6|20|Allatheena ataynahumu alkitaba ya‘rifoonahu kama ya‘rifoona abnaahum allatheena khasiroo anfusahum fahum la yuminoon
6|21|Waman athlamu mimmani iftara ‘ala Allahi kathiban aw kaththaba biayatihi innahu la yuflihu aththalimoon
6|22|Wayawma nahshuruhum jamee‘an thumma naqoolu lillatheena ashrakoo ayna shurakaokumu allatheena kuntum taz‘umoon
6|23|Thumma lam takun fitnatuhum illa an qaloo wallahi rabbina ma kunna mushrikeen
6|24|Onthur kayfa kathaboo ‘ala anfusihim wadalla ‘anhum ma kanoo yaftaroon
6|25|Waminhum man yastami‘u ilayka waja‘alna ‘ala quloobihim akinnatan an yafqahoohu wafee athanihim waqran wain yaraw kulla ayatin la yuminoo biha hatta itha jaooka yujadiloonaka yaqoolu allatheena kafaroo in hatha illa asateeru alawwaleen
6|26|Wahum yanhawna ‘anhu wayanawna ‘anhu wain yuhlikoona illa anfusahum wama yash‘uroon
6|27|Walaw tara ith wuqifoo ‘ala annari faqaloo ya laytana nuraddu wala nukaththiba biayati rabbina wanakoona mina almumineen
6|28|Bal bada lahum ma kanoo yukhfoona min qablu walaw ruddoo la‘adoo lima nuhoo ‘anhu wainnahum lakathiboon
6|29|Waqaloo in hiya illa hayatuna addunya wama nahnu bimab‘ootheen
6|30|Walaw tara ith wuqifoo ‘ala rabbihim qala alaysa hatha bilhaqqi qaloo bala warabbina qala fathooqoo al‘athaba bima kuntum takfuroon
6|31|Qad khasira allatheena kaththaboo biliqai Allahi hatta itha jaathumu assa‘atu baghtatan qaloo ya hasratana ‘ala ma farratna feeha wahum yahmiloona awzarahum ‘ala thuhoorihim ala saa ma yaziroon
6|32|Wama alhayatu addunya illa la‘ibun walahwun waladdaru alakhirati khayrun lillatheena yattaqoona afala ta‘qiloon
6|33|Qad na‘lamu innahu layahzunuka allathee yaqooloona fainnahum la yukaththiboonaka walakinna aththalimeena biayati Allahi yajhadoon
6|34|Walaqad kuththibat rusulun min qablika fasabaroo ‘ala ma kuththiboo waoothoo hatta atahum nasruna wala mubaddila likalimati Allahi walaqad jaaka min nabai almursaleen
6|35|Wain kana kabura ‘alayka i‘raduhum faini istata‘ta an tabtaghiya nafaqan fee alardi aw sullaman fee assamai fatatiyahum biayatin walaw shaa Allahu lajama‘ahum ‘ala alhuda fala takoonanna mina aljahileen
6|36|Innama yastajeebu allatheena yasma‘oona walmawta yab‘athuhumu Allahu thumma ilayhi yurja‘oon
6|37|Waqaloo lawla nuzzila ‘alayhi ayatun min rabbihi qul inna Allaha qadirun ‘ala an yunazzila ayatan walakinna aktharahum la ya‘lamoon
6|38|Wama min dabbatin fee alardi wala tairin yateeru bijanahayhi illa omamun amthalukum ma farratna fee alkitabi min shayin thumma ila rabbihim yuhsharoon
6|39|Wallatheena kaththaboo biayatina summun wabukmun fee aththulumati man yashai Allahu yudlilhu waman yasha yaj‘alhu ‘ala siratin mustaqeem
6|40|Qul araaytakum in atakum ‘athabu Allahi aw atatkumu assa‘atu aghayra Allahi tad‘oona in kuntum sadiqeen
6|41|Bal iyyahu tad‘oona fayakshifu ma tad‘oona ilayhi in shaa watansawna ma tushrikoon
6|42|Walaqad arsalna ila omamin min qablika faakhathnahum bilbasai waddarrai la‘allahum yatadarra‘oon
6|43|Falawla ith jaahum basuna tadarra‘oo walakin qasat quloobuhum wazayyana lahumu ashshaytanu ma kanoo ya‘maloon
6|44|Falamma nasoo ma thukkiroo bihi fatahna ‘alayhim abwaba kulli shayin hatta itha farihoo bima ootoo akhathnahum baghtatan faitha hum mublisoon
6|45|Faquti‘a dabiru alqawmi allatheena thalamoo walhamdu lillahi rabbi al‘alameen
6|46|Qul araaytum in akhatha Allahu sam‘akum waabsarakum wakhatama ‘ala quloobikum man ilahun ghayru Allahi yateekum bihi onthur kayfa nusarrifu alayati thumma hum yasdifoon
6|47|Qul araaytakum in atakum ‘athabu Allahi baghtatan aw jahratan hal yuhlaku illa alqawmu aththalimoon
6|48|Wama nursilu almursaleena illa mubashshireena wamunthireena faman amana waaslaha fala khawfun ‘alayhim wala hum yahzanoon
6|49|Wallatheena kaththaboo biayatina yamassuhumu al‘athabu bima kanoo yafsuqoon
6|50|Qul la aqoolu lakum ‘indee khazainu Allahi wala a‘lamu alghayba wala aqoolu lakum innee malakun in attabi‘u illa ma yooha ilayya qul hal yastawee ala‘ma walbaseeru afala tatafakkaroon
6|51|Waanthir bihi allatheena yakhafoona an yuhsharoo ila rabbihim laysa lahum min doonihi waliyyun wala shafee‘un la‘allahum yattaqoon
6|52|Wala tatrudi allatheena yad‘oona rabbahum bilghadati wal‘ashiyyi yureedoona wajhahu ma ‘alayka min hisabihim min shayin wama min hisabika ‘alayhim min shayin fatatrudahum fatakoona mina aththalimeen
6|53|Wakathalika fatanna ba‘dahum biba‘din liyaqooloo ahaolai manna Allahu ‘alayhim min baynina alaysa Allahu bia‘lama bishshakireen
6|54|Waitha jaaka allatheena yuminoona biayatina faqul salamun ‘alaykum kataba rabbukum ‘ala nafsihi arrahmata annahu man ‘amila minkum sooan bijahalatin thumma taba min ba‘dihi waaslaha faannahu ghafoorun raheem
6|55|Wakathalika nufassilu alayati walitastabeena sabeelu almujrimeen
6|56|Qul innee nuheetu an a‘buda allatheena tad‘oona min dooni Allahi qul la attabi‘u ahwaakum qad dalaltu ithan wama ana mina almuhtadeen
6|57|Qul innee ‘ala bayyinatin min rabbee wakaththabtum bihi ma ‘indee ma tasta‘jiloona bihi ini alhukmu illa lillahi yaqussu alhaqqa wahuwa khayru alfasileen
6|58|Qul law anna ‘indee ma tasta‘jiloona bihi laqudiya alamru baynee wabaynakum wallahu a‘lamu biththalimeen
6|59|Wa‘indahu mafatihu alghaybi la ya‘lamuha illa huwa waya‘lamu ma fee albarri walbahri wama tasqutu min waraqatin illa ya‘lamuha wala habbatin fee thulumati alardi wala ratbin wala yabisin illa fee kitabin mubeen
6|60|Wahuwa allathee yatawaffakum billayli waya‘lamu ma jarahtum binnahari thumma yab‘athukum feehi liyuqda ajalun musamman thumma ilayhi marji‘ukum thumma yunabbiokum bima kuntum ta‘maloon
6|61|Wahuwa alqahiru fawqa ‘ibadihi wayursilu ‘alaykum hafathatan hatta itha jaa ahadakumu almawtu tawaffathu rusuluna wahum la yufarritoon
6|62|Thumma ruddoo ila Allahi mawlahumu alhaqqi ala lahu alhukmu wahuwa asra‘u alhasibeen
6|63|Qul man yunajjeekum min thulumati albarri walbahri tad‘oonahu tadarru‘an wakhufyatan lain anjana min hathihi lanakoonanna mina ashshakireen
6|64|Quli Allahu yunajjeekum minha wamin kulli karbin thumma antum tushrikoon
6|65|Qul huwa alqadiru ‘ala an yab‘atha ‘alaykum ‘athaban min fawqikum aw min tahti arjulikum aw yalbisakum shiya‘an wayutheeqa ba‘dakum basa ba‘din onthur kayfa nusarrifu alayati la‘allahum yafqahoon
6|66|Wakaththaba bihi qawmuka wahuwa alhaqqu qul lastu ‘alaykum biwakeel
6|67|Likulli nabain mustaqarrun wasawfa ta‘lamoon
6|68|Waitha raayta allatheena yakhoodoona fee ayatina faa‘rid ‘anhum hatta yakhoodoo fee hadeethin ghayrihi waimma yunsiyannaka ashshaytanu fala taq‘ud ba‘da aththikra ma‘a alqawmi aththalimeen
6|69|Wama ‘ala allatheena yattaqoona min hisabihim min shayin walakin thikra la‘allahum yattaqoon
6|70|Wathari allatheena ittakhathoo deenahum la‘iban walahwan wagharrathumu alhayatu addunya wathakkir bihi an tubsala nafsun bima kasabat laysa laha min dooni Allahi waliyyun wala shafee‘un wain ta‘dil kulla ‘adlin la yukhath minha olaika allatheena obsiloo bima kasaboo lahum sharabun min hameemin wa‘athabun aleemun bima kanoo yakfuroon
6|71|Qul anad‘oo min dooni Allahi ma la yanfa‘una wala yadurruna wanuraddu ‘ala a‘qabina ba‘da ith hadana Allahu kallathee istahwathu ashshayateenu fee alardi hayrana lahu ashabun yad‘oonahu ila alhuda itina qul inna huda Allahi huwa alhuda waomirna linuslima lirabbi al‘alameen
6|72|Waan aqeemoo assalata wattaqoohu wahuwa allathee ilayhi tuhsharoon
6|73|Wahuwa allathee khalaqa assamawati walarda bilhaqqi wayawma yaqoolu kun fayakoonu qawluhu alhaqqu walahu almulku yawma yunfakhu fee assoori ‘alimu alghaybi washshahadati wahuwa alhakeemu alkhabeer
6|74|Waith qala ibraheemu liabeehi azara atattakhithu asnaman alihatan innee araka waqawmaka fee dalalin mubeen
6|75|Wakathalika nuree ibraheema malakoota assamawati walardi waliyakoona mina almooqineen
6|76|Falamma janna ‘alayhi allaylu raa kawkaban qala hatha rabbee falamma afala qala la ohibbu alafileen
6|77|Falamma raa alqamara bazighan qala hatha rabbee falamma afala qala lain lam yahdinee rabbee laakoonanna mina alqawmi addalleen
6|78|Falamma raa ashshamsa bazighatan qala hatha rabbee hatha akbaru falamma afalat qala ya qawmi innee bareeon mimma tushrikoon
6|79|Innee wajjahtu wajhiya lillathee fatara assamawati walarda haneefan wama ana mina almushrikeen
6|80|Wahajjahu qawmuhu qala atuhajjoonnee fee Allahi waqad hadani wala akhafu ma tushrikoona bihi illa an yashaa rabbee shayan wasi‘a rabbee kulla shayin ‘ilman afala tatathakkaroon
6|81|Wakayfa akhafu ma ashraktum wala takhafoona annakum ashraktum billahi ma lam yunazzil bihi ‘alaykum sultanan faayyu alfareeqayni ahaqqu bilamni in kuntum ta‘lamoon
6|82|Allatheena amanoo walam yalbisoo eemanahum bithulmin olaika lahumu alamnu wahum muhtadoon
6|83|Watilka hujjatuna ataynaha ibraheema ‘ala qawmihi narfa‘u darajatin man nashao inna rabbaka hakeemun ‘aleem
6|84|Wawahabna lahu ishaqa waya‘qooba kullan hadayna wanoohan hadayna min qablu wamin thurriyyatihi dawooda wasulaymana waayyooba wayoosufa wamoosa waharoona wakathalika najzee almuhsineen
6|85|Wazakariyya wayahya wa‘eesa walyasa kullun mina assaliheen
6|86|Waisma‘eela walyasa‘a wayoonusa walootan wakullan faddalna ‘ala al‘alameen
6|87|Wamin abaihim wathurriyyatihim waikhwanihim wajtabaynahum wahadaynahum ila siratin mustaqeem
6|88|Thalika huda Allahi yahdee bihi man yashao min ‘ibadihi walaw ashrakoo lahabita ‘anhum ma kanoo ya‘maloon
6|89|Olaika allatheena ataynahumu alkitaba walhukma wannubuwwata fain yakfur biha haolai faqad wakkalna biha qawman laysoo biha bikafireen
6|90|Olaika allatheena hada Allahu fabihudahumu iqtadih qul la asalukum ‘alayhi ajran in huwa illa thikra lil‘alameen
6|91|Wama qadaroo Allaha haqqa qadrihi ith qaloo ma anzala Allahu ‘ala basharin min shayin qul man anzala alkitaba allathee jaa bihi moosa nooran wahudan linnasi taj‘aloonahu qarateesa tubdoonaha watukhfoona katheeran wa‘ullimtum ma lam ta‘lamoo antum wala abaokum quli Allahu thumma tharhum fee khawdihim yal‘aboon
6|92|Wahatha kitabun anzalnahu mubarakun musaddiqu allathee bayna yadayhi walitunthira omma alqura waman hawlaha wallatheena yuminoona bilakhirati yuminoona bihi wahum ‘ala salatihim yuhafithoon
6|93|Waman athlamu mimmani iftara ‘ala Allahi kathiban aw qala oohiya ilayya walam yooha ilayhi shayon waman qala saonzilu mithla ma anzala Allahu walaw tara ithi aththalimoona fee ghamarati almawti walmalaikatu basitoo aydeehim akhrijoo anfusakumu alyawma tujzawna ‘athaba alhooni bima kuntum taqooloona ‘ala Allahi ghayra alhaqqi wakuntum ‘an ayatihi tastakbiroon
6|94|Walaqad jitumoona furada kama khalaqnakum awwala marratin wataraktum ma khawwalnakum waraa thuhoorikum wama nara ma‘akum shufa‘aakumu allatheena za‘amtum annahum feekum shurakao laqad taqatta‘a baynakum wadalla ‘ankum ma kuntum taz‘umoon
6|95|Inna Allaha faliqu alhabbi wannawa yukhriju alhayya mina almayyiti wamukhriju almayyiti mina alhayyi thalikumu Allahu faanna tufakoon
6|96|Faliqu alisbahi waja‘ala allayla sakanan washshamsa walqamara husbanan thalika taqdeeru al‘azeezi al‘aleem
6|97|Wahuwa allathee ja‘ala lakumu annujooma litahtadoo biha fee thulumati albarri walbahri qad fassalna alayati liqawmin ya‘lamoon
6|98|Wahuwa allathee anshaakum min nafsin wahidatin famustaqarrun wamustawda‘un qad fassalna alayati liqawmin yafqahoon
6|99|Wahuwa allathee anzala mina assamai maan faakhrajna bihi nabata kulli shayin faakhrajna minhu khadiran nukhriju minhu habban mutarakiban wamina annakhli min tal‘iha qinwanun daniyatun wajannatin min a‘nabin wazzaytoona warrummana mushtabihan waghayra mutashabihin onthuroo ila thamarihi itha athmara wayan‘ihi inna fee thalikum laayatin liqawmin yuminoon
6|100|Waja‘aloo lillahi shurakaa aljinna wakhalaqahum wakharaqoo lahu baneena wabanatin bighayri ‘ilmin subhanahu wata‘ala ‘amma yasifoon
6|101|Badee‘u assamawati walardi anna yakoonu lahu waladun walam takun lahu sahibatun wakhalaqa kulla shayin wahuwa bikulli shayin ‘aleem
6|102|Thalikumu Allahu rabbukum la ilaha illa huwa khaliqu kulli shayin fa‘budoohu wahuwa ‘ala kulli shayin wakeel
6|103|La tudrikuhu alabsaru wahuwa yudriku alabsara wahuwa allateefu alkhabeer
6|104|Qad jaakum basairu min rabbikum faman absara falinafsihi waman ‘amiya fa‘alayha wama ana ‘alaykum bihafeeth
6|105|Wakathalika nusarrifu alayati waliyaqooloo darasta walinubayyinahu liqawmin ya‘lamoon
6|106|Ittabi‘ ma oohiya ilayka min rabbika la ilaha illa huwa waa‘rid ‘ani almushrikeen
6|107|Walaw shaa Allahu ma ashrakoo wama ja‘alnaka ‘alayhim hafeethan wama anta ‘alayhim biwakeel
6|108|Wala tasubboo allatheena yad‘oona min dooni Allahi fayasubboo Allaha ‘adwan bighayri ‘ilmin kathalika zayyanna likulli ommatin ‘amalahum thumma ila rabbihim marji‘uhum fayunabbiohum bima kanoo ya‘maloon
6|109|Waaqsamoo billahi jahda aymanihim lain jaathum ayatun layuminunna biha qul innama alayatu ‘inda Allahi wama yush‘irukum annaha itha jaat la yuminoon
6|110|Wanuqallibu afidatahum waabsarahum kama lam yuminoo bihi awwala marratin wanatharuhum fee tughyanihim ya‘mahoon
6|111|Walaw annana nazzalna ilayhimu almalaikata wakallamahumu almawta wahasharna ‘alayhim kulla shayin qubulan ma kanoo liyuminoo illa an yashaa Allahu walakinna aktharahum yajhaloon
6|112|Wakathalika ja‘alna likulli nabiyyin ‘aduwwan shayateena alinsi waljinni yoohee ba‘duhum ila ba‘din zukhrufa alqawli ghurooran walaw shaa rabbuka ma fa‘aloohu fatharhum wama yaftaroon
6|113|Walitasgha ilayhi afidatu allatheena la yuminoona bilakhirati waliyardawhu waliyaqtarifoo ma hum muqtarifoon
6|114|Afaghayra Allahi abtaghee hakaman wahuwa allathee anzala ilaykumu alkitaba mufassalan wallatheena ataynahumu alkitaba ya‘lamoona annahu munazzalun min rabbika bilhaqqi fala takoonanna mina almumtareen
6|115|Watammat kalimatu rabbika sidqan wa‘adlan la mubaddila likalimatihi wahuwa assamee‘u al‘aleem
6|116|Wain tuti‘ akthara man fee alardi yudillooka ‘an sabeeli Allahi in yattabi‘oona illa aththanna wain hum illa yakhrusoon
6|117|Inna rabbaka huwa a‘lamu man yadillu ‘an sabeelihi wahuwa a‘lamu bilmuhtadeen
6|118|Fakuloo mimma thukira ismu Allahi ‘alayhi in kuntum biayatihi mumineen
6|119|Wama lakum alla takuloo mimma thukira ismu Allahi ‘alayhi waqad fassala lakum ma harrama ‘alaykum illa ma idturirtum ilayhi wainna katheeran layudilloona biahwaihim bighayri ‘ilmin inna rabbaka huwa a‘lamu bilmu‘tadeen
6|120|Watharoo thahira alithmi wabatinahu inna allatheena yaksiboona alithma sayujzawna bima kanoo yaqtarifoon
6|121|Wala takuloo mimma lam yuthkari ismu Allahi ‘alayhi wainnahu lafisqun wainna ashshayateena layoohoona ila awliyaihim liyujadilookum wain ata‘tumoohum innakum lamushrikoon
6|122|Awa man kana maytan faahyaynahu waja‘alna lahu nooran yamshee bihi fee annasi kaman mathaluhu fee aththulumati laysa bikharijin minha kathalika zuyyina lilkafireena ma kanoo ya‘maloon
6|123|Wakathalika ja‘alna fee kulli qaryatin akabira mujrimeeha liyamkuroo feeha wama yamkuroona illa bianfusihim wama yash‘uroon
6|124|Waitha jaathum ayatun qaloo lan numina hatta nuta mithla ma ootiya rusulu Allahi Allahu a‘lamu haythu yaj‘alu risalatahu sayuseebu allatheena ajramoo sagharun ‘inda Allahi wa‘athabun shadeedun bima kanoo yamkuroon
6|125|Faman yuridi Allahu an yahdiyahu yashrah sadrahu lilislami waman yurid an yudillahu yaj‘al sadrahu dayyiqan harajan kaannama yassa‘‘adu fee assamai kathalika yaj‘alu Allahu arrijsa ‘ala allatheena la yuminoon
6|126|Wahatha siratu rabbika mustaqeeman qad fassalna alayati liqawmin yaththakkaroon
6|127|Lahum daru assalami ‘inda rabbihim wahuwa waliyyuhum bima kanoo ya‘maloon
6|128|Wayawma yahshuruhum jamee‘an ya ma‘shara aljinni qadi istakthartum mina alinsi waqala awliyaohum mina alinsi rabbana istamta‘a ba‘duna biba‘din wabalaghna ajalana allathee ajjalta lana qala annaru mathwakum khalideena feeha illa ma shaa Allahu inna rabbaka hakeemun ‘aleem
6|129|Wakathalika nuwallee ba‘da aththalimeena ba‘dan bima kanoo yaksiboon
6|130|Ya ma‘shara aljinni walinsi alam yatikum rusulun minkum yaqussoona ‘alaykum ayatee wayunthiroonakum liqaa yawmikum hatha qaloo shahidna ‘ala anfusina wagharrathumu alhayatu addunya washahidoo ‘ala anfusihim annahum kanoo kafireen
6|131|Thalika an lam yakun rabbuka muhlika alqura bithulmin waahluha ghafiloon
6|132|Walikullin darajatun mimma ‘amiloo wama rabbuka bighafilin ‘amma ya‘maloon
6|133|Warabbuka alghaniyyu thoo arrahmati in yasha yuthhibkum wayastakhlif min ba‘dikum ma yashao kama anshaakum min thurriyyati qawmin akhareen
6|134|Inna ma too‘adoona laatin wama antum bimu‘jizeen
6|135|Qul ya qawmi i‘maloo ‘ala makanatikum innee ‘amilun fasawfa ta‘lamoona man takoonu lahu ‘aqibatu addari innahu la yuflihu aththalimoon
6|136|Waja‘aloo lillahi mimma tharaa mina alharthi walan‘ami naseeban faqaloo hatha lillahi biza‘mihim wahatha lishurakaina fama kana lishurakaihim fala yasilu ila Allahi wama kana lillahi fahuwa yasilu ila shurakaihim saa ma yahkumoon
6|137|Wakathalika zayyana likatheerin mina almushrikeena qatla awladihim shurakaohum liyurdoohum waliyalbisoo ‘alayhim deenahum walaw shaa Allahu ma fa‘aloohu fatharhum wama yaftaroon
6|138|Waqaloo hathihi an‘amun waharthun hijrun la yat‘amuha illa man nashao biza‘mihim waan‘amun hurrimat thuhooruha waan‘amun la yathkuroona isma Allahi ‘alayha iftiraan ‘alayhi sayajzeehim bima kanoo yaftaroon
6|139|Waqaloo ma fee butooni hathihi alan‘ami khalisatun lithukoorina wamuharramun ‘ala azwajina wain yakun maytatan fahum feehi shurakao sayajzeehim wasfahum innahu hakeemun ‘aleem
6|140|Qad khasira allatheena qataloo awladahum safahan bighayri ‘ilmin waharramoo ma razaqahumu Allahu iftiraan ‘ala Allahi qad dalloo wama kanoo muhtadeen
6|141|Wahuwa allathee anshaa jannatin ma‘rooshatin waghayra ma‘rooshatin wannakhla wazzar‘a mukhtalifan okuluhu wazzaytoona warrummana mutashabihan waghayra mutashabihin kuloo min thamarihi itha athmara waatoo haqqahu yawma hasadihi wala tusrifoo innahu la yuhibbu almusrifeen
6|142|Wamina alan‘ami hamoolatan wafarshan kuloo mimma razaqakumu Allahu wala tattabi‘oo khutuwati ashshaytani innahu lakum ‘aduwwun mubeen
6|143|Thamaniyata azwajin mina addani ithnayni wamina alma‘zi ithnayni qul aththakarayni harrama ami alonthayayni amma ishtamalat ‘alayhi arhamu alonthayayni nabbioonee bi‘ilmin in kuntum sadiqeen
6|144|Wamina alibili ithnayni wamina albaqari ithnayni qul aththakarayni harrama ami alonthayayni amma ishtamalat ‘alayhi arhamu alonthayayni am kuntum shuhadaa ith wassakumu Allahu bihatha faman athlamu mimmani iftara ‘ala Allahi kathiban liyudilla annasa bighayri ‘ilmin inna Allaha la yahdee alqawma aththalimeen
6|145|Qul la ajidu fee ma oohiya ilayya muharraman ‘ala ta‘imin yat‘amuhu illa an yakoona maytatan aw daman masfoohan aw lahma khinzeerin fainnahu rijsun aw fisqan ohilla lighayri Allahi bihi famani idturra ghayra baghin wala ‘adin fainna rabbaka ghafoorun raheem
6|146|Wa‘ala allatheena hadoo harramna kulla thee thufurin wamina albaqari walghanami harramna ‘alayhim shuhoomahuma illa ma hamalat thuhooruhuma awi alhawaya aw ma ikhtalata bi‘athmin thalika jazaynahum bibaghyihim wainna lasadiqoon
6|147|Fain kaththabooka faqul rabbukum thoo rahmatin wasi‘atin wala yuraddu basuhu ‘ani alqawmi almujrimeen
6|148|Sayaqoolu allatheena ashrakoo law shaa Allahu ma ashrakna wala abaona wala harramna min shayin kathalika kaththaba allatheena min qablihim hatta thaqoo basana qul hal ‘indakum min ‘ilmin fatukhrijoohu lana in tattabi‘oona illa aththanna wain antum illa takhrusoon
6|149|Qul falillahi alhujjatu albalighatu falaw shaa lahadakum ajma‘een
6|150|Qul halumma shuhadaakumu allatheena yashhadoona anna Allaha harrama hatha fain shahidoo fala tashhad ma‘ahum wala tattabi‘ ahwaa allatheena kaththaboo biayatina wallatheena la yuminoona bilakhirati wahum birabbihim ya‘diloon
6|151|Qul ta‘alaw atlu ma harrama rabbukum ‘alaykum alla tushrikoo bihi shayan wabilwalidayni ihsanan wala taqtuloo awladakum min imlaqin nahnu narzuqukum waiyyahum wala taqraboo alfawahisha ma thahara minha wama batana wala taqtuloo annafsa allatee harrama Allahu illa bilhaqqi thalikum wassakum bihi la‘allakum ta‘qiloon
6|152|Wala taqraboo mala alyateemi illa billatee hiya ahsanu hatta yablugha ashuddahu waawfoo alkayla walmeezana bilqisti la nukallifu nafsan illa wus‘aha waitha qultum fa‘diloo walaw kana tha qurba wabi‘ahdi Allahi awfoo thalikum wassakum bihi la‘allakum tathakkaroon
6|153|Waanna hatha siratee mustaqeeman fattabi‘oohu wala tattabi‘oo assubula fatafarraqa bikum ‘an sabeelihi thalikum wassakum bihi la‘allakum tattaqoon
6|154|Thumma atayna moosa alkitaba tamaman ‘ala allathee ahsana watafseelan likulli shayin wahudan warahmatan la‘allahum biliqai rabbihim yuminoon
6|155|Wahatha kitabun anzalnahu mubarakun fattabi‘oohu wattaqoo la‘allakum turhamoon
6|156|An taqooloo innama onzila alkitabu ‘ala taifatayni min qablina wain kunna ‘an dirasatihim laghafileen
6|157|Aw taqooloo law anna onzila ‘alayna alkitabu lakunna ahda minhum faqad jaakum bayyinatun min rabbikum wahudan warahmatun faman athlamu mimman kaththaba biayati Allahi wasadafa ‘anha sanajzee allatheena yasdifoona ‘an ayatina sooa al‘athabi bima kanoo yasdifoon
6|158|Hal yanthuroona illa an tatiyahumu almalaikatu aw yatiya rabbuka aw yatiya ba‘du ayati rabbika yawma yatee ba‘du ayati rabbika la yanfa‘u nafsan eemanuha lam takun amanat min qablu aw kasabat fee eemaniha khayran quli intathiroo inna muntathiroon
6|159|Inna allatheena farraqoo deenahum wakanoo shiya‘an lasta minhum fee shayin innama amruhum ila Allahi thumma yunabbiohum bima kanoo yaf‘aloon
6|160|Man jaa bilhasanati falahu ‘ashru amthaliha waman jaa bissayyiati fala yujza illa mithlaha wahum la yuthlamoon
6|161|Qul innanee hadanee rabbee ila siratin mustaqeemin deenan qiyaman millata ibraheema haneefan wama kana mina almushrikeen

6|163|La shareeka lahu wabithalika omirtu waana awwalu almuslimeen
6|164|Qul aghayra Allahi abghee rabban wahuwa rabbu kulli shayin wala taksibu kullu nafsin illa ‘alayha wala taziru waziratun wizra okhra thumma ila rabbikum marji‘ukum fayunabbiokum bima kuntum feehi takhtalifoon
6|165|Wahuwa allathee ja‘alakum khalaifa alardi warafa‘a ba‘dakum fawqa ba‘din darajatin liyabluwakum fee ma atakum inna rabbaka saree‘u al‘iqabi wainnahu laghafoorun raheem
7|1|Aliflammeemsad

7|20|Fawaswasa lahuma ashshaytanu liyubdiya lahuma ma wooriya ‘anhuma min sawatihima waqala ma nahakuma rabbukuma ‘an hathihi ashshajarati illa an takoona malakayni aw takoona mina alkhalideen

7|26|Ya banee adama qad anzalna ‘alaykum libasan yuwaree sawatikum wareeshan walibasu attaqwa thalika khayrun thalika min ayati Allahi la‘allahum yaththakkaroon
7|27|Ya banee adama la yaftinannakumu ashshaytanu kama akhraja abawaykum mina aljannati yanzi‘u ‘anhuma libasahuma liyuriyahuma sawatihima innahu yarakum huwa waqabeeluhu min haythu la tarawnahum inna ja‘alna ashshayateena awliyaa lillatheena la yuminoon
7|28|Waitha fa‘aloo fahishatan qaloo wajadna ‘alayha abaana wallahu amarana biha qul inna Allaha la yamuru bilfahshai ataqooloona ‘ala Allahi ma la ta‘lamoon
7|29|Qul amara rabbee bilqisti waaqeemoo wujoohakum ‘inda kulli masjidin wad‘oohu mukhliseena lahu addeena kama badaakum ta‘oodoon
7|30|Fareeqan hada wafareeqan haqqa ‘alayhimu addalalatu innahumu ittakhathoo ashshayateena awliyaa min dooni Allahi wayahsaboona annahum muhtadoon

7|32|Qul man harrama zeenata Allahi allatee akhraja li‘ibadihi wattayyibati mina arrizqi qul hiya lillatheena amanoo fee alhayati addunya khalisatan yawma alqiyamati kathalika nufassilu alayati liqawmin ya‘lamoon
7|33|Qul innama harrama rabbiya alfawahisha ma thahara minha wama batana walithma walbaghya bighayri alhaqqi waan tushrikoo billahi ma lam yunazzil bihi sultanan waan taqooloo ‘ala Allahi ma la ta‘lamoon
7|34|Walikulli ommatin ajalun faitha jaa ajaluhum la yastakhiroona sa‘atan wala yastaqdimoon
7|35|Ya banee adama imma yatiyannakum rusulun minkum yaqussoona ‘alaykum ayatee famani ittaqa waaslaha fala khawfun ‘alayhim wala hum yahzanoon
7|36|Wallatheena kaththaboo biayatina wastakbaroo ‘anha olaika ashabu annari hum feeha khalidoon
7|37|Faman athlamu mimmani iftara ‘ala Allahi kathiban aw kaththaba biayatihi olaika yanaluhum naseebuhum mina alkitabi hatta itha jaathum rusuluna yatawaffawnahum qaloo ayna ma kuntum tad‘oona min dooni Allahi qaloo dalloo ‘anna washahidoo ‘ala anfusihim annahum kanoo kafireen
7|38|Qala odkhuloo fee omamin qad khalat min qablikum mina aljinni walinsi fee annari kullama dakhalat ommatun la‘anat okhtaha hatta itha iddarakoo feeha jamee‘an qalat okhrahum lioolahum rabbana haolai adalloona faatihim ‘athaban di‘fan mina annari qala likullin di‘fun walakin la ta‘lamoon

7|40|Inna allatheena kaththaboo biayatina wastakbaroo ‘anha la tufattahu lahum abwabu assamai wala yadkhuloona aljannata hatta yalija aljamalu fee sammi alkhiyati wakathalika najzee almujrimeen
7|41|Lahum min jahannama mihadun wamin fawqihim ghawashin wakathalika najzee aththalimeen

7|44|Wanada ashabu aljannati ashaba annari an qad wajadna ma wa‘adana rabbuna haqqan fahal wajadtum ma wa‘ada rabbukum haqqan qaloo na‘am faaththana muaththinun baynahum an la‘natu Allahi ‘ala aththalimeen
