7|134|Walamma waqa‘a ‘alayhimu arrijzu qaloo ya moosa od‘u lana rabbaka bima ‘ahida ‘indaka lain kashafta ‘anna arrijza lanuminanna laka walanursilanna ma‘aka banee israeel
7|135|Falamma kashafna ‘anhumu arrijza ila ajalin hum balighoohu itha hum yankuthoon
7|136|Fantaqamna minhum faaghraqnahum fee alyammi biannahum kaththaboo biayatina wakanoo ‘anha ghafileen
7|137|Waawrathna alqawma allatheena kanoo yustad‘afoona mashariqa alardi wamagharibaha allatee barakna feeha watammat kalimatu rabbika alhusna ‘ala banee israeela bima sabaroo wadammarna ma kana yasna‘u fir‘awnu waqawmuhu wama kanoo ya‘rishoon
7|138|Wajawazna bibanee israeela albahra faataw ‘ala qawmin ya‘kufoona ‘ala asnamin lahum qaloo ya moosa ij‘al lana ilahan kama lahum alihatun qala innakum qawmun tajhaloon
7|139|Inna haolai mutabbarun ma hum feehi wabatilun ma kanoo ya‘maloon
7|140|Qala aghayra Allahi abgheekum ilahan wahuwa faddalakum ‘ala al‘alameen
7|141|Waith anjaynakum min ali fir‘awna yasoomoonakum sooa al‘athabi yuqattiloona abnaakum wayastahyoona nisaakum wafee thalikum balaon min rabbikum ‘atheem
7|142|Wawa‘adna moosa thalatheena laylatan waatmamnaha bi‘ashrin fatamma meeqatu rabbihi arba‘eena laylatan waqala moosa liakheehi haroona okhlufnee fee qawmee waaslih wala tattabi‘ sabeela almufsideen
7|143|Walamma jaa moosa limeeqatina wakallamahu rabbuhu qala rabbi arinee anthur ilayka qala lan taranee walakini onthur ila aljabali faini istaqarra makanahu fasawfa taranee falamma tajalla rabbuhu liljabali ja‘alahu dakkan wakharra moosa sa‘iqan falamma afaqa qala subhanaka tubtu ilayka waana awwalu almumineen
7|144|Qala ya moosa innee istafaytuka ‘ala annasi birisalatee wabikalamee fakhuth ma ataytuka wakun mina ashshakireen
7|145|Wakatabna lahu fee alalwahi min kulli shayin maw‘ithatan watafseelan likulli shayin fakhuthha biquwwatin wamur qawmaka yakhuthoo biahsaniha saoreekum dara alfasiqeen
7|146|Saasrifu ‘an ayatiya allatheena yatakabbaroona fee alardi bighayri alhaqqi wain yaraw kulla ayatin la yuminoo biha wain yaraw sabeela arrushdi la yattakhithoohu sabeelan wain yaraw sabeela alghayyi yattakhithoohu sabeelan thalika biannahum kaththaboo biayatina wakanoo ‘anha ghafileen
7|147|Wallatheena kaththaboo biayatina waliqai alakhirati habitat a‘maluhum hal yujzawna illa ma kanoo ya‘maloon
7|148|Wattakhatha qawmu moosa min ba‘dihi min huliyyihim ‘ijlan jasadan lahu khuwarun alam yaraw annahu la yukallimuhum wala yahdeehim sabeelan ittakhathoohu wakanoo thalimeen
7|149|Walamma suqita fee aydeehim waraaw annahum qad dalloo qaloo lain lam yarhamna rabbuna wayaghfir lana lanakoonanna mina alkhasireen
7|150|Walamma raja‘a moosa ila qawmihi ghadbana asifan qala bisama khalaftumoonee min ba‘dee a‘ajiltum amra rabbikum waalqa alalwaha waakhatha birasi akheehi yajurruhu ilayhi qala ibna omma inna alqawma istad‘afoonee wakadoo yaqtuloonanee fala tushmit biya ala‘daa wala taj‘alnee ma‘a alqawmi aththalimeen
7|151|Qala rabbi ighfir lee waliakhee waadkhilna fee rahmatika waanta arhamu arrahimeen
7|152|Inna allatheena ittakhathoo al‘ijla sayanaluhum ghadabun min rabbihim wathillatun fee alhayati addunya wakathalika najzee almuftareen
7|153|Wallatheena ‘amiloo assayyiati thumma taboo min ba‘diha waamanoo inna rabbaka min ba‘diha laghafoorun raheem
7|154|Walamma sakata ‘an moosa alghadabu akhatha alalwaha wafee nuskhatiha hudan warahmatun lillatheena hum lirabbihim yarhaboon
7|155|Wakhtara moosa qawmahu sab‘eena rajulan limeeqatina falamma akhathathumu arrajfatu qala rabbi law shita ahlaktahum min qablu waiyyaya atuhlikuna bima fa‘ala assufahao minna in hiya illa fitnatuka tudillu biha man tashao watahdee man tashao anta waliyyuna faghfir lana warhamna waanta khayru alghafireen
7|156|Waktub lana fee hathihi addunya hasanatan wafee alakhirati inna hudna ilayka qala ‘athabee oseebu bihi man ashao warahmatee wasi‘at kulla shayin fasaaktubuha lillatheena yattaqoona wayutoona azzakata wallatheena hum biayatina yuminoon
7|157|Allatheena yattabi‘oona arrasoola annabiyya alommiyya allathee yajidoonahu maktooban ‘indahum fee attawrati walinjeeli yamuruhum bilma‘roofi wayanhahum ‘ani almunkari wayuhillu lahumu attayyibati wayuharrimu ‘alayhimu alkhabaitha wayada‘u ‘anhum israhum walaghlala allatee kanat ‘alayhim fallatheena amanoo bihi wa‘azzaroohu wanasaroohu wattaba‘oo annoora allathee onzila ma‘ahu olaika humu almuflihoon
7|158|Qul ya ayyuha annasu innee rasoolu Allahi ilaykum jamee‘an allathee lahu mulku assamawati walardi la ilaha illa huwa yuhyee wayumeetu faaminoo billahi warasoolihi annabiyyi alommiyyi allathee yuminu billahi wakalimatihi wattabi‘oohu la‘allakum tahtadoon
7|159|Wamin qawmi moosa ommatun yahdoona bilhaqqi wabihi ya‘diloon
7|160|Waqatta‘nahumu ithnatay ‘ashrata asbatan omaman waawhayna ila moosa ithi istasqahu qawmuhu ani idrib bi‘asaka alhajara fanbajasat minhu ithnata ‘ashrata ‘aynan qad ‘alima kullu onasin mashrabahum wathallalna ‘alayhimu alghamama waanzalna ‘alayhimu almanna wassalwa kuloo min tayyibati ma razaqnakum wama thalamoona walakin kanoo anfusahum yathlimoon
7|161|Waith qeela lahumu oskunoo hathihi alqaryata wakuloo minha haythu shitum waqooloo hittatun wadkhuloo albaba sujjadan naghfir lakum khateeatikum sanazeedu almuhsineen
7|162|Fabaddala allatheena thalamoo minhum qawlan ghayra allathee qeela lahum faarsalna ‘alayhim rijzan mina assamai bima kanoo yathlimoon
7|163|Wasalhum ‘ani alqaryati allatee kanat hadirata albahri ith ya‘doona fee assabti ith tateehim heetanuhum yawma sabtihim shurra‘an wayawma la yasbitoona la tateehim kathalika nabloohum bima kanoo yafsuqoon
7|164|Waith qalat ommatun minhum lima ta‘ithoona qawman Allahu muhlikuhum aw mu‘aththibuhum ‘athaban shadeedan qaloo ma‘thiratan ila rabbikum wala‘allahum yattaqoon
7|165|Falamma nasoo ma thukkiroo bihi anjayna allatheena yanhawna ‘ani assooi waakhathna allatheena thalamoo bi‘athabin baeesin bima kanoo yafsuqoon
7|166|Falamma ‘ataw ‘an ma nuhoo ‘anhu qulna lahum koonoo qiradatan khasieen
7|167|Waith taaththana rabbuka layab‘athanna ‘alayhim ila yawmi alqiyamati man yasoomuhum sooa al‘athabi inna rabbaka lasaree‘u al‘iqabi wainnahu laghafoorun raheem
7|168|Waqatta‘nahum fee alardi omaman minhumu assalihoona waminhum doona thalika wabalawnahum bilhasanati wassayyiati la‘allahum yarji‘oon
7|169|Fakhalafa min ba‘dihim khalfun warithoo alkitaba yakhuthoona ‘arada hatha aladna wayaqooloona sayughfaru lana wain yatihim ‘aradun mithluhu yakhuthoohu alam yukhath ‘alayhim meethaqu alkitabi an la yaqooloo ‘ala Allahi illa alhaqqa wadarasoo ma feehi waddaru alakhiratu khayrun lillatheena yattaqoona afala ta‘qiloon
7|170|Wallatheena yumassikoona bilkitabi waaqamoo assalata inna la nudee‘u ajra almusliheen
7|171|Waith nataqna aljabala fawqahum kaannahu thullatun wathannoo annahu waqi‘un bihim khuthoo ma ataynakum biquwwatin wathkuroo ma feehi la‘allakum tattaqoon
7|172|Waith akhatha rabbuka min banee adama min thuhoorihim thurriyyatahum waashhadahum ‘ala anfusihim alastu birabbikum qaloo bala shahidna an taqooloo yawma alqiyamati inna kunna ‘an hatha ghafileen
7|173|Aw taqooloo innama ashraka abaona min qablu wakunna thurriyyatan min ba‘dihim afatuhlikuna bima fa‘ala almubtiloon
7|174|Wakathalika nufassilu alayati wala‘allahum yarji‘oon
7|175|Watlu ‘alayhim nabaa allathee ataynahu ayatina fansalakha minha faatba‘ahu ashshaytanu fakana mina alghaween
7|176|Walaw shina larafa‘nahu biha walakinnahu akhlada ila alardi wattaba‘a hawahu famathaluhu kamathali alkalbi in tahmil ‘alayhi yalhath aw tatrukhu yalhath thalika mathalu alqawmi allatheena kaththaboo biayatina faqsusi alqasasa la‘allahum yatafakkaroon
7|177|Saa mathalan alqawmu allatheena kaththaboo biayatina waanfusahum kanoo yathlimoon
7|178|Man yahdi Allahu fahuwa almuhtadee waman yudlil faolaika humu alkhasiroon
7|179|Walaqad tharana lijahannama katheeran mina aljinni walinsi lahum quloobun la yafqahoona biha walahum a‘yunun la yubsiroona biha walahum athanun la yasma‘oona biha olaika kalan‘ami bal hum adallu olaika humu alghafiloon
7|180|Walillahi alasmao alhusna fad‘oohu biha watharoo allatheena yulhidoona fee asmaihi sayujzawna ma kanoo ya‘maloon
7|181|Wamimman khalaqna ommatun yahdoona bilhaqqi wabihi ya‘diloon
7|182|Wallatheena kaththaboo biayatina sanastadrijuhum min haythu la ya‘lamoon
7|183|Waomlee lahum inna kaydee mateen
7|184|Awalam yatafakkaroo ma bisahibihim min jinnatin in huwa illa natheerun mubeen
7|185|Awalam yanthuroo fee malakooti assamawati walardi wama khalaqa Allahu min shayin waan ‘asa an yakoona qadi iqtaraba ajaluhum fabiayyi hadeethin ba‘dahu yuminoon
7|186|Man yudlili Allahu fala hadiya lahu wayatharuhum fee tughyanihim ya‘mahoon
7|187|Yasaloonaka ‘ani assa‘ati ayyana mursaha qul innama ‘ilmuha ‘inda rabbee la yujalleeha liwaqtiha illa huwa thaqulat fee assamawati walardi la tateekum illa baghtatan yasaloonaka kaannaka hafiyyun ‘anha qul innama ‘ilmuha ‘inda Allahi walakinna akthara annasi la ya‘lamoon
7|188|Qul la amliku linafsee naf‘an wala darran illa ma shaa Allahu walaw kuntu a‘lamu alghayba lastakthartu mina alkhayri wama massaniya assooo in ana illa natheerun wabasheerun liqawmin yuminoon
7|189|Huwa allathee khalaqakum min nafsin wahidatin waja‘ala minha zawjaha liyaskuna ilayha falamma taghashshaha hamalat hamlan khafeefan famarrat bihi falamma athqalat da‘awa Allaha rabbahuma lain ataytana salihan lanakoonanna mina ashshakireen
7|190|Falamma atahuma salihan ja‘ala lahu shurakaa feema atahuma fata‘ala Allahu ‘amma yushrikoon
7|191|Ayushrikoona ma la yakhluqu shayan wahum yukhlaqoon
7|192|Wala yastatee‘oona lahum nasran wala anfusahum yansuroon
7|193|Wain tad‘oohum ila alhuda la yattabi‘ookum sawaon ‘alaykum ada‘awtumoohum am antum samitoon
7|194|Inna allatheena tad‘oona min dooni Allahi ‘ibadun amthalukum fad‘oohum falyastajeeboo lakum in kuntum sadiqeen
7|195|Alahum arjulun yamshoona biha am lahum aydin yabtishoona biha am lahum a‘yunun yubsiroona biha am lahum athanun yasma‘oona biha quli od‘oo shurakaakum thumma keedooni fala tunthiroon
7|196|Inna waliyyiya Allahu allathee nazzala alkitaba wahuwa yatawalla assaliheen
7|197|Wallatheena tad‘oona min doonihi la yastatee‘oona nasrakum wala anfusahum yansuroon
7|198|Wain tad‘oohum ila alhuda la yasma‘oo watarahum yanthuroona ilayka wahum la yubsiroon
7|199|Khuthi al‘afwa wamur bil‘urfi waa‘rid ‘ani aljahileen
7|200|Waimma yanzaghannaka mina ashshaytani nazghun fasta‘ith billahi innahu samee‘un ‘aleem
7|201|Inna allatheena ittaqaw itha massahum taifun mina ashshaytani tathakkaroo faitha hum mubsiroon
7|202|Waikhwanuhum yamuddoonahum fee alghayyi thumma la yuqsiroon
7|203|Waitha lam tatihim biayatin qaloo lawla ijtabaytaha qul innama attabi‘u ma yooha ilayya min rabbee hatha basairu min rabbikum wahudan warahmatun liqawmin yuminoon
7|204|Waitha quria alquranu fastami‘oo lahu waansitoo la‘allakum turhamoon
7|205|Wathkur rabbaka fee nafsika tadarru‘an wakheefatan wadoona aljahri mina alqawli bilghuduwwi walasali wala takun mina alghafileen
7|206|Inna allatheena ‘inda rabbika la yastakbiroona ‘an ‘ibadatihi wayusabbihoonahu walahu yasjudoon
8|1|Yasaloonaka ‘ani alanfali quli alanfalu lillahi warrasooli fattaqoo Allaha waaslihoo thata baynikum waatee‘oo Allaha warasoolahu in kuntum mumineen
8|2|Innama almuminoona allatheena itha thukira Allahu wajilat quloobuhum waitha tuliyat ‘alayhim ayatuhu zadathum eemanan wa‘ala rabbihim yatawakkaloon
8|3|Allatheena yuqeemoona assalata wamimma razaqnahum yunfiqoon
8|4|Olaika humu almuminoona haqqan lahum darajatun ‘inda rabbihim wamaghfiratun warizqun kareem
8|5|Kama akhrajaka rabbuka min baytika bilhaqqi wainna fareeqan mina almumineena lakarihoon
8|6|Yujadiloonaka fee alhaqqi ba‘dama tabayyana kaannama yusaqoona ila almawti wahum yanthuroon
8|7|Waith ya‘idukumu Allahu ihda attaifatayni annaha lakum watawaddoona anna ghayra thati ashshawkati takoonu lakum wayureedu Allahu an yuhiqqa alhaqqa bikalimatihi wayaqta‘a dabira alkafireen
8|8|Liyuhiqqa alhaqqa wayubtila albatila walaw kariha almujrimoon
8|9|Ith tastagheethoona rabbakum fastajaba lakum annee mumiddukum bialfin mina almalaikati murdifeen
8|10|Wama ja‘alahu Allahu illa bushra walitatmainna bihi quloobukum wama annasru illa min ‘indi Allahi inna Allaha ‘azeezun hakeem
8|11|Ith yughashsheekumu annu‘asa amanatan minhu wayunazzilu ‘alaykum mina assamai maan liyutahhirakum bihi wayuthhiba ‘ankum rijza ashshaytani waliyarbita ‘ala quloobikum wayuthabbita bihi alaqdam
8|12|Ith yoohee rabbuka ila almalaikati annee ma‘akum fathabbitoo allatheena amanoo saolqee fee quloobi allatheena kafaroo arru‘ba fadriboo fawqa ala‘naqi wadriboo minhum kulla banan
8|13|Thalika biannahum shaqqoo Allaha warasoolahu waman yushaqiqi Allaha warasoolahu fainna Allaha shadeedu al‘iqab
8|14|Thalikum fathooqoohu waanna lilkafireena ‘athaba annar
8|15|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo itha laqeetumu allatheena kafaroo zahfan fala tuwalloohumu aladbar
8|16|Waman yuwallihim yawmaithin duburahu illa mutaharrifan liqitalin aw mutahayyizan ila fiatin faqad baa bighadabin mina Allahi wamawahu jahannamu wabisa almaseer
8|17|Falam taqtuloohum walakinna Allaha qatalahum wama ramayta ith ramayta walakinna Allaha rama waliyubliya almumineena minhu balaan hasanan inna Allaha samee‘un ‘aleem
8|18|Thalikum waanna Allaha moohinu kaydi alkafireen
8|19|In tastaftihoo faqad jaakumu alfathu wain tantahoo fahuwa khayrun lakum wain ta‘oodoo na‘ud walan tughniya ‘ankum fiatukum shayan walaw kathurat waanna Allaha ma‘a almumineen
8|20|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo atee‘oo Allaha warasoolahu wala tawallaw ‘anhu waantum tasma‘oon
8|21|Wala takoonoo kallatheena qaloo sami‘na wahum la yasma‘oon
8|22|Inna sharra addawabbi ‘inda Allahi assummu albukmu allatheena la ya‘qiloon
8|23|Walaw ‘alima Allahu feehim khayran laasma‘ahum walaw asma‘ahum latawallaw wahum mu‘ridoon
8|24|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo istajeeboo lillahi walirrasooli itha da‘akum lima yuhyeekum wa‘lamoo anna Allaha yahoolu bayna almari waqalbihi waannahu ilayhi tuhsharoon
8|25|Wattaqoo fitnatan la tuseebanna allatheena thalamoo minkum khassatan wa‘lamoo anna Allaha shadeedu al‘iqab
8|26|Wathkuroo ith antum qaleelun mustad‘afoona fee alardi takhafoona an yatakhattafakumu annasu faawakum waayyadakum binasrihi warazaqakum mina attayyibati la‘allakum tashkuroon
8|27|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo la takhoonoo Allaha warrasoola watakhoonoo amanatikum waantum ta‘lamoon
8|28|Wa‘lamoo annama amwalukum waawladukum fitnatun waanna Allaha ‘indahu ajrun ‘atheem
8|29|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo in tattaqoo Allaha yaj‘al lakum furqanan wayukaffir ‘ankum sayyiatikum wayaghfir lakum wallahu thoo alfadli al‘atheem
8|30|Waith yamkuru bika allatheena kafaroo liyuthbitooka aw yaqtulooka aw yukhrijooka wayamkuroona wayamkuru Allahu wallahu khayru almakireen
8|31|Waitha tutla ‘alayhim ayatuna qaloo qad sami‘na law nashao laqulna mithla hatha in hatha illa asateeru alawwaleen
8|32|Waith qaloo allahumma in kana hatha huwa alhaqqa min ‘indika faamtir ‘alayna hijaratan mina assamai awi itina bi‘athabin aleem
8|33|Wama kana Allahu liyu‘aththibahum waanta feehim wama kana Allahu mu‘aththibahum wahum yastaghfiroon
8|34|Wama lahum alla yu‘aththibahumu Allahu wahum yasuddoona ‘ani almasjidi alharami wama kanoo awliyaahu in awliyaohu illa almuttaqoona walakinna aktharahum la ya‘lamoon
8|35|Wama kana salatuhum ‘inda albayti illa mukaan watasdiyatan fathooqoo al‘athaba bima kuntum takfuroon
8|36|Inna allatheena kafaroo yunfiqoona amwalahum liyasuddoo ‘an sabeeli Allahi fasayunfiqoonaha thumma takoonu ‘alayhim hasratan thumma yughlaboona wallatheena kafaroo ila jahannama yuhsharoon
8|37|Liyameeza Allahu alkhabeetha mina attayyibi wayaj‘ala alkhabeetha ba‘dahu ‘ala ba‘din fayarkumahu jamee‘an fayaj‘alahu fee jahannama olaika humu alkhasiroon
8|38|Qul lillatheena kafaroo in yantahoo yughfar lahum ma qad salafa wain ya‘oodoo faqad madat sunnatu alawwaleen
8|39|Waqatiloohum hatta la takoona fitnatun wayakoona addeenu kulluhu lillahi faini intahaw fainna Allaha bima ya‘maloona baseer
8|40|Wain tawallaw fa‘lamoo anna Allaha mawlakum ni‘ma almawla wani‘ma annaseer
8|41|Wa‘lamoo annama ghanimtum min shayin faanna lillahi khumusahu walirrasooli walithee alqurba walyatama walmasakeeni wabni assabeeli in kuntum amantum billahi wama anzalna ‘ala ‘abdina yawma alfurqani yawma iltaqa aljam‘ani wallahu ‘ala kulli shayin qadeer
8|42|Ith antum bil‘udwati addunya wahum bil‘udwati alquswa warrakbu asfala minkum walaw tawa‘adtum lakhtalaftum fee almee‘adi walakin liyaqdiya Allahu amran kana maf‘oolan liyahlika man halaka ‘an bayyinatin wayahya man hayya ‘an bayyinatin wainna Allaha lasamee‘un ‘aleem
8|43|Ith yureekahumu Allahu fee manamika qaleelan walaw arakahum katheeran lafashiltum walatanaza‘tum fee alamri walakinna Allaha sallama innahu ‘aleemun bithati assudoor
8|44|Waith yureekumoohum ithi iltaqaytum fee a‘yunikum qaleelan wayuqallilukum fee a‘yunihim liyaqdiya Allahu amran kana maf‘oolan waila Allahi turja‘u alomoor
8|45|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo itha laqeetum fiatan fathbutoo wathkuroo Allaha katheeran la‘allakum tuflihoon
8|46|Waatee‘oo Allaha warasoolahu wala tanaza‘oo fatafshaloo watathhaba reehukum wasbiroo inna Allaha ma‘a assabireen
8|47|Wala takoonoo kallatheena kharajoo min diyarihim bataran wariaa annasi wayasuddoona ‘an sabeeli Allahi wallahu bima ya‘maloona muheet
8|48|Waith zayyana lahumu ashshaytanu a‘malahum waqala la ghaliba lakumu alyawma mina annasi wainnee jarun lakum falamma taraati alfiatani nakasa ‘ala ‘aqibayhi waqala innee bareeon minkum innee ara ma la tarawna innee akhafu Allaha wallahu shadeedu al‘iqab
8|49|Ith yaqoolu almunafiqoona wallatheena fee quloobihim maradun gharra haolai deenuhum waman yatawakkal ‘ala Allahi fainna Allaha ‘azeezun hakeem
8|50|Walaw tara ith yatawaffa allatheena kafaroo almalaikatu yadriboona wujoohahum waadbarahum wathooqoo ‘athaba alhareeq
8|51|Thalika bima qaddamat aydeekum waanna Allaha laysa bithallamin lil‘abeed
8|52|Kadabi ali fir‘awna wallatheena min qablihim kafaroo biayati Allahi faakhathahumu Allahu bithunoobihim inna Allaha qawiyyun shadeedu al‘iqab
8|53|Thalika bianna Allaha lam yaku mughayyiran ni‘matan an‘amaha ‘ala qawmin hatta yughayyiroo ma bianfusihim waanna Allaha samee‘un ‘aleem
8|54|Kadabi ali fir‘awna wallatheena min qablihim kaththaboo biayati rabbihim faahlaknahum bithunoobihim waaghraqna ala fir‘awna wakullun kanoo thalimeen
8|55|Inna sharra addawabbi ‘inda Allahi allatheena kafaroo fahum la yuminoon
8|56|Allatheena ‘ahadta minhum thumma yanqudoona ‘ahdahum fee kulli marratin wahum la yattaqoon
8|57|Faimma tathqafannahum fee alharbi fasharrid bihim man khalfahum la‘allahum yaththakkaroon
8|58|Waimma takhafanna min qawmin khiyanatan fanbith ilayhim ‘ala sawain inna Allaha la yuhibbu alkhaineen
8|59|Wala yahsabanna allatheena kafaroo sabaqoo innahum la yu‘jizoon
8|60|Waa‘iddoo lahum ma istata‘tum min quwwatin wamin ribati alkhayli turhiboona bihi ‘aduwwa Allahi wa‘aduwwakum waakhareena min doonihim la ta‘lamoonahumu Allahu ya‘lamuhum wama tunfiqoo min shayin fee sabeeli Allahi yuwaffa ilaykum waantum la tuthlamoon
8|61|Wain janahoo lissalmi fajnah laha watawakkal ‘ala Allahi innahu huwa assamee‘u al‘aleem
8|62|Wain yureedoo an yakhda‘ooka fainna hasbaka Allahu huwa allathee ayyadaka binasrihi wabilmumineen
8|63|Waallafa bayna quloobihim law anfaqta ma fee alardi jamee‘an ma allafta bayna quloobihim walakinna Allaha allafa baynahum innahu ‘azeezun hakeem
8|64|Ya ayyuha annabiyyu hasbuka Allahu wamani ittaba‘aka mina almumineen
8|65|Ya ayyuha annabiyyu harridi almumineena ‘ala alqitali in yakun minkum ‘ishroona sabiroona yaghliboo miatayni wain yakun minkum miatun yaghliboo alfan mina allatheena kafaroo biannahum qawmun la yafqahoon
8|66|Alana khaffafa Allahu ‘ankum wa‘alima anna feekum da‘fan fain yakun minkum miatun sabiratun yaghliboo miatayni wain yakun minkum alfun yaghliboo alfayni biithni Allahi wallahu ma‘a assabireen
8|67|Ma kana linabiyyin an yakoona lahu asra hatta yuthkhina fee alardi tureedoona ‘arada addunya wallahu yureedu alakhirata wallahu ‘azeezun hakeem
8|68|Lawla kitabun mina Allahi sabaqa lamassakum feema akhathtum ‘athabun ‘atheem
8|69|Fakuloo mimma ghanimtum halalan tayyiban wattaqoo Allaha inna Allaha ghafoorun raheem
8|70|Ya ayyuha annabiyyu qul liman fee aydeekum mina alasra in ya‘lami Allahu fee quloobikum khayran yutikum khayran mimma okhitha minkum wayaghfir lakum wallahu ghafoorun raheem
8|71|Wain yureedoo khiyanataka faqad khanoo Allaha min qablu faamkana minhum wallahu ‘aleemun hakeem
8|72|Inna allatheena amanoo wahajaroo wajahadoo biamwalihim waanfusihim fee sabeeli Allahi wallatheena awaw wanasaroo olaika ba‘duhum awliyao ba‘din wallatheena amanoo walam yuhajiroo ma lakum min walayatihim min shayin hatta yuhajiroo waini istansarookum fee addeeni fa‘alaykumu annasru illa ‘ala qawmin baynakum wabaynahum meethaqun wallahu bima ta‘maloona baseer
8|73|Wallatheena kafaroo ba‘duhum awliyao ba‘din illa taf‘aloohu takun fitnatun fee alardi wafasadun kabeer
8|74|Wallatheena amanoo wahajaroo wajahadoo fee sabeeli Allahi wallatheena awaw wanasaroo olaika humu almuminoona haqqan lahum maghfiratun warizqun kareem
8|75|Wallatheena amanoo min ba‘du wahajaroo wajahadoo ma‘akum faolaika minkum waoloo alarhami ba‘duhum awla biba‘din fee kitabi Allahi inna Allaha bikulli shayin ‘aleem
9|1|Baraatun mina Allahi warasoolihi ila allatheena ‘ahadtum mina almushrikeen
9|2|Faseehoo fee alardi arba‘ata ashhurin wa‘lamoo annakum ghayru mu‘jizee Allahi waanna Allaha mukhzee alkafireen
9|3|Waathanun mina Allahi warasoolihi ila annasi yawma alhajji alakbari anna Allaha bareeon mina almushrikeena warasooluhu fain tubtum fahuwa khayrun lakum wain tawallaytum fa‘lamoo annakum ghayru mu‘jizee Allahi wabashshiri allatheena kafaroo bi‘athabin aleem
9|4|Illa allatheena ‘ahadtum mina almushrikeena thumma lam yanqusookum shayan walam yuthahiroo ‘alaykum ahadan faatimmoo ilayhim ‘ahdahum ila muddatihim inna Allaha yuhibbu almuttaqeen
9|5|Faitha insalakha alashhuru alhurumu faqtuloo almushrikeena haythu wajadtumoohum wakhuthoohum wahsuroohum waq‘udoo lahum kulla marsadin fain taboo waaqamoo assalata waatawoo azzakata fakhalloo sabeelahum inna Allaha ghafoorun raheem
9|6|Wain ahadun mina almushrikeena istajaraka faajirhu hatta yasma‘a kalama Allahi thumma ablighhu mamanahu thalika biannahum qawmun la ya‘lamoon
9|7|Kayfa yakoonu lilmushrikeena ‘ahdun ‘inda Allahi wa‘inda rasoolihi illa allatheena ‘ahadtum ‘inda almasjidi alharami fama istaqamoo lakum fastaqeemoo lahum inna Allaha yuhibbu almuttaqeen
9|8|Kayfa wain yathharoo ‘alaykum la yarquboo feekum illan wala thimmatan yurdoonakum biafwahihim wataba quloobuhum waaktharuhum fasiqoon
9|9|Ishtaraw biayati Allahi thamanan qaleelan fasaddoo ‘an sabeelihi innahum saa ma kanoo ya‘maloon
9|10|La yarquboona fee muminin illan wala thimmatan waolaika humu almu‘tadoon
9|11|Fain taboo waaqamoo assalata waatawoo azzakata faikhwanukum fee addeeni wanufassilu alayati liqawmin ya‘lamoon
9|12|Wain nakathoo aymanahum min ba‘di ‘ahdihim wata‘anoo fee deenikum faqatiloo aimmata alkufri innahum la aymana lahum la‘allahum yantahoon
9|13|Ala tuqatiloona qawman nakathoo aymanahum wahammoo biikhraji arrasooli wahum badaookum awwala marratin atakhshawnahum fallahu ahaqqu an takhshawhu in kuntum mumineen
9|14|Qatiloohum yu‘aththibhumu Allahu biaydeekum wayukhzihim wayansurkum ‘alayhim wayashfi sudoora qawmin mumineen
9|15|Wayuthhib ghaytha quloobihim wayatoobu Allahu ‘ala man yashao wallahu ‘aleemun hakeem
9|16|Am hasibtum an tutrakoo walamma ya‘lami Allahu allatheena jahadoo minkum walam yattakhithoo min dooni Allahi wala rasoolihi wala almumineena waleejatan wallahu khabeerun bima ta‘maloon
9|17|Ma kana lilmushrikeena an ya‘muroo masajida Allahi shahideena ‘ala anfusihim bilkufri olaika habitat a‘maluhum wafee annari hum khalidoon
9|18|Innama ya‘muru masajida Allahi man amana billahi walyawmi alakhiri waaqama assalata waata azzakata walam yakhsha illa Allaha fa‘asa olaika an yakoonoo mina almuhtadeen
9|19|Aja‘altum siqayata alhajji wa‘imarata almasjidi alharami kaman amana billahi walyawmi alakhiri wajahada fee sabeeli Allahi la yastawoona ‘inda Allahi wallahu la yahdee alqawma aththalimeen
9|20|Allatheena amanoo wahajaroo wajahadoo fee sabeeli Allahi biamwalihim waanfusihim a‘thamu darajatan ‘inda Allahi waolaika humu alfaizoon
9|21|Yubashshiruhum rabbuhum birahmatin minhu waridwanin wajannatin lahum feeha na‘eemun muqeem
9|22|Khalideena feeha abadan inna Allaha ‘indahu ajrun ‘atheem
9|23|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo la tattakhithoo abaakum waikhwanakum awliyaa ini istahabboo alkufra ‘ala aleemani waman yatawallahum minkum faolaika humu aththalimoon
9|24|Qul in kana abaokum waabnaokum waikhwanukum waazwajukum wa‘asheeratukum waamwalun iqtaraftumooha watijaratun takhshawna kasadaha wamasakinu tardawnaha ahabba ilaykum mina Allahi warasoolihi wajihadin fee sabeelihi fatarabbasoo hatta yatiya Allahu biamrihi wallahu la yahdee alqawma alfasiqeen
9|25|Laqad nasarakumu Allahu fee mawatina katheeratin wayawma hunaynin ith a‘jabatkum kathratukum falam tughni ‘ankum shayan wadaqat ‘alaykumu alardu bima rahubat thumma wallaytum mudbireen
9|26|Thumma anzala Allahu sakeenatahu ‘ala rasoolihi wa‘ala almumineena waanzala junoodan lam tarawha wa‘aththaba allatheena kafaroo wathalika jazao alkafireen
9|27|Thumma yatoobu Allahu min ba‘di thalika ‘ala man yashao wallahu ghafoorun raheem
9|28|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo innama almushrikoona najasun fala yaqraboo almasjida alharama ba‘da ‘amihim hatha wain khiftum ‘aylatan fasawfa yughneekumu Allahu min fadlihi in shaa inna Allaha ‘aleemun hakeem
9|29|Qatiloo allatheena la yuminoona billahi wala bilyawmi alakhiri wala yuharrimoona ma harrama Allahu warasooluhu wala yadeenoona deena alhaqqi mina allatheena ootoo alkitaba hatta yu‘too aljizyata ‘an yadin wahum saghiroon
9|30|Waqalati alyahoodu ‘uzayrun ibnu Allahi waqalati annasara almaseehu ibnu Allahi thalika qawluhum biafwahihim yudahioona qawla allatheena kafaroo min qablu qatalahumu Allahu anna yufakoon
9|31|Ittakhathoo ahbarahum waruhbanahum arbaban min dooni Allahi walmaseeha ibna maryama wama omiroo illa liya‘budoo ilahan wahidan la ilaha illa huwa subhanahu ‘amma yushrikoon
9|32|Yureedoona an yutfioo noora Allahi biafwahihim wayaba Allahu illa an yutimma noorahu walaw kariha alkafiroon
9|33|Huwa allathee arsala rasoolahu bilhuda wadeeni alhaqqi liyuthhirahu ‘ala addeeni kullihi walaw kariha almushrikoon
9|34|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo inna katheeran mina alahbari warruhbani layakuloona amwala annasi bilbatili wayasuddoona ‘an sabeeli Allahi wallatheena yaknizoona aththahaba walfiddata wala yunfiqoonaha fee sabeeli Allahi fabashshirhum bi‘athabin aleem
9|35|Yawma yuhma ‘alayha fee nari jahannama fatukwa biha jibahuhum wajunoobuhum wathuhooruhum hatha ma kanaztum lianfusikum fathooqoo ma kuntum taknizoon
9|36|Inna ‘iddata ashshuhoori ‘inda Allahi ithna ‘ashara shahran fee kitabi Allahi yawma khalaqa assamawati walarda minha arba‘atun hurumun thalika addeenu alqayyimu fala tathlimoo feehinna anfusakum waqatiloo almushrikeena kaffatan kama yuqatiloonakum kaffatan wa‘lamoo anna Allaha ma‘a almuttaqeen
9|37|Innama annaseeo ziyadatun fee alkufri yudallu bihi allatheena kafaroo yuhilloonahu ‘aman wayuharrimoonahu ‘aman liyuwatioo ‘iddata ma harrama Allahu fayuhilloo ma harrama Allahu zuyyina lahum sooo a‘malihim wallahu la yahdee alqawma alkafireen
9|38|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo ma lakum itha qeela lakumu infiroo fee sabeeli Allahi iththaqaltum ila alardi aradeetum bilhayati addunya mina alakhirati fama mata‘u alhayati addunya fee alakhirati illa qaleel
9|39|Illa tanfiroo yu‘aththibkum ‘athaban aleeman wayastabdil qawman ghayrakum wala tadurroohu shayan wallahu ‘ala kulli shayin qadeer
9|40|Illa tansuroohu faqad nasarahu Allahu ith akhrajahu allatheena kafaroo thaniya ithnayni ith huma fee alghari ith yaqoolu lisahibihi la tahzan inna Allaha ma‘ana faanzala Allahu sakeenatahu ‘alayhi waayyadahu bijunoodin lam tarawha waja‘ala kalimata allatheena kafaroo assufla wakalimatu Allahi hiya al‘ulya wallahu ‘azeezun hakeem
9|41|Infiroo khifafan wathiqalan wajahidoo biamwalikum waanfusikum fee sabeeli Allahi thalikum khayrun lakum in kuntum ta‘lamoon
9|42|Law kana ‘aradan qareeban wasafaran qasidan lattaba‘ooka walakin ba‘udat ‘alayhimu ashshuqqatu wasayahlifoona billahi lawi istata‘na lakharajna ma‘akum yuhlikoona anfusahum wallahu ya‘lamu innahum lakathiboon
9|43|‘afa Allahu ‘anka lima athinta lahum hatta yatabayyana laka allatheena sadaqoo wata‘lama alkathibeen
9|44|La yastathinuka allatheena yuminoona billahi walyawmi alakhiri an yujahidoo biamwalihim waanfusihim wallahu ‘aleemun bilmuttaqeen
9|45|Innama yastathinuka allatheena la yuminoona billahi walyawmi alakhiri wartabat quloobuhum fahum fee raybihim yataraddadoon
9|46|Walaw aradoo alkhurooja laa‘addoo lahu ‘uddatan walakin kariha Allahu inbi‘athahum fathabbatahum waqeela oq‘udoo ma‘a alqa‘ideen
9|47|Law kharajoo feekum ma zadookum illa khabalan walaawda‘oo khilalakum yabghoonakumu alfitnata wafeekum samma‘oona lahum wallahu ‘aleemun biththalimeen
9|48|Laqadi ibtaghawoo alfitnata min qablu waqallaboo laka alomoora hatta jaa alhaqqu wathahara amru Allahi wahum karihoon
9|49|Waminhum man yaqoolu ithan lee wala taftinnee ala fee alfitnati saqatoo wainna jahannama lamuheetatun bilkafireen
9|50|In tusibka hasanatun tasuhum wain tusibka museebatun yaqooloo qad akhathna amrana min qablu wayatawallaw wahum farihoon
9|51|Qul lan yuseebana illa ma kataba Allahu lana huwa mawlana wa‘ala Allahi falyatawakkali almuminoon
9|52|Qul hal tarabbasoona bina illa ihda alhusnayayni wanahnu natarabbasu bikum an yuseebakumu Allahu bi‘athabin min ‘indihi aw biaydeena fatarabbasoo inna ma‘akum mutarabbisoon
9|53|Qul anfiqoo taw‘an aw karhan lan yutaqabbala minkum innakum kuntum qawman fasiqeen
9|54|Wama mana‘ahum an tuqbala minhum nafaqatuhum illa annahum kafaroo billahi wabirasoolihi wala yatoona assalata illa wahum kusala wala yunfiqoona illa wahum karihoon
9|55|Fala tu‘jibka amwaluhum wala awladuhum innama yureedu Allahu liyu‘aththibahum biha fee alhayati addunya watazhaqa anfusuhum wahum kafiroon
9|56|Wayahlifoona billahi innahum laminkum wama hum minkum walakinnahum qawmun yafraqoon
9|57|Law yajidoona maljaan aw magharatin aw muddakhalan lawallaw ilayhi wahum yajmahoon
9|58|Waminhum man yalmizuka fee assadaqati fain o‘too minha radoo wain lam yu‘taw minha itha hum yaskhatoon
9|59|Walaw annahum radoo ma atahumu Allahu warasooluhu waqaloo hasbuna Allahu sayuteena Allahu min fadlihi warasooluhu inna ila Allahi raghiboon
9|60|Innama assadaqatu lilfuqarai walmasakeeni wal‘amileena ‘alayha walmuallafati quloobuhum wafee arriqabi walgharimeena wafee sabeeli Allahi wabni assabeeli fareedatan mina Allahi wallahu ‘aleemun hakeemun
9|61|Waminhumu allatheena yuthoona annabiyya wayaqooloona huwa othunun qul othunu khayrin lakum yuminu billahi wayuminu lilmumineena warahmatun lillatheena amanoo minkum wallatheena yuthoona rasoola Allahi lahum ‘athabun aleem
9|62|Yahlifoona billahi lakum liyurdookum wallahu warasooluhu ahaqqu an yurdoohu in kanoo mumineen
9|63|Alam ya‘lamoo annahu man yuhadidi Allaha warasoolahu faanna lahu nara jahannama khalidan feeha thalika alkhizyu al‘atheem
9|64|Yahtharu almunafiqoona an tunazzala ‘alayhim sooratun tunabbiohum bima fee quloobihim quli istahzioo inna Allaha mukhrijun ma tahtharoon
9|65|Walain saaltahum layaqoolunna innama kunna nakhoodu wanal‘abu qul abillahi waayatihi warasoolihi kuntum tastahzioon
9|66|La ta‘tathiroo qad kafartum ba‘da eemanikum in na‘fu ‘an taifatin minkum nu‘aththib taifatan biannahum kanoo mujrimeen
9|67|Almunafiqoona walmunafiqatu ba‘duhum min ba‘din yamuroona bilmunkari wayanhawna ‘ani alma‘roofi wayaqbidoona aydiyahum nasoo Allaha fanasiyahum inna almunafiqeena humu alfasiqoon
9|68|Wa‘ada Allahu almunafiqeena walmunafiqati walkuffara nara jahannama khalideena feeha hiya hasbuhum wala‘anahumu Allahu walahum ‘athabun muqeem
9|69|Kallatheena min qablikum kanoo ashadda minkum quwwatan waakthara amwalan waawladan fastamta‘oo bikhalaqihim fastamta‘tum bikhalaqikum kama istamta‘a allatheena min qablikum bikhalaqihim wakhudtum kallathee khadoo olaika habitat a‘maluhum fee addunya walakhirati waolaika humu alkhasiroon
9|70|Alam yatihim nabao allatheena min qablihim qawmi noohin wa‘adin wathamooda waqawmi ibraheema waashabi madyana walmutafikati atathum rusuluhum bilbayyinati fama kana Allahu liyathlimahum walakin kanoo anfusahum yathlimoon
9|71|Walmuminoona walmuminatu ba‘duhum awliyao ba‘din yamuroona bilma‘roofi wayanhawna ‘ani almunkari wayuqeemoona assalata wayutoona azzakata wayutee‘oona Allaha warasoolahu olaika sayarhamuhumu Allahu inna Allaha ‘azeezun hakeem
9|72|Wa‘ada Allahu almumineena walmuminati jannatin tajree min tahtiha alanharu khalideena feeha wamasakina tayyibatan fee jannati ‘adnin waridwanun mina Allahi akbaru thalika huwa alfawzu al‘atheem
9|73|Ya ayyuha annabiyyu jahidi alkuffara walmunafiqeena waghluth ‘alayhim wamawahum jahannamu wabisa almaseer
9|74|Yahlifoona billahi ma qaloo walaqad qaloo kalimata alkufri wakafaroo ba‘da islamihim wahammoo bima lam yanaloo wama naqamoo illa an aghnahumu Allahu warasooluhu min fadlihi fain yatooboo yaku khayran lahum wain yatawallaw yu‘aththibhumu Allahu ‘athaban aleeman fee addunya walakhirati wama lahum fee alardi min waliyyin wala naseer
9|75|Waminhum man ‘ahada Allaha lain atana min fadlihi lanassaddaqanna walanakoonanna mina assaliheen
9|76|Falamma atahum min fadlihi bakhiloo bihi watawallaw wahum mu‘ridoon
9|77|Faa‘qabahum nifaqan fee quloobihim ila yawmi yalqawnahu bima akhlafoo Allaha ma wa‘adoohu wabima kanoo yakthiboon
9|78|Alam ya‘lamoo anna Allaha ya‘lamu sirrahum wanajwahum waanna Allaha ‘allamu alghuyoob
9|79|Allatheena yalmizoona almuttawwi‘eena mina almumineena fee assadaqati wallatheena la yajidoona illa juhdahum fayaskharoona minhum sakhira Allahu minhum walahum ‘athabun aleem
9|80|Istaghfir lahum aw la tastaghfir lahum in tastaghfir lahum sab‘eena marratan falan yaghfira Allahu lahum thalika biannahum kafaroo billahi warasoolihi wallahu la yahdee alqawma alfasiqeen
9|81|Fariha almukhallafoona bimaq‘adihim khilafa rasooli Allahi wakarihoo an yujahidoo biamwalihim waanfusihim fee sabeeli Allahi waqaloo la tanfiroo fee alharri qul naru jahannama ashaddu harran law kanoo yafqahoon
9|82|Falyadhakoo qaleelan walyabkoo katheeran jazaan bima kanoo yaksiboon
9|83|Fain raja‘aka Allahu ila taifatin minhum fastathanooka lilkhurooji faqul lan takhrujoo ma‘iya abadan walan tuqatiloo ma‘iya ‘aduwwan innakum radeetum bilqu‘oodi awwala marratin faq‘udoo ma‘a alkhalifeen
9|84|Wala tusalli ‘ala ahadin minhum mata abadan wala taqum ‘ala qabrihi innahum kafaroo billahi warasoolihi wamatoo wahum fasiqoon
9|85|Wala tu‘jibka amwaluhum waawladuhum innama yureedu Allahu an yu‘aththibahum biha fee addunya watazhaqa anfusuhum wahum kafiroon
9|86|Waitha onzilat sooratun an aminoo billahi wajahidoo ma‘a rasoolihi istathanaka oloo attawli minhum waqaloo tharna nakun ma‘a alqa‘ideen
9|87|Radoo bian yakoonoo ma‘a alkhawalifi watubi‘a ‘ala quloobihim fahum la yafqahoon
9|88|Lakini arrasoolu wallatheena amanoo ma‘ahu jahadoo biamwalihim waanfusihim waolaika lahumu alkhayratu waolaika humu almuflihoon
9|89|a‘adda Allahu lahum jannatin tajree min tahtiha alanharu khalideena feeha thalika alfawzu al‘atheem
9|90|Wajaa almu‘aththiroona mina ala‘rabi liyuthana lahum waqa‘ada allatheena kathaboo Allaha warasoolahu sayuseebu allatheena kafaroo minhum ‘athabun aleem
9|91|Laysa ‘ala addu‘afai wala ‘ala almarda wala ‘ala allatheena la yajidoona ma yunfiqoona harajun itha nasahoo lillahi warasoolihi ma ‘ala almuhsineena min sabeelin wallahu ghafoorun raheem
9|92|Wala ‘ala allatheena itha ma atawka litahmilahum qulta la ajidu ma ahmilukum ‘alayhi tawallaw waa‘yunuhum tafeedu mina addam‘i hazanan alla yajidoo ma yunfiqoon
9|93|Innama assabeelu ‘ala allatheena yastathinoonaka wahum aghniyao radoo bian yakoonoo ma‘a alkhawalifi wataba‘a Allahu ‘ala quloobihim fahum la ya‘lamoon
9|94|Ya‘tathiroona ilaykum itha raja‘tum ilayhim qul la ta‘tathiroo lan numina lakum qad nabbaana Allahu min akhbarikum wasayara Allahu ‘amalakum warasooluhu thumma turaddoona ila ‘alimi alghaybi washshahadati fayunabbiokum bima kuntum ta‘maloon
9|95|Sayahlifoona billahi lakum itha inqalabtum ilayhim litu‘ridoo ‘anhum faa‘ridoo ‘anhum innahum rijsun wamawahum jahannamu jazaan bima kanoo yaksiboon
9|96|Yahlifoona lakum litardaw ‘anhum fain tardaw ‘anhum fainna Allaha la yarda ‘ani alqawmi alfasiqeen
9|97|Ala‘rabu ashaddu kufran wanifaqan waajdaru alla ya‘lamoo hudooda ma anzala Allahu ‘ala rasoolihi wallahu ‘aleemun hakeem
9|98|Wamina ala‘rabi man yattakhithu ma yunfiqu maghraman wayatarabbasu bikumu addawaira ‘alayhim dairatu assawi wallahu samee‘un ‘aleem
9|99|Wamina ala‘rabi man yuminu billahi walyawmi alakhiri wayattakhithu ma yunfiqu qurubatin ‘inda Allahi wasalawati arrasooli ala innaha qurbatun lahum sayudkhiluhumu Allahu fee rahmatihi inna Allaha ghafoorun raheem
9|100|Wassabiqoona alawwaloona mina almuhajireena walansari wallatheena ittaba‘oohum biihsanin radiya Allahu ‘anhum waradoo ‘anhu waa‘adda lahum jannatin tajree tahtaha alanharu khalideena feeha abadan thalika alfawzu al‘atheem
9|101|Wamimman hawlakum mina ala‘rabi munafiqoona wamin ahli almadeenati maradoo ‘ala annifaqi la ta‘lamuhum nahnu na‘lamuhum sanu‘aththibuhum marratayni thumma yuraddoona ila ‘athabin ‘atheem
9|102|Waakharoona i‘tarafoo bithunoobihim khalatoo ‘amalan salihan waakhara sayyian ‘asa Allahu an yatooba ‘alayhim inna Allaha ghafoorun raheem
9|103|Khuth min amwalihim sadaqatan tutahhiruhum watuzakkeehim biha wasalli ‘alayhim inna salataka sakanun lahum wallahu samee‘un ‘aleem
9|104|Alam ya‘lamoo anna Allaha huwa yaqbalu attawbata ‘an ‘ibadihi wayakhuthu assadaqati waanna Allaha huwa attawwabu arraheem
9|105|Waquli i‘maloo fasayara Allahu ‘amalakum warasooluhu walmuminoona wasaturaddoona ila ‘alimi alghaybi washshahadati fayunabbiokum bima kuntum ta‘maloon
9|106|Waakharoona murjawna liamri Allahi imma yu‘aththibuhum waimma yatoobu ‘alayhim wallahu ‘aleemun hakeem
9|107|Wallatheena ittakhathoo masjidan diraran wakufran watafreeqan bayna almumineena wairsadan liman haraba Allaha warasoolahu min qablu walayahlifunna in aradna illa alhusna wallahu yashhadu innahum lakathiboon
9|108|La taqum feehi abadan lamasjidun ossisa ‘ala attaqwa min awwali yawmin ahaqqu an taqooma feehi feehi rijalun yuhibboona an yatatahharoo wallahu yuhibbu almuttahhireen
9|109|Afaman assasa bunyanahu ‘ala taqwa mina Allahi waridwanin khayrun am man assasa bunyanahu ‘ala shafa jurufin harin fanhara bihi fee nari jahannama wallahu la yahdee alqawma aththalimeen
9|110|La yazalu bunyanuhumu allathee banaw reebatan fee quloobihim illa an taqatta‘a quloobuhum wallahu ‘aleemun hakeem
9|111|Inna Allaha ishtara mina almumineena anfusahum waamwalahum bianna lahumu aljannata yuqatiloona fee sabeeli Allahi fayaqtuloona wayuqtaloona wa‘dan ‘alayhi haqqan fee attawrati walinjeeli walqurani waman awfa bi‘ahdihi mina Allahi fastabshiroo bibay‘ikumu allathee baya‘tum bihi wathalika huwa alfawzu al‘atheem
9|112|Attaiboona al‘abidoona alhamidoona assaihoona arraki‘oona assajidoona alamiroona bilma‘roofi wannahoona ‘ani almunkari walhafithoona lihudoodi Allahi wabashshiri almumineen
9|113|Ma kana linnabiyyi wallatheena amanoo an yastaghfiroo lilmushrikeena walaw kanoo olee qurba min ba‘di ma tabayyana lahum annahum ashabu aljaheem
9|114|Wama kana istighfaru ibraheema liabeehi illa ‘an maw‘idatin wa‘adaha iyyahu falamma tabayyana lahu annahu ‘aduwwun lillahi tabarraa minhu inna ibraheema laawwahun haleem
9|115|Wama kana Allahu liyudilla qawman ba‘da ith hadahum hatta yubayyina lahum ma yattaqoona inna Allaha bikulli shayin ‘aleem
9|116|Inna Allaha lahu mulku assamawati walardi yuhyee wayumeetu wama lakum min dooni Allahi min waliyyin wala naseer
9|117|Laqad taba Allahu ‘ala annabiyyi walmuhajireena walansari allatheena ittaba‘oohu fee sa‘ati al‘usrati min ba‘di ma kada yazeeghu quloobu fareeqin minhum thumma taba ‘alayhim innahu bihim raoofun raheem
9|118|Wa‘ala aththalathati allatheena khullifoo hatta itha daqat ‘alayhimu alardu bima rahubat wadaqat ‘alayhim anfusuhum wathannoo an la maljaa mina Allahi illa ilayhi thumma taba ‘alayhim liyatooboo inna Allaha huwa attawwabu arraheem
9|119|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo ittaqoo Allaha wakoonoo ma‘a assadiqeen
9|120|Ma kana liahli almadeenati waman hawlahum mina ala‘rabi an yatakhallafoo ‘an rasooli Allahi wala yarghaboo bianfusihim ‘an nafsihi thalika biannahum la yuseebuhum thamaon wala nasabun wala makhmasatun fee sabeeli Allahi wala yataoona mawtian yagheethu alkuffara wala yanaloona min ‘aduwwin naylan illa kutiba lahum bihi ‘amalun salihun inna Allaha la yudee‘u ajra almuhsineen
9|121|Wala yunfiqoona nafaqatan sagheeratan wala kabeeratan wala yaqta‘oona wadiyan illa kutiba lahum liyajziyahumu Allahu ahsana ma kanoo ya‘maloon
9|122|Wama kana almuminoona liyanfiroo kaffatan falawla nafara min kulli firqatin minhum taifatun liyatafaqqahoo fee addeeni waliyunthiroo qawmahum itha raja‘oo ilayhim la‘allahum yahtharoon
9|123|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo qatiloo allatheena yaloonakum mina alkuffari walyajidoo feekum ghilthatan wa‘lamoo anna Allaha ma‘a almuttaqeen
9|124|Waitha ma onzilat sooratun faminhum man yaqoolu ayyukum zadathu hathihi eemanan faamma allatheena amanoo fazadathum eemanan wahum yastabshiroon
9|125|Waamma allatheena fee quloobihim maradun fazadathum rijsan ila rijsihim wamatoo wahum kafiroon
9|126|Awala yarawna annahum yuftanoona fee kulli ‘amin marratan aw marratayni thumma la yatooboona wala hum yaththakkaroon
9|127|Waitha ma onzilat sooratun nathara ba‘duhum ila ba‘din hal yarakum min ahadin thumma insarafoo sarafa Allahu quloobahum biannahum qawmun la yafqahoon
9|128|Laqad jaakum rasoolun min anfusikum ‘azeezun ‘alayhi ma ‘anittum hareesun ‘alaykum bilmumineena raoofun raheem
9|129|Fain tawallaw faqul hasbiya Allahu la ilaha illa huwa ‘alayhi tawakkaltu wahuwa rabbu al‘arshi al‘atheem
10|1|Aliflamra tilka ayatu alkitabi alhakeem
10|2|Akana linnasi ‘ajaban an awhayna ila rajulin minhum an anthiri annasa wabashshiri allatheena amanoo anna lahum qadama sidqin ‘inda rabbihim qala alkafiroona inna hatha lasahirun mubeen
10|3|Inna rabbakumu Allahu allathee khalaqa assamawati walarda fee sittati ayyamin thumma istawa ‘ala al‘arshi yudabbiru alamra ma min shafee‘in illa min ba‘di ithnihi thalikumu Allahu rabbukum fa‘budoohu afala tathakkaroon
10|4|Ilayhi marji‘ukum jamee‘an wa‘da Allahi haqqan innahu yabdao alkhalqa thumma yu‘eeduhu liyajziya allatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati bilqisti wallatheena kafaroo lahum sharabun min hameemin wa‘athabun aleemun bima kanoo yakfuroon
10|5|Huwa allathee ja‘ala ashshamsa diyaan walqamara nooran waqaddarahu manazila lita‘lamoo ‘adada assineena walhisaba ma khalaqa Allahu thalika illa bilhaqqi yufassilu alayati liqawmin ya‘lamoon
10|6|Inna fee ikhtilafi allayli wannahari wama khalaqa Allahu fee assamawati walardi laayatin liqawmin yattaqoon
10|7|Inna allatheena la yarjoona liqaana waradoo bilhayati addunya watmaannoo biha wallatheena hum ‘an ayatina ghafiloon
10|8|Olaika mawahumu annaru bima kanoo yaksiboon
10|9|Inna allatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati yahdeehim rabbuhum bieemanihim tajree min tahtihimu alanharu fee jannati anna‘eem
10|10|Da‘wahum feeha subhanaka allahumma watahiyyatuhum feeha salamun waakhiru da‘wahum ani alhamdu lillahi rabbi al‘alameen
10|11|Walaw yu‘ajjilu Allahu linnasi ashsharra isti‘jalahum bilkhayri laqudiya ilayhim ajaluhum fanatharu allatheena la yarjoona liqaana fee tughyanihim ya‘mahoon
10|12|Waitha massa alinsana addurru da‘ana lijanbihi aw qa‘idan aw qaiman falamma kashafna ‘anhu durrahu marra kaan lam yad‘una ila durrin massahu kathalika zuyyina lilmusrifeena ma kanoo ya‘maloon
10|13|Walaqad ahlakna alquroona min qablikum lamma thalamoo wajaathum rusuluhum bilbayyinati wama kanoo liyuminoo kathalika najzee alqawma almujrimeen
10|14|Thumma ja‘alnakum khalaifa fee alardi min ba‘dihim linanthura kayfa ta‘maloon
10|15|Waitha tutla ‘alayhim ayatuna bayyinatin qala allatheena la yarjoona liqaana iti biquranin ghayri hatha aw baddilhu qul ma yakoonu lee an obaddilahu min tilqai nafsee in attabi‘u illa ma yooha ilayya innee akhafu in ‘asaytu rabbee ‘athaba yawmin ‘atheem
10|16|Qul law shaa Allahu ma talawtuhu ‘alaykum wala adrakum bihi faqad labithtu feekum ‘umuran min qablihi afala ta‘qiloon
10|17|Faman athlamu mimmani iftara ‘ala Allahi kathiban aw kaththaba biayatihi innahu la yuflihu almujrimoon
10|18|Waya‘budoona min dooni Allahi ma la yadurruhum wala yanfa‘uhum wayaqooloona haolai shufa‘aona ‘inda Allahi qul atunabbioona Allaha bima la ya‘lamu fee assamawati wala fee alardi subhanahu wata‘ala ‘amma yushrikoon
10|19|Wama kana annasu illa ommatan wahidatan fakhtalafoo walawla kalimatun sabaqat min rabbika laqudiya baynahum feema feehi yakhtalifoon
10|20|Wayaqooloona lawla onzila ‘alayhi ayatun min rabbihi faqul innama alghaybu lillahi fantathiroo innee ma‘akum mina almuntathireen
10|21|Waitha athaqna annasa rahmatan min ba‘di darraa massathum itha lahum makrun fee ayatina quli Allahu asra‘u makran inna rusulana yaktuboona ma tamkuroon
10|22|Huwa allathee yusayyirukum fee albarri walbahri hatta itha kuntum fee alfulki wajarayna bihim bireehin tayyibatin wafarihoo biha jaatha reehun ‘asifun wajaahumu almawju min kulli makanin wathannoo annahum oheeta bihim da‘awoo Allaha mukhliseena lahu addeena lain anjaytana min hathihi lanakoonanna mina ashshakireen
10|23|Falamma anjahum itha hum yabghoona fee alardi bighayri alhaqqi ya ayyuha annasu innama baghyukum ‘ala anfusikum mata‘a alhayati addunya thumma ilayna marji‘ukum fanunabbiokum bima kuntum ta‘maloon
10|24|Innama mathalu alhayati addunya kamain anzalnahu mina assamai fakhtalata bihi nabatu alardi mimma yakulu annasu walan‘amu hatta itha akhathati alardu zukhrufaha wazzayyanat wathanna ahluha annahum qadiroona ‘alayha ataha amruna laylan aw naharan faja‘alnaha haseedan kaan lam taghna bilamsi kathalika nufassilu alayati liqawmin yatafakkaroon
10|25|Wallahu yad‘oo ila dari assalami wayahdee man yashao ila siratin mustaqeem
10|26|Lillatheena ahsanoo alhusna waziyadatun wala yarhaqu wujoohahum qatarun wala thillatun olaika ashabu aljannati hum feeha khalidoon
10|27|Wallatheena kasaboo assayyiati jazao sayyiatin bimithliha watarhaquhum thillatun ma lahum mina Allahi min ‘asimin kaannama oghshiyat wujoohuhum qita‘an mina allayli muthliman olaika ashabu annari hum feeha khalidoon
10|28|Wayawma nahshuruhum jamee‘an thumma naqoolu lillatheena ashrakoo makanakum antum washurakaokum fazayyalna baynahum waqala shurakaohum ma kuntum iyyana ta‘budoon
10|29|Fakafa billahi shaheedan baynana wabaynakum in kunna ‘an ‘ibadatikum laghafileen
10|30|Hunalika tabloo kullu nafsin ma aslafat waruddoo ila Allahi mawlahumu alhaqqi wadalla ‘anhum ma kanoo yaftaroon
10|31|Qul man yarzuqukum mina assamai walardi amman yamliku assam‘a walabsara waman yukhriju alhayya mina almayyiti wayukhriju almayyita mina alhayyi waman yudabbiru alamra fasayaqooloona Allahu faqul afala tattaqoon
10|32|Fathalikumu Allahu rabbukumu alhaqqu famatha ba‘da alhaqqi illa addalalu faanna tusrafoon
10|33|Kathalika haqqat kalimatu rabbika ‘ala allatheena fasaqoo annahum la yuminoon
10|34|Qul hal min shurakaikum man yabdao alkhalqa thumma yu‘eeduhu quli Allahu yabdao alkhalqa thumma yu‘eeduhu faanna tufakoon
10|35|Qul hal min shurakaikum man yahdee ila alhaqqi quli Allahu yahdee lilhaqqi afaman yahdee ila alhaqqi ahaqqu an yuttaba‘a amman la yahiddee illa an yuhda fama lakum kayfa tahkumoon
10|36|Wama yattabi‘u aktharuhum illa thannan inna aththanna la yughnee mina alhaqqi shayan inna Allaha ‘aleemun bima yaf‘aloon
10|37|Wama kana hatha alquranu an yuftara min dooni Allahi walakin tasdeeqa allathee bayna yadayhi watafseela alkitabi la rayba feehi min rabbi al‘alameen
10|38|Am yaqooloona iftarahu qul fatoo bisooratin mithlihi wad‘oo mani istata‘tum min dooni Allahi in kuntum sadiqeen
10|39|Bal kaththaboo bima lam yuheetoo bi‘ilmihi walamma yatihim taweeluhu kathalika kaththaba allatheena min qablihim fanthur kayfa kana ‘aqibatu aththalimeen
10|40|Waminhum man yuminu bihi waminhum man la yuminu bihi warabbuka a‘lamu bilmufsideen
10|41|Wain kaththabooka faqul lee ‘amalee walakum ‘amalukum antum bareeoona mimma a‘malu waana bareeon mimma ta‘maloon
10|42|Waminhum man yastami‘oona ilayka afaanta tusmi‘u assumma walaw kanoo la ya‘qiloon
10|43|Waminhum man yanthuru ilayka afaanta tahdee al‘umya walaw kanoo la yubsiroon
10|44|Inna Allaha la yathlimu annasa shayan walakinna annasa anfusahum yathlimoon
10|45|Wayawma yahshuruhum kaan lam yalbathoo illa sa‘atan mina annahari yata‘arafoona baynahum qad khasira allatheena kaththaboo biliqai Allahi wama kanoo muhtadeen
10|46|Waimma nuriyannaka ba‘da allathee na‘iduhum aw natawaffayannaka failayna marji‘uhum thumma Allahu shaheedun ‘ala ma yaf‘aloon
10|47|Walikulli ommatin rasoolun faitha jaa rasooluhum qudiya baynahum bilqisti wahum la yuthlamoon
10|48|Wayaqooloona mata hatha alwa‘du in kuntum sadiqeen
10|49|Qul la amliku linafsee darran wala naf‘an illa ma shaa Allahu likulli ommatin ajalun itha jaa ajaluhum fala yastakhiroona sa‘atan wala yastaqdimoon
10|50|Qul araaytum in atakum ‘athabuhu bayatan aw naharan matha yasta‘jilu minhu almujrimoon
10|51|Athumma itha ma waqa‘a amantum bihi alana waqad kuntum bihi tasta‘jiloon
10|52|Thumma qeela lillatheena thalamoo thooqoo ‘athaba alkhuldi hal tujzawna illa bima kuntum taksiboon
10|53|Wayastanbioonaka ahaqqun huwa qul ee warabbee innahu lahaqqun wama antum bimu‘jizeen
10|54|Walaw anna likulli nafsin thalamat ma fee alardi laftadat bihi waasarroo annadamata lamma raawoo al‘athaba waqudiya baynahum bilqisti wahum la yuthlamoon
10|55|Ala inna lillahi ma fee assamawati walardi ala inna wa‘da Allahi haqqun walakinna aktharahum la ya‘lamoon
10|56|Huwa yuhyee wayumeetu wailayhi turja‘oon
10|57|Ya ayyuha annasu qad jaatkum maw‘ithatun min rabbikum washifaon lima fee assudoori wahudan warahmatun lilmumineen
10|58|Qul bifadli Allahi wabirahmatihi fabithalika falyafrahoo huwa khayrun mimma yajma‘oon
10|59|Qul araaytum ma anzala Allahu lakum min rizqin faja‘altum minhu haraman wahalalan qul allahu athina lakum am ‘ala Allahi taftaroon
10|60|Wama thannu allatheena yaftaroona ‘ala Allahi alkathiba yawma alqiyamati inna Allaha lathoo fadlin ‘ala annasi walakinna aktharahum la yashkuroon
10|61|Wama takoonu fee shanin wama tatloo minhu min quranin wala ta‘maloona min ‘amalin illa kunna ‘alaykum shuhoodan ith tufeedoona feehi wama ya‘zubu ‘an rabbika min mithqali tharratin fee alardi wala fee assamai wala asghara min thalika wala akbara illa fee kitabin mubeen
10|62|Ala inna awliyaa Allahi la khawfun ‘alayhim wala hum yahzanoon
10|63|Allatheena amanoo wakanoo yattaqoon
10|64|Lahumu albushra fee alhayati addunya wafee alakhirati la tabdeela likalimati Allahi thalika huwa alfawzu al‘atheem
10|65|Wala yahzunka qawluhum inna al‘izzata lillahi jamee‘an huwa assamee‘u al‘aleem
10|66|Ala inna lillahi man fee assamawati waman fee alardi wama yattabi‘u allatheena yad‘oona min dooni Allahi shurakaa in yattabi‘oona illa aththanna wain hum illa yakhrusoon
10|67|Huwa allathee ja‘ala lakumu allayla litaskunoo feehi wannahara mubsiran inna fee thalika laayatin liqawmin yasma‘oon
10|68|Qaloo ittakhatha Allahu waladan subhanahu huwa alghaniyyu lahu ma fee assamawati wama fee alardi in ‘indakum min sultanin bihatha ataqooloona ‘ala Allahi ma la ta‘lamoon
10|69|Qul inna allatheena yaftaroona ‘ala Allahi alkathiba la yuflihoon
10|70|Mata‘un fee addunya thumma ilayna marji‘uhum thumma nutheequhumu al‘athaba ashshadeeda bima kanoo yakfuroon
10|71|Watlu ‘alayhim nabaa noohin ith qala liqawmihi ya qawmi in kana kabura ‘alaykum maqamee watathkeeree biayati Allahi fa‘ala Allahi tawakkaltu faajmi‘oo amrakum washurakaakum thumma la yakun amrukum ‘alaykum ghummatan thumma iqdoo ilayya wala tunthiroon
10|72|Fain tawallaytum fama saaltukum min ajrin in ajriya illa ‘ala Allahi waomirtu an akoona mina almuslimeen
10|73|Fakaththaboohu fanajjaynahu waman ma‘ahu fee alfulki waja‘alnahum khalaifa waaghraqna allatheena kaththaboo biayatina fanthur kayfa kana ‘aqibatu almunthareen
10|74|Thumma ba‘athna min ba‘dihi rusulan ila qawmihim fajaoohum bilbayyinati fama kanoo liyuminoo bima kaththaboo bihi min qablu kathalika natba‘u ‘ala quloobi almu‘tadeen
10|75|Thumma ba‘athna min ba‘dihim moosa waharoona ila fir‘awna wamalaihi biayatina fastakbaroo wakanoo qawman mujrimeen
10|76|Falamma jaahumu alhaqqu min ‘indina qaloo inna hatha lasihrun mubeen
10|77|Qala moosa ataqooloona lilhaqqi lamma jaakum asihrun hatha wala yuflihu assahiroon
10|78|Qaloo ajitana litalfitana ‘amma wajadna ‘alayhi abaana watakoona lakuma alkibriyao fee alardi wama nahnu lakuma bimumineen
10|79|Waqala fir‘awnu itoonee bikulli sahirin ‘aleem
10|80|Falamma jaa assaharatu qala lahum moosa alqoo ma antum mulqoon
10|81|Falamma alqaw qala moosa ma jitum bihi assihru inna Allaha sayubtiluhu inna Allaha la yuslihu ‘amala almufsideen
10|82|Wayuhiqqu Allahu alhaqqa bikalimatihi walaw kariha almujrimoon
10|83|Fama amana limoosa illa thurriyyatun min qawmihi ‘ala khawfin min fir‘awna wamalaihim an yaftinahum wainna fir‘awna la‘alin fee alardi wainnahu lamina almusrifeen
10|84|Waqala moosa ya qawmi in kuntum amantum billahi fa‘alayhi tawakkaloo in kuntum muslimeen
10|85|Faqaloo ‘ala Allahi tawakkalna rabbana la taj‘alna fitnatan lilqawmi aththalimeen
10|86|Wanajjina birahmatika mina alqawmi alkafireen
10|87|Waawhayna ila moosa waakheehi an tabawwaa liqawmikuma bimisra buyootan waj‘aloo buyootakum qiblatan waaqeemoo assalata wabashshiri almumineen
10|88|Waqala moosa rabbana innaka atayta fir‘awna wamalaahu zeenatan waamwalan fee alhayati addunya rabbana liyudilloo ‘an sabeelika rabbana itmis ‘ala amwalihim washdud ‘ala quloobihim fala yuminoo hatta yarawoo al‘athaba alaleem
10|89|Qala qad ojeebat da‘watukuma fastaqeema wala tattabi‘anni sabeela allatheena la ya‘lamoon
10|90|Wajawazna bibanee israeela albahra faatba‘ahum fir‘awnu wajunooduhu baghyan wa‘adwan hatta itha adrakahu algharaqu qala amantu annahu la ilaha illa allathee amanat bihi banoo israeela waana mina almuslimeen
10|91|Alana waqad ‘asayta qablu wakunta mina almufsideen
10|92|Falyawma nunajjeeka bibadanika litakoona liman khalfaka ayatan wainna katheeran mina annasi ‘an ayatina laghafiloon
10|93|Walaqad bawwana banee israeela mubawwaa sidqin warazaqnahum mina attayyibati fama ikhtalafoo hatta jaahumu al‘ilmu inna rabbaka yaqdee baynahum yawma alqiyamati feema kanoo feehi yakhtalifoon
10|94|Fain kunta fee shakkin mimma anzalnna ilayka fasali allatheena yaqraoona alkitaba min qablika laqad jaaka alhaqqu min rabbika fala takoonanna mina almumtareen
10|95|Wala takoonanna mina allatheena kaththaboo biayati Allahi fatakoona mina alkhasireen
10|96|Inna allatheena haqqat ‘alayhim kalimatu rabbika la yuminoon
10|97|Walaw jaathum kullu ayatin hatta yarawoo al‘athaba alaleem
10|98|Falawla kanat qaryatun amanat fanafa‘aha eemanuha illa qawma yoonusa lamma amanoo kashafna ‘anhum ‘athaba alkhizyi fee alhayati addunya wamatta‘nahum ila heen
10|99|Walaw shaa rabbuka laamana man fee alardi kulluhum jamee‘an afaanta tukrihu annasa hatta yakoonoo mumineen
10|100|Wama kana linafsin an tumina illa biithni Allahi wayaj‘alu arrijsa ‘ala allatheena la ya‘qiloon
10|101|Quli onthuroo matha fee assamawati walardi wama tughnee alayatu wannuthuru ‘an qawmin la yuminoon
10|102|Fahal yantathiroona illa mithla ayyami allatheena khalaw min qablihim qul fantathiroo innee ma‘akum mina almuntathireen
10|103|Thumma nunajjee rusulana wallatheena amanoo kathalika haqqan ‘alayna nunjee almumineen
10|104|Qul ya ayyuha annasu in kuntum fee shakkin min deenee fala a‘budu allatheena ta‘budoona min dooni Allahi walakin a‘budu Allaha allathee yatawaffakum waomirtu an akoona mina almumineen
10|105|Waan aqim wajhaka liddeeni haneefan wala takoonanna mina almushrikeen
10|106|Wala tad‘u min dooni Allahi ma la yanfa‘uka wala yadurruka fain fa‘alta fainnaka ithan mina aththalimeen
10|107|Wain yamsaska Allahu bidurrin fala kashifa lahu illa huwa wain yuridka bikhayrin fala radda lifadlihi yuseebu bihi man yashao min ‘ibadihi wahuwa alghafooru arraheem
10|108|Qul ya ayyuha annasu qad jaakumu alhaqqu min rabbikum famani ihtada fainnama yahtadee linafsihi waman dalla fainnama yadillu ‘alayha wama ana ‘alaykum biwakeel
10|109|Wattabi‘ ma yooha ilayka wasbir hatta yahkuma Allahu wahuwa khayru alhakimeen
11|1|Aliflamra kitabun ohkimat ayatuhu thumma fussilat min ladun hakeemin khabeer
11|2|Alla ta‘budoo illa Allaha innanee lakum minhu natheerun wabasheer
11|3|Waani istaghfiroo rabbakum thumma tooboo ilayhi yumatti‘kum mata‘an hasanan ila ajalin musamman wayuti kulla thee fadlin fadlahu wain tawallaw fainee akhafu ‘alaykum ‘athaba yawmin kabeer
11|4|Ila Allahi marji‘ukum wahuwa ‘ala kulli shayin qadeer
11|5|Ala innahum yathnoona sudoorahum liyastakhfoo minhu ala heena yastaghshoona thiyabahum ya‘lamu ma yusirroona wama yu‘linoona innahu ‘aleemun bithati assudoor
11|6|Wama min dabbatin fee alardi illa ‘ala Allahi rizquha waya‘lamu mustaqarraha wamustawda‘aha kullun fee kitabin mubeen
11|7|Wahuwa allathee khalaqa assamawati walarda fee sittati ayyamin wakana ‘arshuhu ‘ala almai liyabluwakum ayyukum ahsanu ‘amalan walain qulta innakum mab‘oothoona min ba‘di almawti layaqoolanna allatheena kafaroo in hatha illa sihrun mubeen
11|8|Walain akhkharna ‘anhumu al‘athaba ila ommatin ma‘doodatin layaqoolunna ma yahbisuhu ala yawma yateehim laysa masroofan ‘anhum wahaqa bihim ma kanoo bihi yastahzioon
11|9|Walain athaqna alinsana minna rahmatan thumma naza‘naha minhu innahu layaoosun kafoor
11|10|Walain athaqnahu na‘maa ba‘da darraa massathu layaqoolanna thahaba assayyiatu ‘annee innahu lafarihun fakhoor
11|11|Illa allatheena sabaroo wa‘amiloo assalihati olaika lahum maghfiratun waajrun kabeer
11|12|Fala‘allaka tarikun ba‘da ma yooha ilayka wadaiqun bihi sadruka an yaqooloo lawla onzila ‘alayhi kanzun aw jaa ma‘ahu malakun innama anta natheerun wallahu ‘ala kulli shayin wakeel
11|13|Am yaqooloona iftarahu qul fatoo bi‘ashri suwarin mithlihi muftarayatin wad‘oo mani istata‘tum min dooni Allahi in kuntum sadiqeen
11|14|Fai llam yastajeeboo lakum fa‘lamoo annama onzila bi‘ilmi Allahi waan la ilaha illa huwa fahal antum muslimoon
11|15|Man kana yureedu alhayata addunya wazeenataha nuwaffi ilayhim a‘malahum feeha wahum feeha la yubkhasoon
11|16|Olaika allatheena laysa lahum fee alakhirati illa annaru wahabita ma sana‘oo feeha wabatilun ma kanoo ya‘maloon
11|17|Afaman kana ‘ala bayyinatin min rabbihi wayatloohu shahidun minhu wamin qablihi kitabu moosa imaman warahmatan olaika yuminoona bihi waman yakfur bihi mina alahzabi fannaru maw‘iduhu fala taku fee miryatin minhu innahu alhaqqu min rabbika walakinna akthara annasi la yuminoon
11|18|Waman athlamu mimmani iftara ‘ala Allahi kathiban olaika yu‘radoona ‘ala rabbihim wayaqoolu alashhadu haolai allatheena kathaboo ‘ala rabbihim ala la‘natu Allahi ‘ala aththalimeen
11|19|Allatheena yasuddoona ‘an sabeeli Allahi wayabghoonaha ‘iwajan wahum bilakhirati hum kafiroon
11|20|Olaika lam yakoonoo mu‘jizeena fee alardi wama kana lahum min dooni Allahi min awliyaa yuda‘afu lahumu al‘athabu ma kanoo yastatee‘oona assam‘a wama kanoo yubsiroon
11|21|Olaika allatheena khasiroo anfusahum wadalla ‘anhum ma kanoo yaftaroon
11|22|La jarama annahum fee alakhirati humu alakhsaroon
11|23|Inna allatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo asalihati waakhbatoo ila rabbihim olaika ashabu aljannati hum feeha khalidoon
11|24|Mathalu alfareeqayni kala‘ma walasammi walbaseeri wassamee‘i hal yastawiyani mathalan afala tathakkaroon
11|25|Walaqad arsalna noohan ila qawmihi innee lakum natheerun mubeen
11|26|An la ta‘budoo illa Allaha innee akhafu ‘alaykum ‘athaba yawmin aleem
11|27|Faqala almalao allatheena kafaroo min qawmihi ma naraka illa basharan mithlana wama naraka ittaba‘aka illa allatheena hum arathiluna badiya arrayi wama nara lakum ‘alayna min fadlin bal nathunnukum kathibeen
11|28|Qala ya qawmi araaytum in kuntu ‘ala bayyinatin min rabbee waatanee rahmatan min ‘indihi fa‘ummiyat ‘alaykum anulzimukumooha waantum laha karihoon
11|29|Waya qawmi la asalukum ‘alayhi malan in ajriya illa ‘ala Allahi wama ana bitaridi allatheena amanoo innahum mulaqoo rabbihim walakinnee arakum qawman tajhaloon
11|30|Waya qawmi man yansurunee mina Allahi in taradtuhum afala tathakkaroon
11|31|Wala aqoolu lakum ‘indee khazainu Allahi wala a‘lamu alghayba wala aqoolu innee malakun wala aqoolu lillatheena tazdaree a‘yunukum lan yutiyahumu Allahu khayran Allahu a‘lamu bima fee anfusihim innee ithan lamina aththalimeen
11|32|Qaloo ya noohu qad jadaltana faaktharta jidalana fatina bima ta‘iduna in kunta mina assadiqeen
11|33|Qala innama yateekum bihi Allahu in shaa wama antum bimu‘jizeen
11|34|Wala yanfa‘ukum nushee in aradtu an ansaha lakum in kana Allahu yureedu an yughwiyakum huwa rabbukum wailayhi turja‘oon
11|35|Am yaqooloona iftarahu qul ini iftaraytuhu fa‘alayya ijramee waana bareeon mimma tujrimoon
11|36|Waoohiya ila noohin annahu lan yumina min qawmika illa man qad amana fala tabtais bima kanoo yaf‘aloon
11|37|Wasna‘i alfulka bia‘yunina wawahyina wala tukhatibnee fee allatheena thalamoo innahum mughraqoon
11|38|Wayasna‘u alfulka wakullama marra ‘alayhi malaon min qawmihi sakhiroo minhu qala in taskharoo minna fainna naskharu minkum kama taskharoon
11|39|Fasawfa ta‘lamoona man yateehi ‘athabun yukhzeehi wayahillu ‘alayhi ‘athabun muqeem
11|40|Hatta itha jaa amruna wafara attannooru qulna ihmil feeha min kullin zawjayni ithnayni waahlaka illa man sabaqa ‘alayhi alqawlu waman amana wama amana ma‘ahu illa qaleel
11|41|Waqala irkaboo feeha bismi Allahi majraha wamursaha inna rabbee laghafoorun raheem
11|42|Wahiya tajree bihim fee mawjin kaljibali wanada noohunu ibnahu wakana fee ma‘zilin ya bunayya irkab ma‘ana wala takun ma‘a alkafireen
11|43|Qala saawee ila jabalin ya‘simunee mina almai qala la ‘asima alyawma min amri Allahi illa man rahima wahala baynahuma almawju fakana mina almughraqeen
11|44|Waqeela ya ardu ibla‘ee maaki waya samao aqli‘ee wagheeda almao waqudiya alamru wastawat ‘ala aljoodiyyi waqeela bu‘dan lilqawmi aththalimeen
11|45|Wanada noohun rabbahu faqala rabbi inna ibnee min ahlee wainna wa‘daka alhaqqu waanta ahkamu alhakimeen
11|46|Qala ya noohu innahu laysa min ahlika innahu ‘amalun ghayru salihin fala tasalni ma laysa laka bihi ‘ilmun innee a‘ithuka an takoona mina aljahileen
11|47|Qala rabbi innee a‘oothu bika an asalaka ma laysa lee bihi ‘ilmun wailla taghfir lee watarhamnee akun mina alkhasireen
11|48|Qeela ya noohu ihbit bisalamin minna wabarakatin ‘alayka wa‘ala omamin mimman ma‘aka waomamun sanumatti‘uhum thumma yamassuhum minna ‘athabun aleem
11|49|Tilka min anbai alghaybi nooheeha ilayka ma kunta ta‘lamuha anta wala qawmuka min qabli hatha fasbir inna al‘aqibata lilmuttaqeen
11|50|Waila ‘adin akhahum hoodan qala ya qawmi o‘budoo Allaha ma lakum min ilahin ghayruhu in antum illa muftaroon
11|51|Ya qawmi la asalukum ‘alayhi ajran in ajriya illa ‘ala allathee fataranee afala ta‘qiloon
11|52|Waya qawmi istaghfiroo rabbakum thumma tooboo ilayhi yursili assamaa ‘alaykum midraran wayazidkum quwwatan ila quwwatikum wala tatawallaw mujrimeen
11|53|Qaloo ya hoodu ma jitana bibayyinatin wama nahnu bitarikee alihatina ‘an qawlika wama nahnu laka bimumineen
11|54|In naqoolu illa i‘taraka ba‘du alihatina bisooin qala innee oshhidu Allaha washhadoo annee bareeon mimma tushrikoon
11|55|Min doonihi fakeedoonee jamee‘an thumma la tunthiroon
11|56|Innee tawakkaltu ‘ala Allahi rabbee warabbikum ma min dabbatin illa huwa akhithun binasiyatiha inna rabbee ‘ala siratin mustaqeem
11|57|Fain tawallaw faqad ablaghtukum ma orsiltu bihi ilaykum wayastakhlifu rabbee qawman ghayrakum wala tadurroonahu shayan inna rabbee ‘ala kulli shayin hafeeth
11|58|Walamma jaa amruna najjayna hoodan wallatheena amanoo ma‘ahu birahmatin minna wanajjaynahum min ‘athabin ghaleeth
11|59|Watilka ‘adun jahadoo biayati rabbihim wa‘asaw rusulahu wattaba‘oo amra kulli jabbarin ‘aneed
11|60|Waotbi‘oo fee hathihi addunya la‘natan wayawma alqiyamati ala inna ‘adan kafaroo rabbahum ala bu‘dan li‘adin qawmi hood
11|61|Waila thamooda akhahum salihan qala ya qawmi o‘budoo Allaha ma lakum min ilahin ghayruhu huwa anshaakum mina alardi wasta‘marakum feeha fastaghfiroohu thumma tooboo ilayhi inna rabbee qareebun mujeeb
11|62|Qaloo ya salihu qad kunta feena marjuwwan qabla hatha atanhana an na‘buda ma ya‘budu abaona wainnana lafee shakkin mimma tad‘oona ilayhi mureeb
11|63|Qala ya qawmi araaytum in kuntu ‘ala bayyinatin min rabbee waatanee minhu rahmatan faman yansurunee mina Allahi in ‘asaytuhu fama tazeedoonanee ghayra takhseer
11|64|Waya qawmi hathihi naqatu Allahi lakum ayatan fatharooha takul fee ardi Allahi wala tamassooha bisooin fayakhuthakum ‘athabun qareeb
11|65|Fa‘aqarooha faqala tamatta‘oo fee darikum thalathata ayyamin thalika wa‘dun ghayru makthoob
11|66|Falamma jaa amruna najjayna salihan wallatheena amanoo ma‘ahu birahmatin minna wamin khizyi yawmiithin inna rabbaka huwa alqawiyyu al‘azeez
11|67|Waakhatha allatheena thalamoo assayhatu faasbahoo fee diyarihim jathimeen
11|68|Kaan lam yaghnaw feeha ala inna thamooda kafaroo rabbahum ala bu‘dan lithamood
11|69|Walaqad jaat rusuluna ibraheema bilbushra qaloo salaman qala salamun fama labitha an jaa bi‘ijlin haneeth
11|70|Falamma raa aydiyahum la tasilu ilayhi nakirahum waawjasa minhum kheefatan qaloo la takhaf inna orsilna ila qawmi loot
11|71|Wamraatuhu qaimatun fadahikat fabashsharnaha biishaqa wamin warai ishaqa ya‘qoob
11|72|Qalat ya waylata aalidu waana ‘ajoozun wahatha ba‘lee shaykhan inna hatha lashayon ‘ajeeb
11|73|Qaloo ata‘jabeena min amri Allahi rahmatu Allahi wabarakatuhu ‘alaykum ahla albayti innahu hameedun majeed
11|74|Falamma thahaba ‘an ibraheema arraw‘u wajaathu albushra yujadiluna fee qawmi loot
11|75|Inna ibraheema lahaleemun awwahun muneeb
11|76|Ya ibraheemu a‘rid ‘an hatha innahu qad jaa amru rabbika wainnahum ateehim ‘athabun ghayru mardood
11|77|Walamma jaat rusuluna lootan seea bihim wadaqa bihim thar‘an waqala hatha yawmun ‘aseeb
11|78|Wajaahu qawmuhu yuhra‘oona ilayhi wamin qablu kanoo ya‘maloona assayyiati qala ya qawmi haolai banatee hunna atharu lakum fattaqoo Allaha wala tukhzooni fee dayfee alaysa minkum rajulun rasheed
11|79|Qaloo laqad ‘alimta ma lana fee banatika min haqqin wainnaka lata‘lamu ma nureed
11|80|Qala law anna lee bikum quwwatan aw awee ila ruknin shadeed
11|81|Qaloo ya lootu inna rusulu rabbika lan yasiloo ilayka faasri biahlika biqit‘in mina allayli wala yaltafit minkum ahadun illa imraataka innahu museebuha ma asabahum inna maw‘idahumu assubhu alaysa assubhu biqareeb
11|82|Falamma jaa amruna ja‘alna ‘aliyaha safilaha waamtarna ‘alayha hijaratan min sijjeelin mandood
11|83|Musawwamatan ‘inda rabbika wama hiya mina aththalimeena biba‘eed
11|84|Waila madyana akhahum shu‘ayban qala ya qawmi o‘budoo Allaha ma lakum min ilahin ghayruhu wala tanqusoo almikyala walmeezana innee arakum bikhayrin wainnee akhafu ‘alaykum ‘athaba yawmin muheet
11|85|Waya qawmi awfoo almikyala walmeezana bilqisti wala tabkhasoo annasa ashyaahum wala ta‘thaw fee alardi mufsideen
11|86|Baqiyyatu Allahi khayrun lakum in kuntum mumineena wama ana ‘alaykum bihafeeth
11|87|Qaloo ya shu‘aybu asalatuka tamuruka an natruka ma ya‘budu abaona aw an naf‘ala fee amwalina ma nashao innaka laanta alhaleemu arrasheed
11|88|Qala ya qawmi araaytum in kuntu ‘ala bayyinatin min rabbee warazaqanee minhu rizqan hasanan wama oreedu an okhalifakum ila ma anhakum ‘anhu in oreedu illa alislaha ma istata‘tu wama tawfeeqee illa billahi ‘alayhi tawakkaltu wailayhi oneeb
11|89|Waya qawmi la yajrimannakum shiqaqee an yuseebakum mithlu ma asaba qawma noohin aw qawma hoodin aw qawma salihin wama qawmu lootin minkum biba‘eed
11|90|Wastaghfiroo rabbakum thumma tooboo ilayhi inna rabbee raheemun wadood
11|91|Qaloo ya shu‘aybu ma nafqahu katheeran mimma taqoolu wainna lanaraka feena da‘eefan walawla rahtuka larajamnaka wama anta ‘alayna bi‘azeez
11|92|Qala ya qawmi arahtee a‘azzu ‘alaykum mina Allahi wattakhathtumoohu waraakum thihriyyan inna rabbee bima ta‘maloona muheet
11|93|Waya qawmi i‘maloo ‘ala makanatikum innee ‘amilun sawfa ta‘lamoona man yateehi ‘athabun yukhzeehi waman huwa kathibun wartaqiboo inne ma‘akum raqeeb
11|94|Walamma jaa amruna najjayna shu‘ayban wallatheena amanoo ma‘ahu birahmatin minna waakhathati allatheena thalamoo assayhatu faasbahoo fee diyarihim jathimeen
11|95|Kaan lam yaghnaw feeha ala bu‘dan limadyana kama ba‘idat thamood
11|96|Walaqad arsalna moosa biayatina wasultanin mubeen
11|97|Ila fir‘awna wamalaihi fattaba‘oo amra fir‘awna wama amru fir‘awna birasheed
11|98|Yaqdumu qawmahu yawma alqiyamati faawradahumu annara wabisa alwirdu almawrood
11|99|Waotbi‘oo fee hathihi la‘natan wayawma alqiyamati bisa arrifdu almarfood
11|100|Thalika min anbai alqura naqussuhu ‘alayka minha qaimun wahaseed
11|101|Wama thalamnahum walakin thalamoo anfusahum fama aghnat ‘anhum alihatuhumu allatee yad‘oona min dooni Allahi min shayin lamma jaa amru rabbika wama zadoohum ghayra tatbeeb
11|102|Wakathalika akhthu rabbika itha akhatha alqura wahiya thalimatun inna akhthahu aleemun shadeed
11|103|Inna fee thalika laayatan liman khafa ‘athaba alakhirati thalika yawmun majmoo‘un lahu annasu wathalika yawmun mashhoodun
11|104|Wama nuakhkhiruhu illa liajalin ma‘dood
11|105|Yawma yati la takallamu nafsun illa biithnihi faminhum shaqiyyun wasa‘eed
11|106|Faamma allatheena shaqoo fafee annari lahum feeha zafeerun washaheeq
11|107|Khalideena feeha ma damati assamawatu walardu illa ma shaa rabbuka inna rabbaka fa‘‘alun lima yureed
11|108|Waamma allatheena su‘idoo fafee aljannati khalideena feeha ma damati assamawatu walardu illa ma shaa rabbuka ‘ataan ghayra majthooth
11|109|Fala taku fee miryatin mimma ya‘budu haolai ma ya‘budoona illa kama ya‘budu abaohum min qablu wainna lamuwaffoohum naseebahum ghayra manqoos
11|110|Walaqad atayna moosa alkitaba fakhtulifa feehi walawla kalimatun sabaqat min rabbika laqudiya baynahum wainnahum lafee shakkin minhu mureeb
11|111|Wainna kullan lamma layuwaffiyannahum rabbuka a‘malahum innahu bima ya‘maloona khabeer
11|112|Fastaqim kama omirta waman taba ma‘aka wala tatghaw innahu bima ta‘maloona baseer
11|113|Wala tarkanoo ila allatheena thalamoo fatamassakumu annaru wama lakum min dooni Allahi min awliyaa thumma la tunsaroon
11|114|Waaqimi assalata tarafayi annahari wazulafan mina allayli inna alhasanati yuthhibna assayyiati thalika thikra liththakireen
11|115|Wasbir fainna Allaha la yudee‘u ajra almuhsineen
11|116|Falawla kana mina alqurooni min qablikum oloo baqiyyatin yanhawna ‘ani alfasadi fee alardi illa qaleelan mimman anjayna minhum wattaba‘a allatheena thalamoo ma otrifoo feehi wakanoo mujrimeen
11|117|Wama kana rabbuka liyuhlika alqura bithulmin waahluha muslihoon
11|118|Walaw shaa rabbuka laja‘ala annasa ommatan wahidatan wala yazaloona mukhtalifeen
11|119|Illa man rahima rabbuka walithalika khalaqahum watammat kalimatu rabbika laamlaanna jahannama mina aljinnati wannasi ajma‘een
11|120|Wakullan naqussu ‘alayka min anbai arrusuli ma nuthabbitu bihi fuadaka wajaaka fee hathihi alhaqqu wamaw‘ithatun wathikra lilmumineen
11|121|Waqul lillatheena la yuminoona i‘maloo ‘ala makanatikum inna ‘amiloon
11|122|Wantathiroo inna muntathiroon
11|123|Walillahi ghaybu assamawati walardi wailayhi yurja‘u alamru kulluhu fa‘budhu watawakkal ‘alayhi wama rabbuka bighafilin ‘amma ta‘maloon
12|1|Aliflamra tilka ayatu alkitabi almubeen
12|2|Inna anzalnahu quranan ‘arabiyyan la‘allakum ta‘qiloon
12|3|Nahnu naqussu ‘alayka ahsana alqasasi bima awhayna ilayka hatha alqurana wain kunta min qablihi lamina alghafileen
12|4|Ith qala yoosufu liabeehi ya abati innee raaytu ahada ‘ashara kawkaban washshamsa walqamara raaytuhum lee sajideen
12|5|Qala ya bunayya la taqsus ruyaka ‘ala ikhwatika fayakeedoo laka kaydan inna ashshaytana lilinsani ‘aduwwun mubeen
12|6|Wakathalika yajtabeeka rabbuka wayu‘allimuka min taweeli alahadeethi wayutimmu ni‘matahu ‘alayka wa‘ala ali ya‘qooba kama atammaha ‘ala abawayka min qablu ibraheema waishaqa inna rabbaka ‘aleemun hakeem
12|7|Laqad kana fee yoosufa waikhwatihi ayatun lissaileen
12|8|Ith qaloo layoosufu waakhoohu ahabbu ila abeena minna wanahnu ‘usbatun inna abana lafee dalalin mubeen
12|9|Oqtuloo yoosufa awi itrahoohu ardan yakhlu lakum wajhu abeekum watakoonoo min ba‘dihi qawman saliheen
12|10|Qala qailun minhum la taqtuloo yoosufa waalqoohu fee ghayabati aljubbi yaltaqithu ba‘du assayyarati in kuntum fa‘ileen
12|11|Qaloo ya abana ma laka la tamanna ‘ala yoosufa wainna lahu lanasihoon
12|12|Arsilhu ma‘ana ghadan yarta‘ wayal‘ab wainna lahu lahafithoon
12|13|Qala innee layahzununee an thathhaboo bihi waakhafu an yakulahu aththibu waantum ‘anhu ghafiloon
12|14|Qaloo lain akalahu aththibu wanahnu ‘usbatun inna ithan lakhasiroon
12|15|Falamma thahaboo bihi waajma‘oo an yaj‘aloohu fee ghayabati aljubbi waawhayna ilayhi latunabiannahum biamrihim hatha wahum la yash‘uroon
12|16|Wajaoo abahum ‘ishaan yabkoon
12|17|Qaloo ya abana inna thahabna nastabiqu watarakna yoosufa ‘inda mata‘ina faakalahu aththibu wama anta bimuminin lana walaw kunna sadiqeen
12|18|Wajaoo ‘ala qameesihi bidamin kathibin qala bal sawwalat lakum anfusukum amran fasabrun jameelun wallahu almusta‘anu ‘ala ma tasifoon
12|19|Wajaat sayyaratun faarsaloo waridahum faadla dalwahu qala ya bushra hatha ghulamun waasarroohu bida‘atan wallahu ‘aleemun bima ya‘maloon
12|20|Washarawhu bithamanin bakhsin darahima ma‘doodatin wakanoo feehi mina azzahideen
12|21|Waqala allathee ishtarahu min misra limraatihi akrimee mathwahu ‘asa an yanfa‘ana aw nattakhithahu waladan wakathalika makkanna liyoosufa fee alardi walinu‘allimahu min taweeli alahadeethi wallahu ghalibun ‘ala amrihi walakinna akthara annasi la ya‘lamoon
12|22|Walamma balagha ashuddahu ataynahu hukman wa‘ilman wakathalika najzee almuhsineen
12|23|Warawadathu allatee huwa fee baytiha ‘an nafsihi waghallaqati alabwaba waqalat hayta laka qala ma‘atha Allahi innahu rabbee ahsana mathwaya innahu la yuflihu aththalimoon
12|24|Walaqad hammat bihi wahamma biha lawla an raa burhana rabbihi kathalika linasrifa ‘anhu assooa walfahshaa innahu min ‘ibadina almukhlaseen
12|25|Wastabaqa albaba waqaddat qameesahu min duburin waalfaya sayyidaha lada albabi qalat ma jazao man arada biahlika sooan illa an yusjana aw ‘athabun aleem
12|26|Qala hiya rawadatnee ‘an nafsee washahida shahidun min ahliha in kana qameesuhu qudda min qubulin fasadaqat wahuwa mina alkathibeen
12|27|Wain kana qameesuhu qudda min duburin fakathabat wahuwa mina assadiqeen
12|28|Falamma raa qameesahu qudda min duburin qala innahu min kaydikunna inna kaydakunna ‘atheem
12|29|Yoosufu a‘rid ‘an hatha wastaghfiree lithanbiki innaki kunti mina alkhatieen
12|30|Waqala niswatun fee almadeenati imraatu al‘azeezi turawidu fataha ‘an nafsihi qad shaghafaha hubban inna lanaraha fee dalalin mubeen
12|31|Falamma sami‘at bimakrihinna arsalat ilayhinna waa‘tadat lahunna muttakaan waatat kulla wahidatin minhunna sikkeenan waqalati okhruj ‘alayhinna falamma raaynahu akbarnahu waqatta‘na aydiyahunna waqulna hasha lillahi ma hatha basharan in hatha illa malakun kareem
12|32|Qalat fathalikunna allathee lumtunnanee feehi walaqad rawadtuhu ‘an nafsihi fast‘sama walain lam yaf‘al ma amuruhu layusjananna walayakoonan mina assaghireen
12|33|Qala rabbi assijnu ahabbu ilayya mimma yad‘oonanee ilayhi wailla tasrif ‘annee kaydahunna asbu ilayhinna waakun mina aljahileen
12|34|Fastajaba lahu rabbuhu fasarafa ‘anhu kaydahunna innahu huwa assamee‘u al‘aleem
12|35|Thumma bada lahum min ba‘di ma raawoo alayati layasjununnahu hatta heen

14|5|Walaqad arsalna moosa biayatina an akhrij qawmaka mina aththulumati ila annoori wathakkirhum biayyami Allahi inna fee thalika laayatin likulli sabbarin shakoor

14|10|Qalat rusuluhum afee Allahi shakkun fatiri assamawati walardi yad‘ookum liyaghfira lakum min thunoobikum wayuakhkhirakum ila ajalin musamman qaloo in antum illa basharun mithluna tureedoona an tasuddoona ‘amma kana ya‘budu abaona fatoona bisultanin mubeen
14|11|Qalat lahum rusuluhum in nahnu illa basharun mithlukum walakinna Allaha yamunnu ‘ala man yashao min ‘ibadihi wama kana lana an natiyakum bisultanin illa biithni Allahi wa‘ala Allahi falyatawakkali almuminoon
14|12|Wama lana alla natawakkala ‘ala Allahi waqad hadana subulana walanasbiranna ‘ala ma athaytumoona wa‘ala Allahi falyatawakkali almutawakkiloon

17|1|Subhana allathee asra bi‘abdihi laylan mina almasjidi alharami ila almasjidi alaqsa allathee barakna hawlahu linuriyahu min ayatina innahu huwa assamee‘u albaseer
