21|112|Qala rabbi ohkum bilhaqqi warabbuna arrahmanu almusta‘anu ‘ala ma tasifoon
22|1|Ya ayyuha annasu ittaqoo rabbakum inna zalzalata assa‘ati shayon ‘atheem
22|2|Yawma tarawnaha tathhalu kullu murdi‘atin ‘amma arda‘at watada‘u kullu thati hamlin hamlaha watara annasa sukara wama hum bisukara walakinna ‘athaba Allahi shadeed
22|3|Wamina annasi man yujadilu fee Allahi bighayri ‘ilmin wayattabi‘u kulla shaytanin mareed
22|4|Kutiba ‘alayhi annahu man tawallahu faannahu yudilluhu wayahdeehi ila ‘athabi assa‘eer
22|5|Ya ayyuha annasu in kuntum fee raybin mina alba‘thi fainna khalaqnakum min turabin thumma min nutfatin thumma min ‘alaqatin thumma min mudghatin mukhallaqatin waghayri mukhallaqatin linubayyina lakum wanuqirru fee alarhami ma nashao ila ajalin musamman thumma nukhrijukum tiflan thumma litablughoo ashuddakum waminkum man yutawaffa waminkum man yuraddu ila arthali al‘umuri likayla ya‘lama min ba‘di ‘ilmin shayan watara alarda hamidatan faitha anzalna ‘alayha almaa ihtazzat warabat waanbatat min kulli zawjin baheej
22|6|Thalika bianna Allaha huwa alhaqqu waannahu yuhyee almawta waannahu ‘ala kulli shayin qadeer
22|7|Waanna assa‘ata atiyatun la rayba feeha waanna Allaha yab‘athu man fee alquboor
22|8|Wamina annasi man yujadilu fee Allahi bighayri ‘ilmin wala hudan wala kitabin muneer
22|9|Thaniya ‘itfihi liyudilla ‘an sabeeli Allahi lahu fee addunya khizyun wanutheequhu yawma alqiyamati ‘athaba alhareeq
22|10|Thalika bima qaddamat yadaka waanna Allaha laysa bithallamin lil‘abeed
22|11|Wamina annasi man ya‘budu Allaha ‘ala harfin fain asabahu khayrun itmaanna bihi wain asabathu fitnatun inqalaba ‘ala wajhihi khasira addunya walakhirata thalika huwa alkhusranu almubeen
22|12|Yad‘oo min dooni Allahi ma la yadurruhu wama la yanfa‘uhu thalika huwa addalalu alba‘eed
22|13|Yad‘oo laman darruhu aqrabu min naf‘ihi labisa almawla walabisa al‘asheer
22|14|Inna Allaha yudkhilu allatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati jannatin tajree min tahtiha alanharu inna Allaha yaf‘alu ma yureed
22|15|Man kana yathunnu an lan yansurahu Allahu fee addunya walakhirati falyamdud bisababin ila assamai thumma liyaqta‘ falyanthur hal yuthhibanna kayduhu ma yagheeth
22|16|Wakathalika anzalnahu ayatin bayyinatin waanna Allaha yahdee man yureed
22|17|Inna allatheena amanoo wallatheena hadoo wassabieena wannasara walmajoosa wallatheena ashrakoo inna Allaha yafsilu baynahum yawma alqiyamati inna Allaha ‘ala kulli shayin shaheed
22|18|Alam tara anna Allaha yasjudu lahu man fee assamawati waman fee alardi washshamsu walqamaru wannujoomu waljibalu washshajaru waddawabbu wakatheerun mina annasi wakatheerun haqqa ‘alayhi al‘athabu waman yuhini Allahu fama lahu min mukrimin inna Allaha yaf‘alu ma yasha
22|19|Hathani khasmani ikhtasamoo fee rabbihim fallatheena kafaroo qutti‘at lahum thiyabun min narin yusabbu min fawqi ruoosihimu alhameem
22|20|Yusharu bihi ma fee butoonihim waljulood
22|21|Walahum maqami‘u min hadeed
22|22|Kullama aradoo an yakhrujoo minha min ghammin o‘eedoo feeha wathooqoo ‘athaba alhareeq
22|23|Inna Allaha yudkhilu allatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati jannatin tajree min tahtiha alanharu yuhallawna feeha min asawira min thahabin waluluan walibasuhum feeha hareer
22|24|Wahudoo ila attayyibi mina alqawli wahudoo ila sirati alhameed
22|25|Inna allatheena kafaroo wayasuddoona ‘an sabeeli Allahi walmasjidi alharami allathee ja‘alnahu linnasi sawaan al‘akifu feehi walbadi waman yurid feehi biilhadin bithulmin nuthiqhu min ‘athabin aleem
22|26|Waith bawwana liibraheema makana albayti an la tushrik bee shayan watahhir baytiya littaifeena walqaimeena warrukka‘i assujood
22|27|Waaththin fee annasi bilhajji yatooka rijalan wa‘ala kulli damirin yateena min kulli fajjin ‘ameeq
22|28|Liyashhadoo manafi‘a lahum wayathkuroo isma Allahi fee ayyamin ma‘loomatin ‘ala ma razaqahum min baheemati alan‘ami fakuloo minha waat‘imoo albaisa alfaqeer
22|29|Thumma lyaqdoo tafathahum walyoofoo nuthoorahum walyattawwafoo bilbayti al‘ateeq
22|30|Thalika waman yu‘aththim hurumati Allahi fahuwa khayrun lahu ‘inda rabbihi waohillat lakumu alan‘amu illa ma yutla ‘alaykum fajtaniboo arrijsa mina alawthani wajtaniboo qawla azzoor
22|31|Hunafaa lillahi ghayra mushrikeena bihi waman yushrik billahi fakaannama kharra mina assamai fatakhtafuhu attayru aw tahwee bihi arreehu fee makanin saheeq
22|32|Thalika waman yu‘aththim sha‘aira Allahi fainnaha min taqwa alquloob

22|34|Walikulli ommatin ja‘alna mansakan liyathkuroo isma Allahi ‘ala ma razaqahum min baheemati alan‘ami failahukum ilahun wahidun falahu aslimoo wabashshiri almukhbiteen
22|35|Allatheena itha thukira Allahu wajilat quloobuhum wassabireena ‘ala ma asabahum walmuqeemee assalati wamimma razaqnahum yunfiqoon
22|36|Walbudna ja‘alnaha lakum min sha‘airi Allahi lakum feeha khayrun fathkuroo isma Allahi ‘alayha sawaffa faitha wajabat junoobuha fakuloo minha waat‘imoo alqani‘a walmu‘tarra kathalika sakhkharnaha lakum la‘allakum tashkuroon
22|37|Lan yanala Allaha luhoomuha wala dimaoha walakin yanaluhu attaqwa minkum kathalika sakhkharaha lakum litukabbiroo Allaha ‘ala ma hadakum wabashshiri almuhsineen
22|38|Inna Allaha yudafi‘u ‘ani allatheena amanoo inna Allaha la yuhibbu kulla khawwanin kafoor

22|66|Wahuwa allathee ahyakum thumma yumeetukum thumma yuhyeekum inna alinsana lakafoor

23|24|Faqala almalao allatheena kafaroo min qawmihi ma hatha illa basharun mithlukum yureedu an yatafaddala ‘alaykum walaw shaa Allahu laanzala malaikatan ma sami‘na bihatha fee abaina alawwaleen

23|34|Walain ata‘tum basharan mithlakum innakum ithan lakhasiroon

27|60|Amman khalaqa assamawati walarda waanzala lakum mina assamai maan faanbatna bihi hadaiqa thata bahjatin ma kana lakum an tunbitoo shajaraha ailahun ma‘a Allahi bal hum qawmun ya‘diloon
27|61|Amman ja‘ala alarda qararan waja‘ala khilalaha anharan waja‘ala laha rawasiya waja‘ala bayna albahrayni hajizan ailahun ma‘a Allahi bal aktharuhum la ya‘lamoon

28|1|Taseenmeem
28|2|Tilka ayatu alkitabi almubeen

28|4|Inna fir‘awna ‘ala fee alardi waja‘ala ahlaha shiya‘an yastad‘ifu taifatan minhum yuthabbihu abnaahum wayastahyee nisaahum innahu kana mina almufsideen
28|5|Wanureedu an namunna ‘ala allatheena istud‘ifoo fee alardi wanaj‘alahum aimmatan wanaj‘alahumu alwaritheen
28|6|Wanumakkina lahum fee alardi wanuriya fir‘awna wahamana wajunoodahuma minhum ma kanoo yahtharoon
28|7|Waawhayna ila ommi moosa an ardi‘eehi faitha khifti ‘alayhi faalqeehi fee alyammi wala takhafee wala tahzanee inna raddoohu ilayki waja‘iloohu mina almursaleen
28|8|Faltaqatahu alu fir‘awna liyakoona lahum ‘aduwwan wahazanan inna fir‘awna wahamana wajunoodahuma kanoo khatieen

28|13|Faradadnahu ila ommihi kay taqarra ‘aynuha wala tahzana walita‘lama anna wa‘da Allahi haqqun walakinna aktharahum la ya‘lamoon
28|14|Walamma balagha ashuddahu wastawa ataynahu hukman wa‘ilman wakathalika najzee almuhsineen
28|15|Wadakhala almadeenata ‘ala heeni ghaflatin min ahliha fawajada feeha rajulayni yaqtatilani hatha min shee‘atihi wahatha min ‘aduwwihi fastaghathahu allathee min shee‘atihi ‘ala allathee min ‘aduwwihi fawakazahu moosa faqada ‘alayhi qala hatha min ‘amali ashshaytani innahu ‘aduwwun mudillun mubeen

28|19|Falamma an arada an yabtisha billathee huwa ‘aduwwun lahuma qala ya moosa atureedu an taqtulanee kama qatalta nafsan bilamsi in tureedu illa an takoona jabbaran fee alardi wama tureedu an takoona mina almusliheen

28|22|Walamma tawajjaha tilqaa madyana qala ‘asa rabbee an yahdiyanee sawaa assabeel
28|23|Walamma warada maa madyana wajada ‘alayhi ommatan mina annasi yasqoona wawajada min doonihimu imraatayni tathoodani qala ma khatbukuma qalata la nasqee hatta yusdira arri‘ao waaboona shaykhun kabeer

28|25|Fajaathu ihdahuma tamshee ‘ala istihyain qalat inna abee yad‘ooka liyajziyaka ajra ma saqayta lana falamma jaahu waqassa ‘alayhi alqasasa qala la takhaf najawta mina alqawmi aththalimeen

28|73|Wamin rahmatihi ja‘ala lakumu allayla wannahara litaskunoo feehi walitabtaghoo min fadlihi wala‘allakum tashkuroon
28|74|Wayawma yunadeehim fayaqoolu ayna shurakaiya allatheena kuntum taz‘umoon
28|75|Wanaza‘na min kulli ommatin shaheedan faqulna hatoo burhanakum fa‘alimoo anna alhaqqa lillahi wadalla ‘anhum ma kanoo yaftaroon
28|76|Inna qaroona kana min qawmi moosa fabagha ‘alayhim waataynahu mina alkunoozi ma inna mafatihahu latanooo bil‘usbati olee alquwwati ith qala lahu qawmuhu la tafrah inna Allaha la yuhibbu alfariheen
28|77|Wabtaghi feema ataka Allahu addara alakhirata wala tansa naseebaka mina addunya waahsin kama ahsana Allahu ilayka wala tabghi alfasada fee alardi inna Allaha la yuhibbu almufsideen
28|78|Qala innama ooteetuhu ‘ala ‘ilmin ‘indee awalam ya‘lam anna Allaha qad ahlaka min qablihi mina alqurooni man huwa ashaddu minhu quwwatan waaktharu jam‘an wala yusalu ‘an thunoobihimu almujrimoon
28|79|Fakharaja ‘ala qawmihi fee zeenatihi qala allatheena yureedoona alhayata addunya ya layta lana mithla ma ootiya qaroonu innahu lathoo haththin ‘atheem
28|80|Waqala allatheena ootoo al‘ilma waylakum thawabu Allahi khayrun liman amana wa‘amila salihan wala yulaqqaha illa assabiroon
28|81|Fakhasafna bihi wabidarihi alarda fama kana lahu min fiatin yansuroonahu min dooni Allahi wama kana mina almuntasireen

31|30|Thalika bianna Allaha huwa alhaqqu waanna ma yad‘oona min doonihi albatilu waanna Allaha huwa al‘aliyyu alkabeer
31|31|Alam tara anna alfulka tajree fee albahri bini‘mati Allahi liyuriyakum min ayatihi inna fee thalika laayatin likulli sabbarin shakoor
31|32|Waitha ghashiyahum mawjun kaththulali da‘awoo Allaha mukhliseena lahu addeena falamma najjahum ila albarri faminhum muqtasidun wama yajhadu biayatina illa kullu khattarin kafoor
31|33|Ya ayyuha annasu ittaqoo rabbakum wakhshaw yawman la yajzee walidun ‘an waladihi wala mawloodun huwa jazin ‘an walidihi shayan inna wa‘da Allahi haqqun fala taghurrannakumu alhayatu addunya wala yaghurrannakum billahi algharoor
31|34|Inna Allaha ‘indahu ‘ilmu assa‘ati wayunazzilu alghaytha waya‘lamu ma fee alarhami wama tadree nafsun matha taksibu ghadan wama tadree nafsun biayyi ardin tamootu inna Allaha ‘aleemun khabeer
32|1|Aliflammeem
32|2|Tanzeelu alkitabi la rayba feehi min rabbi al‘alameen
32|3|Am yaqooloona iftarahu bal huwa alhaqqu min rabbika litunthira qawman ma atahum min natheerin min qablika la‘allahum yahtadoon
32|4|Allahu allathee khalaqa assamawati walarda wama baynahuma fee sittati ayyamin thumma istawa ‘ala al‘arshi ma lakum min doonihi min waliyyin wala shafee‘in afala tatathakkaroon
32|5|Yudabbiru alamra mina assamai ila alardi thumma ya‘ruju ilayhi fee yawmin kana miqdaruhu alfa sanatin mimma ta‘uddoon
32|6|Thalika ‘alimu alghaybi washshahadati al‘azeezu arraheem
32|7|Allathee ahsana kulla shayin khalaqahu wabadaa khalqa alinsani min teen
32|8|Thumma ja‘ala naslahu min sulalatin min main maheen
32|9|Thumma sawwahu wanafakha feehi min roohihi waja‘ala lakumu assam‘a walabsara walafidata qaleelan ma tashkuroon
32|10|Waqaloo aitha dalalna fee alardi ainna lafee khalqin jadeedin bal hum biliqai rabbihim kafiroon
32|11|Qul yatawaffakum malaku almawti allathee wukkila bikum thumma ila rabbikum turja‘oon
32|12|Walaw tara ithi almujrimoona nakisoo ruoosihim ‘inda rabbihim rabbana absarna wasami‘na faarji‘na na‘mal salihan inna mooqinoon
32|13|Walaw shina laatayna kulla nafsin hudaha walakin haqqa alqawlu minnee laamlaanna jahannama mina aljinnati wannasi ajma‘een
32|14|Fathooqoo bima naseetum liqaa yawmikum hatha inna naseenakum wathooqoo ‘athaba alkhuldi bima kuntum ta‘maloon
32|15|Innama yuminu biayatina allatheena itha thukkiroo biha kharroo sujjadan wasabbahoo bihamdi rabbihim wahum la yastakbiroon
32|16|Tatajafa junoobuhum ‘ani almadaji‘i yad‘oona rabbahum khawfan watama‘an wamimma razaqnahum yunfiqoon
32|17|Fala ta‘lamu nafsun ma okhfiya lahum min qurrati a‘yunin jazaan bima kanoo ya‘maloon
32|18|Afaman kana muminan kaman kana fasiqan la yastawoon
32|19|Amma allatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati falahum jannatu almawa nuzulan bima kanoo ya‘maloon
32|20|Waamma allatheena fasaqoo famawahumu annaru kullama aradoo an yakhrujoo minha o‘eedoo feeha waqeela lahum thooqoo ‘athaba annari allathee kuntum bihi tukaththiboon
32|21|Walanutheeqannahum mina al‘athabi aladna doona al‘athabi alakbari la‘allahum yarji‘oon
32|22|Waman athlamu mimman thukkira biayati rabbihi thumma a‘rada ‘anha inna mina almujrimeena muntaqimoon
32|23|Walaqad atayna moosa alkitaba fala takun fee miryatin min liqaihi waja‘alnahu hudan libanee israeel
32|24|Waja‘alna minhum aimmatan yahdoona biamrina lamma sabaroo wakanoo biayatina yooqinoon
32|25|Inna rabbaka huwa yafsilu baynahum yawma alqiyamati feema kanoo feehi yakhtalifoon
32|26|Awalam yahdi lahum kam ahlakna min qablihim mina alqurooni yamshoona fee masakinihim inna fee thalika laayatin afala yasma‘oon
32|27|Awalam yaraw anna nasooqu almaa ila alardi aljuruzi fanukhriju bihi zar‘an takulu minhu an‘amuhum waanfusuhum afala yubsiroon
32|28|Wayaqooloona mata hatha alfathu in kuntum sadiqeen
32|29|Qul yawma alfathi la yanfa‘u allatheena kafaroo eemanuhum wala hum yuntharoon
32|30|Faa‘rid ‘anhum wantathir innahum muntathiroon
33|1|Ya ayyuha annabiyyu ittaqi Allaha wala tuti‘i alkafireena walmunafiqeena inna Allaha kana ‘aleeman hakeema
33|2|Wattabi‘ ma yooha ilayka min rabbika inna Allaha kana bima ta‘maloona khabeera
33|3|Watawakkal ‘ala Allahi wakafa billahi wakeela
33|4|Ma ja‘ala Allahu lirajulin min qalbayni fee jawfihi wama ja‘ala azwajakumu allaee tuthahiroona minhunna ommahatikum wama ja‘ala ad‘iyaakum abnaakum thalikum qawlukum biafwahikum wallahu yaqoolu alhaqqa wahuwa yahdee assabeel
33|5|Od‘oohum liabaihim huwa aqsatu ‘inda Allahi fain lam ta‘lamoo abaahum faikhwanukum fee addeeni wamawaleekum walaysa ‘alaykum junahun feema akhtatum bihi walakin ma ta‘ammadat quloobukum wakana Allahu ghafooran raheema
33|6|Annabiyyu awla bilmumineena min anfusihim waazwajuhu ommahatuhum waoloo alarhami ba‘duhum awla biba‘din fee kitabi Allahi mina almumineena walmuhajireena illa an taf‘aloo ila awliyaikum ma‘roofan kana thalika fee alkitabi mastoora
33|7|Waith akhathna mina annabiyyeena meethaqahum waminka wamin noohin waibraheema wamoosa wa‘eesa ibni maryama waakhathna minhum meethaqan ghaleetha
33|8|Liyasala assadiqeena ‘an sidqihim waa‘adda lilkafireena ‘athaban aleema
33|9|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo othkuroo ni‘mata Allahi ‘alaykum ith jaatkum junoodun faarsalna ‘alayhim reehan wajunoodan lam tarawha wakana Allahu bima ta‘maloona baseera
33|10|Ith jaookum min fawqikum wamin asfala minkum waith zaghati alabsaru wabalaghati alquloobu alhanajira watathunnoona billahi aththunoona
33|11|Hunalika ibtuliya almuminoona wazulziloo zilzalan shadeeda
33|12|Waith yaqoolu almunafiqoona wallatheena fee quloobihim maradun ma wa‘adana Allahu warasooluhu illa ghuroora
33|13|Waith qalat taifatun minhum ya ahla yathriba la muqama lakum farji‘oo wayastathinu fareequn minhumu annabiyya yaqooloona inna buyootana ‘awratun wama hiya bi‘awratin in yureedoona illa firara
33|14|Walaw dukhilat ‘alayhim min aqtariha thumma suiloo alfitnata laatawha wama talabbathoo biha illa yaseera
33|15|Walaqad kanoo ‘ahadoo Allaha min qablu la yuwalloona aladbara wakana ‘ahdu Allahi masoola
33|16|Qul lan yanfa‘akumu alfiraru in farartum mina almawti awi alqatli waithan la tumatta‘oona illa qaleela
33|17|Qul man tha allathee ya‘simukum mina Allahi in arada bikum sooan aw arada bikum rahmatan wala yajidoona lahum min dooni Allahi waliyyan wala naseera
33|18|Qad ya‘lamu Allahu almu‘awwiqeena minkum walqaileena liikhwanihim halumma ilayna wala yatoona albasa illa qaleela
33|19|Ashihhatan ‘alaykum faitha jaa alkhawfu raaytahum yanthuroona ilayka tadooru a‘yunuhum kallathee yughsha ‘alayhi mina almawti faitha thahaba alkhawfu salaqookum bialsinatin hidadin ashihhatan ‘ala alkhayri olaika lam yuminoo faahbata Allahu a‘malahum wakana thalika ‘ala Allahi yaseera
33|20|Yahsaboona alahzaba lam yathhaboo wain yati alahzabu yawaddoo law annahum badoona fee ala‘rabi yasaloona ‘an anbaikum walaw kanoo feekum ma qataloo illa qaleela
33|21|Laqad kana lakum fee rasooli Allahi oswatun hasanatun liman kana yarjoo Allaha walyawma alakhira wathakara Allaha katheera
33|22|Walamma raa almuminoona alahzaba qaloo hatha ma wa‘adana Allahu warasooluhu wasadaqa Allahu warasooluhu wama zadahum illa eemanan watasleema
33|23|Mina almumineena rijalun sadaqoo ma ‘ahadoo Allaha ‘alayhi faminhum man qada nahbahu waminhum man yantathiru wama baddaloo tabdeela
33|24|Liyajziya Allahu assadiqeena bisidqihim wayu‘aththiba almunafiqeena in shaa aw yatooba ‘alayhim inna Allaha kana ghafooran raheema
33|25|Waradda Allahu allatheena kafaroo bighaythihim lam yanaloo khayran wakafa Allahu almumineena alqitala wakana Allahu qawiyyan ‘azeeza
33|26|Waanzala allatheena thaharoohum min ahli alkitabi min sayaseehim waqathafa fee quloobihimu arru‘ba fareeqan taqtuloona watasiroona fareeqa
33|27|Waawrathakum ardahum wadiyarahum waamwalahum waardan lam tataooha wakana Allahu ‘ala kulli shayin qadeera
33|28|Ya ayyuha annabiyyu qul liazwajika in kuntunna turidna alhayata addunya wazeenataha fata‘alayna omatti‘kunna waosarrihkunna sarahan jameela
33|29|Wain kuntunna turidna Allaha warasoolahu waddara alakhirata fainna Allaha a‘adda lilmuhsinati minkunna ajran ‘atheema
33|30|Ya nisaa annabiyyi man yati minkunna bifahishatin mubayyinatin yuda‘af laha al‘athabu di‘fayni wakana thalika ‘ala Allahi yaseera
33|31|Waman yaqnut minkunna lillahi warasoolihi wata‘mal salihan nutiha ajraha marratayni waa‘tadna laha rizqan kareema
33|32|Ya nisaa annabiyyi lastunna kaahadin mina annisai ini ittaqaytunna fala takhda‘na bilqawli fayatma‘a allathee fee qalbihi maradun waqulna qawlan ma‘roofa
33|33|Waqarna fee buyootikunna wala tabarrajna tabarruja aljahiliyyati aloola waaqimna assalata waateena azzakata waati‘na Allaha warasoolahu innama yureedu Allahu liyuthhiba ‘ankumu arrijsa ahla albayti wayutahhirakum tatheera
33|34|Wathkurna ma yutla fee buyootikunna min ayati Allahi walhikmati inna Allaha kana lateefan khabeera
33|35|Inna almuslimeena walmuslimati walmumineena walmuminati walqaniteena walqanitati wassadiqeena wassadiqati wassabireena wassabirati walkhashi‘eena walkhashi‘ati walmutasaddiqeena walmutasaddiqati wassaimeena wassaimati walhafitheena furoojahum walhafithati waththakireena Allaha katheeran waththakirati a‘adda Allahu lahum maghfiratan waajran ‘atheema
33|36|Wama kana limuminin wala muminatin itha qada Allahu warasooluhu amran an yakoona lahumu alkhiyaratu min amrihim waman ya‘si Allaha warasoolahu faqad dalla dalalan mubeena
33|37|Waith taqoolu lillathee an‘ama Allahu ‘alayhi waan‘amta ‘alayhi amsik ‘alayka zawjaka wattaqi Allaha watukhfee fee nafsika ma Allahu mubdeehi watakhsha annasa wallahu ahaqqu an takhshahu falamma qada zaydun minha wataran zawwajnakaha likay la yakoona ‘ala almumineena harajun fee azwaji ad‘iyaihim itha qadaw minhunna wataran wakana amru Allahi maf‘oola
33|38|Ma kana ‘ala annabiyyi min harajin feema farada Allahu lahu sunnata Allahi fee allatheena khalaw min qablu wakana amru Allahi qadaran maqdoora

33|40|Ma kana muhammadun aba ahadin min rijalikum walakin rasoola Allahi wakhatama annabiyyeena wakana Allahu bikulli shayin ‘aleema
33|41|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo othkuroo Allaha thikran katheera
33|42|Wasabbihoohu bukratan waaseela

33|49|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo itha nakahtumu almuminati thumma tallaqtumoohunna min qabli an tamassoohunna fama lakum ‘alayhinna min ‘iddatin ta‘taddoonaha famatti‘oohunna wasarrihoohunna sarahan jameela
33|50|Ya ayyuha annabiyyu inna ahlalna laka azwajaka allatee atayta ojoorahunna wama malakat yameenuka mimma afaa Allahu ‘alayka wabanati ‘ammika wabanati ‘ammatika wabanati khalika wabanati khalatika allatee hajarna ma‘aka wamraatan muminatan in wahabat nafsaha linnabiyyi in arada annabiyyu an yastankihaha khalisatan laka min dooni almumineena qad ‘alimna ma faradna ‘alayhim fee azwajihim wama malakat aymanuhum likayla yakoona ‘alayka harajun wakana Allahu ghafooran raheema
33|51|Turjee man tashao minhunna watuwee ilayka man tashao wamani ibtaghayta mimman ‘azalta fala junaha ‘alayka thalika adna an taqarra a‘yunuhunna wala yahzanna wayardayna bima ataytahunna kulluhunna wallahu ya‘lamu ma fee quloobikum wakana Allahu ‘aleeman haleema
33|52|La yahillu laka annisao min ba‘du wala an tabaddala bihinna min azwajin walaw a‘jabaka husnuhunna illa ma malakat yameenuka wakana Allahu ‘ala kulli shayin raqeeba
33|53|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo la tadkhuloo buyoota annabiyyi illa an yuthana lakum ila ta‘amin ghayra nathireena inahu walakin itha du‘eetum fadkhuloo faitha ta‘imtum fantashiroo wala mustaniseena lihadeethin inna thalikum kana yuthee annabiyya fayastahyee minkum wallahu la yastahyee mina alhaqqi waitha saaltumoohunna mata‘an fasaloohunna min warai hijabin thalikum atharu liquloobikum waquloobihinna wama kana lakum an tuthoo rasoola Allahi wala an tankihoo azwajahu min ba‘dihi abadan inna thalikum kana ‘inda Allahi ‘atheema
33|54|In tubdoo shayan aw tukhfoohu fainna Allaha kana bikulli shayin ‘aleema
33|55|La junaha ‘alayhinna fee abaihinna wala abnaihinna wala ikhwanihinna wala abnai ikhwanihinna wala abnai akhawatihinna wala nisaihinna wala ma malakat aymanuhunna wattaqeena Allaha inna Allaha kana ‘ala kulli shayin shaheeda
33|56|Inna Allaha wamalaikatahu yusalloona ‘ala annabiyyi ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo salloo ‘alayhi wasallimoo tasleema
33|57|Inna allatheena yuthoona Allaha warasoolahu la‘anahumu Allahu fee addunya walakhirati waa‘adda lahum ‘athaban muheena
33|58|Wallatheena yuthoona almumineena walmuminati bighayri ma iktasaboo faqadi ihtamaloo buhtanan waithman mubeena
33|59|Ya ayyuha annabiyyu qul liazwajika wabanatika wanisai almumineena yudneena ‘alayhinna min jalabeebihinna thalika adna an yu‘rafna fala yuthayna wakana Allahu ghafooran raheema
33|60|Lain lam yantahi almunafiqoona wallatheena fee quloobihim maradun walmurjifoona fee almadeenati lanughriyannaka bihim thumma la yujawiroonaka feeha illa qaleela
33|61|Mal‘ooneena aynama thuqifoo okhithoo waquttiloo taqteela
33|62|Sunnata Allahi fee allatheena khalaw min qablu walan tajida lisunnati Allahi tabdeela
33|63|Yasaluka annasu ‘ani assa‘ati qul innama ‘ilmuha ‘inda Allahi wama yudreeka la‘alla assa‘ata takoonu qareeba
33|64|Inna Allaha la‘ana alkafireena waa‘adda lahum sa‘eera
33|65|Khalideena feeha abadan la yajidoona waliyyan wala naseera
33|66|Yawma tuqallabu wujoohuhum fee annari yaqooloona ya laytana ata‘na Allaha waata‘na arrasoola
33|67|Waqaloo rabbana inna ata‘na sadatana wakubaraana faadalloona assabeela
33|68|Rabbana atihim di‘fayni mina al‘athabi wal‘anhum la‘nan kabeera
33|69|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo la takoonoo kallatheena athaw moosa fabarraahu Allahu mimma qaloo wakana ‘inda Allahi wajeeha
33|70|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo ittaqoo Allaha waqooloo qawlan sadeeda
33|71|Yuslih lakum a‘malakum wayaghfir lakum thunoobakum waman yuti‘i Allaha warasoolahu faqad faza fawzan ‘atheema
33|72|Inna ‘aradna alamanata ‘ala assamawati walardi waljibali faabayna an yahmilnaha waashfaqna minha wahamalaha alinsanu innahu kana thalooman jahoola
33|73|Liyu‘aththiba Allahu almunafiqeena walmunafiqati walmushrikeena walmushrikati wayatooba Allahu ‘ala almumineena walmuminati wakana Allahu ghafooran raheema
34|1|Alhamdu lillahi allathee lahu ma fee assamawati wama fee alardi walahu alhamdu fee alakhirati wahuwa alhakeemu alkhabeer
34|2|Ya‘lamu ma yaliju fee alardi wama yakhruju minha wama yanzilu mina assamai wama ya‘ruju feeha wahuwa arraheemu alghafoor
34|3|Waqala allatheena kafaroo la tateena assa‘atu qul bala warabbee latatiyannakum ‘alimi alghaybi la ya‘zubu ‘anhu mithqalu tharratin fee assamawati wala fee alardi wala asgharu min thalika wala akbaru illa fee kitabin mubeen
34|4|Liyajziya allatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati olaika lahum maghfiratun warizqun kareem
34|5|Wallatheena sa‘aw fee ayatina mu‘ajizeena olaika lahum ‘athabun min rijzin aleem
34|6|Wayara allatheena ootoo al‘ilma allathee onzila ilayka min rabbika huwa alhaqqa wayahdee ila sirati al‘azeezi alhameed
34|7|Waqala allatheena kafaroo hal nadullukum ‘ala rajulin yunabbiokum itha muzziqtum kulla mumazzaqin innakum lafee khalqin jadeed
34|8|Aftara ‘ala Allahi kathiban am bihi jinnatun bali allatheena la yuminoona bilakhirati fee al‘athabi waddalali alba‘eed
34|9|Afalam yaraw ila ma bayna aydeehim wama khalfahum mina assamai walardi in nasha nakhsif bihimu alarda aw nusqit ‘alayhim kisafan mina assamai inna fee thalika laayatan likulli ‘abdin muneeb
34|10|Walaqad atayna dawooda minna fadlan ya jibalu awwibee ma‘ahu wattayra waalanna lahu alhadeed
34|11|Ani i‘mal sabighatin waqaddir fee assardi wa‘maloo salihan innee bima ta‘maloona baseer
34|12|Walisulaymana arreeha ghuduwwuha shahrun warawahuha shahrun waasalna lahu ‘ayna alqitri wamina aljinni man ya‘malu bayna yadayhi biithni rabbihi waman yazigh minhum ‘an amrina nuthiqhu min ‘athabi assa‘eer
34|13|Ya‘maloona lahu ma yashao min mahareeba watamatheela wajifanin kaljawabi waqudoorin rasiyatin i‘maloo ala dawooda shukran waqaleelun min ‘ibadiya ashshakoor
34|14|Falamma qadayna ‘alayhi almawta ma dallahum ‘ala mawtihi illa dabbatu alardi takulu minsaatahu falamma kharra tabayyanati aljinnu an law kanoo ya‘lamoona alghayba ma labithoo fee al‘athabi almuheen
34|15|Laqad kana lisabain fee maskanihim ayatun jannatani ‘an yameenin washimalin kuloo min rizqi rabbikum washkuroo lahu baldatun tayyibatun warabbun ghafoor
34|16|Faa‘radoo faarsalna ‘alayhim sayla al‘arimi wabaddalnahum bijannatayhim jannatayni thawatay okulin khamtin waathlin washayin min sidrin qaleel
34|17|Thalika jazaynahum bima kafaroo wahal nujazee illa alkafoor
34|18|Waja‘alna baynahum wabayna alqura allatee barakna feeha quran thahiratan waqaddarna feeha assayra seeroo feeha layaliya waayyaman amineen
34|19|Faqaloo rabbana ba‘id bayna asfarina wathalamoo anfusahum faja‘alnahum ahadeetha wamazzaqnahum kulla mumazzaqin inna fee thalika laayatin likulli sabbarin shakoor
34|20|Walaqad saddaqa ‘alayhim ibleesu thannahu fattaba‘oohu illa fareeqan mina almumineen
34|21|Wama kana lahu ‘alayhim min sultanin illa lina‘lama man yuminu bilakhirati mimman huwa minha fee shakkin warabbuka ‘ala kulli shayin hafeeth
34|22|Quli od‘oo allatheena za‘amtum min dooni Allahi la yamlikoona mithqala tharratin fee assamawati wala fee alardi wama lahum feehima min shirkin wama lahu minhum min thaheer
34|23|Wala tanfa‘u ashshafa‘atu ‘indahu illa liman athina lahu hatta itha fuzzi‘a ‘an quloobihim qaloo matha qala rabbukum qaloo alhaqqa wahuwa al‘aliyyu alkabeer
34|24|Qul man yarzuqukum mina assamawati walardi quli Allahu wainna aw iyyakum la‘ala hudan aw fee dalalin mubeen
34|25|Qul la tusaloona ‘amma ajramna wala nusalu ‘amma ta‘maloon
34|26|Qul yajma‘u baynana rabbuna thumma yaftahu baynana bilhaqqi wahuwa alfattahu al‘aleem
34|27|Qul arooniya allatheena alhaqtum bihi shurakaa kalla bal huwa Allahu al‘azeezu alhakeem
34|28|Wama arsalnaka illa kaffatan linnasi basheeran wanatheeran walakinna akthara annasi la ya‘lamoon
34|29|Wayaqooloona mata hatha alwa‘du in kuntum sadiqeen
34|30|Qul lakum mee‘adu yawmin la tastakhiroona ‘anhu sa‘atan wala tastaqdimoon
34|31|Waqala allatheena kafaroo lan numina bihatha alqurani wala billathee bayna yadayhi walaw tara ithi aththalimoona mawqoofoona ‘inda rabbihim yarji‘u ba‘duhum ila ba‘din alqawla yaqoolu allatheena istud‘ifoo lillatheena istakbaroo lawla antum lakunna mumineen
34|32|Qala allatheena istakbaroo lillatheena istud‘ifoo anahnu sadadnakum ‘ani alhuda ba‘da ith jaakum bal kuntum mujrimeen
34|33|Waqala allatheena istud‘ifoo lillatheena istakbaroo bal makru allayli wannahari ith tamuroonana an nakfura billahi wanaj‘ala lahu andadan waasarroo annadamata lamma raawoo al‘athaba waja‘alna alaghlala fee a‘naqi allatheena kafaroo hal yujzawna illa ma kanoo ya‘maloon
34|34|Wama arsalna fee qaryatin min natheerin illa qala mutrafooha inna bima orsiltum bihi kafiroon
34|35|Waqaloo nahnu aktharu amwalan waawladan wama nahnu bimu‘aththabeen
34|36|Qul inna rabbee yabsutu arrizqa liman yashao wayaqdiru walakinna akthara annasi la ya‘lamoon
34|37|Wama amwalukum wala awladukum billatee tuqarribukum ‘indana zulfa illa man amana wa‘amila salihan faolaika lahum jazao addi‘fi bima ‘amiloo wahum fee alghurufati aminoon
34|38|Wallatheena yas‘awna fee ayatina mu‘ajizeena olaika fee al‘athabi muhdaroon
34|39|Qul inna rabbee yabsutu arrizqa liman yashao min ‘ibadihi wayaqdiru lahu wama anfaqtum min shayin fahuwa yukhlifuhu wahuwa khayru arraziqeen
34|40|Wayawma yahshuruhum jamee‘an thumma yaqoolu lilmalaikati ahaolai iyyakum kanoo ya‘budoon
34|41|Qaloo subhanaka anta waliyyuna min doonihim bal kanoo ya‘budoona aljinna aktharuhum bihim muminoon
34|42|Falyawma la yamliku ba‘dukum liba‘din naf‘an wala darran wanaqoolu lillatheena thalamoo thooqoo ‘athaba annari allatee kuntum biha tukaththiboon
34|43|Waitha tutla ‘alayhim ayatuna bayyinatin qaloo ma hatha illa rajulun yureedu an yasuddakum ‘amma kana ya‘budu abaokum waqaloo ma hatha illa ifkun muftaran waqala allatheena kafaroo lilhaqqi lamma jaahum in hatha illa sihrun mubeen
34|44|Wama ataynahum min kutubin yadrusoonaha wama arsalna ilayhim qablaka min natheer
34|45|Wakaththaba allatheena min qablihim wama balaghoo mi‘shara ma ataynahum fakaththaboo rusulee fakayfa kana nakeer
34|46|Qul innama a‘ithukum biwahidatin an taqoomoo lillahi mathna wafurada thumma tatafakkaroo ma bisahibikum min jinnatin in huwa illa natheerun lakum bayna yaday ‘athabin shadeed
34|47|Qul ma saaltukum min ajrin fahuwa lakum in ajriya illa ‘ala Allahi wahuwa ‘ala kulli shayin shaheed
34|48|Qul inna rabbee yaqthifu bilhaqqi ‘allamu alghuyoob
34|49|Qul jaa alhaqqu wama yubdio albatilu wama yu‘eed
34|50|Qul in dalaltu fainnama adillu ‘ala nafsee waini ihtadaytu fabima yoohee ilayya rabbee innahu samee‘un qareeb
34|51|Walaw tara ith fazi‘oo fala fawta waokhithoo min makanin qareeb
34|52|Waqaloo amanna bihi waanna lahumu attanawushu min makanin ba‘eed

34|54|Waheela baynahum wabayna ma yashtahoona kama fu‘ila biashya‘ihim min qablu innahum kanoo fee shakkin mureeb
35|1|Alhamdu lillahi fatiri assamawati walardi ja‘ili almalaikati rusulan olee ajnihatin mathna wathulatha waruba‘a yazeedu fee alkhalqi ma yashao inna Allaha ‘ala kulli shayin qadeer
35|2|Ma yaftahi Allahu linnasi min rahmatin fala mumsika laha wama yumsik fala mursila lahu min ba‘dihi wahuwa al‘azeezu alhakeem
35|3|Ya ayyuha annasu othkuroo ni‘mata Allahi ‘alaykum hal min khaliqin ghayru Allahi yarzuqukum mina assamai walardi la ilaha illa huwa faanna tufakoon
35|4|Wain yukaththibooka faqad kuththibat rusulun min qablika waila Allahi turja‘u alomoor
35|5|Ya ayyuha annasu inna wa‘da Allahi haqqun fala taghurrannakumu alhayatu addunya wala yaghurrannakum billahi algharoor
35|6|Inna ashshaytana lakum ‘aduwwun fattakhithoohu ‘aduwwan innama yad‘oo hizbahu liyakoonoo min ashabi assa‘eer
35|7|Allatheena kafaroo lahum ‘athabun shadeedun wallatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati lahum maghfiratun waajrun kabeer
35|8|Afaman zuyyina lahu sooo ‘amalihi faraahu hasanan fainna Allaha yudillu man yashao wayahdee man yashao fala tathhab nafsuka ‘alayhim hasaratin inna Allaha ‘aleemun bima yasna‘oon
35|9|Wallahu allathee arsala arriyaha fatutheeru sahaban fasuqnahu ila baladin mayyitin faahyayna bihi alarda ba‘da mawtiha kathalika annushoor
35|10|Man kana yureedu al‘izzata falillahi al‘izzatu jamee‘an ilayhi yas‘adu alkalimu attayyibu wal‘amalu assalihu yarfa‘uhu wallatheena yamkuroona assayyiati lahum ‘athabun shadeedun wamakru olaika huwa yaboor
35|11|Wallahu khalaqakum min turabin thumma min nutfatin thumma ja‘alakum azwajan wama tahmilu min ontha wala tada‘u illa bi‘ilmihi wama yu‘ammaru min mu‘ammarin wala yunqasu min ‘umurihi illa fee kitabin inna thalika ‘ala Allahi yaseer
35|12|Wama yastawee albahrani hatha ‘athbun furatun saighun sharabuhu wahatha milhun ojajun wamin kullin takuloona lahman tariyyan watastakhrijoona hilyatan talbasoonaha watara alfulka feehi mawakhira litabtaghoo min fadlihi wala‘allakum tashkuroon
35|13|Yooliju allayla fee annahari wayooliju annahara fee allayli wasakhkhara ashshamsa walqamara kullun yajree liajalin musamman thalikumu Allahu rabbukum lahu almulku wallatheena tad‘oona min doonihi ma yamlikoona min qitmeer
35|14|In tad‘oohum la yasma‘oo du‘aakum walaw sami‘oo ma istajaboo lakum wayawma alqiyamati yakfuroona bishirkikum wala yunabbioka mithlu khabeer

39|1|Tanzeelu alkitabi mina Allahi al‘azeezi alhakeem

39|3|Ala lillahi addeenu alkhalisu wallatheena ittakhathoo min doonihi awliyaa ma na‘buduhum illa liyuqarriboona ila Allahi zulfa inna Allaha yahkumu baynahum fee ma hum feehi yakhtalifoona inna Allaha la yahdee man huwa kathibun kaffar

39|5|Khalaqa assamawati walarda bilhaqqi yukawwiru allayla ‘ala annahari wayukawwiru annahara ‘ala allayli wasakhkhara ashshamsa walqamara kullun yajree liajalin musamman ala huwa al‘azeezu alghaffar
39|6|Khalaqakum min nafsin wahidatin thumma ja‘ala minha zawjaha waanzala lakum mina alan‘ami thamaniyata azwajin yakhluqukum fee butooni ommahatikum khalqan min ba‘di khalqin fee thulumatin thalathin thalikumu Allahu rabbukum lahu almulku la ilaha illa huwa faanna tusrafoon
39|7|In takfuroo fainna Allaha ghaniyyun ‘ankum wala yarda li‘ibadihi alkufra wain tashkuroo yardahu lakum wala taziru waziratun wizra okhra thumma ila rabbikum marji‘ukum fayunabbiokum bima kuntum ta‘maloona innahu ‘aleemun bithati assudoor
39|8|Waitha massa alinsana durrun da‘a rabbahu muneeban ilayhi thumma itha khawwalahu ni‘matan minhu nasiya ma kana yad‘oo ilayhi min qablu waja‘ala lillahi andadan liyudilla ‘an sabeelihi qul tamatta‘ bikufrika qaleelan innaka min ashabi annar
39|9|Amman huwa qanitun anaa allayli sajidan waqaiman yahtharu alakhirata wayarjoo rahmata rabbihi qul hal yastawee allatheena ya‘lamoona wallatheena la ya‘lamoona innama yatathakkaru oloo alalbab
39|10|Qul ya ‘ibadi allatheena amanoo ittaqoo rabbakum lillatheena ahsanoo fee hathihi addunya hasanatun waardu Allahi wasi‘atun innama yuwaffa assabiroona ajrahum bighayri hisab

39|21|Alam tara anna Allaha anzala mina assamai maan fasalakahu yanabee‘a fee alardi thumma yukhriju bihi zar‘an mukhtalifan alwanuhu thumma yaheeju fatarahu musfarran thumma yaj‘aluhu hutaman inna fee thalika lathikra liolee alalbab

39|38|Walain saaltahum man khalaqa assamawati walarda layaqoolunna Allahu qul afaraaytum ma tad‘oona min dooni Allahi in aradaniya Allahu bidurrin hal hunna kashifatu durrihi aw aradanee birahmatin hal hunna mumsikatu rahmatihi qul hasbiya Allahu ‘alayhi yatawakkalu almutawakkiloon
39|39|Qul ya qawmi i‘maloo ‘ala makanatikum innee ‘amilun fasawfa ta‘lamoon

39|42|Allahu yatawaffa alanfusa heena mawtiha wallatee lam tamut fee manamiha fayumsiku allatee qada ‘alayha almawta wayursilu alokhra ila ajalin musamman inna fee thalika laayatin liqawmin yatafakkaroon
39|43|Ami ittakhathoo min dooni Allahi shufa‘aa qul awalaw kanoo la yamlikoona shayan wala ya‘qiloon
39|44|Qul lillahi ashshafa‘atu jamee‘an lahu mulku assamawati walardi thumma ilayhi turja‘oon
39|45|Waitha thukira Allahu wahdahu ishmaazzat quloobu allatheena la yuminoona bilakhirati waitha thukira allatheena min doonihi itha hum yastabshiroon
39|46|Quli allahumma fatira assamawati walardi ‘alima alghaybi washshahadati anta tahkumu bayna ‘ibadika fee ma kanoo feehi yakhtalifoon
39|47|Walaw anna lillatheena thalamoo ma fee alardi jamee‘an wamithlahu ma‘ahu laftadaw bihi min sooi al‘athabi yawma alqiyamati wabada lahum mina Allahi ma lam yakoonoo yahtasiboon
39|48|Wabada lahum sayyiatu ma kasaboo wahaqa bihim ma kanoo bihi yastahzioon
39|49|Faitha massa alinsana durrun da‘ana thumma itha khawwalnahu ni‘matan minna qala innama ooteetuhu ‘ala ‘ilmin bal hiya fitnatun walakinna aktharahum la ya‘lamoon
39|50|Qad qalaha allatheena min qablihim fama aghna ‘anhum ma kanoo yaksiboon
39|51|Faasabahum sayyiatu ma kasaboo wallatheena thalamoo min haolai sayuseebuhum sayyiatu ma kasaboo wama hum bimu‘jizeen
39|52|Awalam ya‘lamoo anna Allaha yabsutu arrizqa liman yashao wayaqdiru inna fee thalika laayatin liqawmin yuminoon
39|53|Qul ya ‘ibadiya allatheena asrafoo ‘ala anfusihim la taqnatoo min rahmati Allahi inna Allaha yaghfiru aththunooba jamee‘an innahu huwa alghafooru arraheem
39|54|Waaneeboo ila rabbikum waaslimoo lahu min qabli an yatiyakumu al‘athabu thumma la tunsaroon
39|55|Wattabi‘oo ahsana ma onzila ilaykum min rabbikum min qabli an yatiyakumu al‘athabu baghtatan waantum la tash‘uroon
39|56|An taqoola nafsun ya hasrata ‘ala ma farrattu fee janbi Allahi wain kuntu lamina assakhireen
39|57|Aw taqoola law anna Allaha hadanee lakuntu mina almuttaqeen

39|68|Wanufikha fee assoori fasa‘iqa man fee assamawati waman fee alardi illa man shaa Allahu thumma nufikha feehi okhra faitha hum qiyamun yanthuroon

39|71|Waseeqa allatheena kafaroo ila jahannama zumaran hatta itha jaooha futihat abwabuha waqala lahum khazanatuha alam yatikum rusulun minkum yatloona ‘alaykum ayati rabbikum wayunthiroonakum liqaa yawmikum hatha qaloo bala walakin haqqat kalimatu al‘athabi ‘ala alkafireen

40|1|Hameem
40|2|Tanzeelu alkitabi mina Allahi al‘azeezi al‘aleem
40|3|Ghafiri aththanbi waqabili attawbi shadeedi al‘iqabi thee attawli la ilaha illa huwa ilayhi almaseer
40|4|Ma yujadilu fee ayati Allahi illa allatheena kafaroo fala yaghrurka taqallubuhum fee albilad
40|5|Kaththabat qablahum qawmu noohin walahzabu min ba‘dihim wahammat kullu ommatin birasoolihim liyakhuthoohu wajadaloo bilbatili liyudhidoo bihi alhaqqa faakhathtuhum fakayfa kana ‘iqab
40|6|Wakathalika haqqat kalimatu rabbika ‘ala allatheena kafaroo annahum ashabu annar
40|7|Allatheena yahmiloona al‘arsha waman hawlahu yusabbihoona bihamdi rabbihim wayuminoona bihi wayastaghfiroona lillatheena amanoo rabbana wasi‘ta kulla shayin rahmatan wa‘ilman faghfir lillatheena taboo wattaba‘oo sabeelaka waqihim ‘athaba aljaheem
40|8|Rabbana waadkhilhum jannati ‘adnin allatee wa‘adtahum waman salaha min abaihim waazwajihim wathurriyyatihim innaka anta al‘azeezu alhakeem

40|23|Walaqad arsalna moosa biayatina wasultanin mubeen
40|24|Ila fir‘awna wahamana waqaroona faqaloo sahirun kaththab

40|28|Waqala rajulun muminun min ali fir‘awna yaktumu eemanahu ataqtuloona rajulan an yaqoola rabbiyya Allahu waqad jaakum bilbayyinati min rabbikum wain yaku kathiban fa‘alayhi kathibuhu wain yaku sadiqan yusibkum ba‘du allathee ya‘idukum inna Allaha la yahdee man huwa musrifun kaththab
40|29|Ya qawmi lakumu almulku alyawma thahireena fee alardi faman yansuruna min basi Allahi in jaana qala fir‘awnu ma oreekum illa ma ara wama ahdeekum illa sabeela arrashad

40|35|Allatheena yujadiloona fee ayati Allahi bighayri sultanin atahum kabura maqtan ‘inda Allahi wa‘inda allatheena amanoo kathalika yatba‘u Allahu ‘ala kulli qalbi mutakabbirin jabbar

40|38|Waqala allathee amana ya qawmi ittabi‘ooni ahdikum sabeela arrashad

42|5|Takadu assamawatu yatafattarna min fawqihinna walmalaikatu yusabbihoona bihamdi rabbihim wayastaghfiroona liman fee alardi ala inna Allaha huwa alghafooru arraheem
42|6|Wallatheena ittakhathoo min doonihi awliyaa Allahu hafeethun ‘alayhim wama anta ‘alayhim biwakeel
42|7|Wakathalika awhayna ilayka quranan ‘arabiyyan litunthira omma alqura waman hawlaha watunthira yawma aljam‘i la rayba feehi fareequn fee aljannati wafareequn fee assa‘eer
42|8|Walaw shaa Allahu laja‘alahum ommatan wahidatan walakin yudkhilu man yashao fee rahmatihi waththalimoona ma lahum min waliyyin wala naseer
42|9|Ami ittakhathoo min doonihi awliyaa fallahu huwa alwaliyyu wahuwa yuhyee almawta wahuwa ‘ala kulli shayin qadeer
42|10|Wama ikhtalaftum feehi min shayin fahukmuhu ila Allahi thalikumu Allahu rabbee ‘alayhi tawakkaltu wailayhi oneeb
42|11|Fatiru assamawati walardi ja‘ala lakum min anfusikum azwajan wamina alan‘ami azwajan yathraokum feehi laysa kamithlihi shayon wahuwa assamee‘u albaseer
42|12|Lahu maqaleedu assamawati walardi yabsutu arrizqa liman yashao wayaqdiru innahu bikulli shayin ‘aleem
42|13|Shara‘a lakum mina addeeni ma wassa bihi noohan wallathee awhayna ilayka wama wassayna bihi ibraheema wamoosa wa‘eesa an aqeemoo addeena wala tatafarraqoo feehi kabura ‘ala almushrikeena ma tad‘oohum ilayhi Allahu yajtabee ilayhi man yashao wayahdee ilayhi man yuneeb
42|14|Wama tafarraqoo illa min ba‘di ma jaahumu al‘ilmu baghyan baynahum walawla kalimatun sabaqat min rabbika ila ajalin musamman laqudiya baynahum wainna allatheena oorithoo alkitaba min ba‘dihim lafee shakkin minhu mureeb
42|15|Falithalika fad‘u wastaqim kama omirta wala tattabi‘ ahwaahum waqul amantu bima anzala Allahu min kitabin waomirtu lia‘dila baynakum Allahu rabbuna warabbukum lana a‘maluna walakum a‘malukum la hujjata baynana wabaynakumu Allahu yajma‘u baynana wailayhi almaseer
42|16|Wallatheena yuhajjoona fee Allahi min ba‘di ma istujeeba lahu hujjatuhum dahidatun ‘inda rabbihim wa‘alayhim ghadabun walahum ‘athabun shadeed
42|17|Allahu allathee anzala alkitaba bilhaqqi walmeezani wama yudreeka la‘alla assa‘ata qareeb
42|18|Yasta‘jilu biha allatheena la yuminoona biha wallatheena amanoo mushfiqoona minha waya‘lamoona annaha alhaqqu ala inna allatheena yumaroona fee assa‘ati lafee dalalin ba‘eed
42|19|Allahu lateefun bi‘ibadihi yarzuqu man yashao wahuwa alqawiyyu al‘azeez
42|20|Man kana yureedu hartha alakhirati nazid lahu fee harthihi waman kana yureedu hartha addunya nutihi minha wama lahu fee alakhirati min naseeb
42|21|Am lahum shurakao shara‘oo lahum mina addeeni ma lam yathan bihi Allahu walawla kalimatu alfasli laqudiya baynahum wainna aththalimeena lahum ‘athabun aleem
42|22|Tara aththalimeena mushfiqeena mimma kasaboo wahuwa waqi‘un bihim wallatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati fee rawdati aljannati lahum ma yashaoona ‘inda rabbihim thalika huwa alfadlu alkabeer
42|23|Thalika allathee yubashshiru Allahu ‘ibadahu allatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati qul la asalukum ‘alayhi ajran illa almawaddata fee alqurba waman yaqtarif hasanatan nazid lahu feeha husnan inna Allaha ghafoorun shakoor
42|24|Am yaqooloona iftara ‘ala Allahi kathiban fain yashai Allahu yakhtim ‘ala qalbika wayamhu Allahu albatila wayuhiqqu alhaqqa bikalimatihi innahu ‘aleemun bithati assudoor
42|25|Wahuwa allathee yaqbalu attawbata ‘an ‘ibadihi waya‘foo ‘ani assayyiati waya‘lamu ma taf‘aloon
42|26|Wayastajeebu allatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati wayazeeduhum min fadlihi walkafiroona lahum ‘athabun shadeed
42|27|Walaw basata Allahu arrizqa li‘ibadihi labaghaw fee alardi walakin yunazzilu biqadarin ma yashao innahu bi‘ibadihi khabeerun baseer
42|28|Wahuwa allathee yunazzilu alghaytha min ba‘di ma qanatoo wayanshuru rahmatahu wahuwa alwaliyyu alhameed
42|29|Wamin ayatihi khalqu assamawati walardi wama baththa feehima min dabbatin wahuwa ‘ala jam‘ihim itha yashao qadeer
42|30|Wama asabakum min museebatin fabima kasabat aydeekum waya‘foo ‘an katheer
42|31|Wama antum bimu‘jizeena fee alardi wama lakum min dooni Allahi min waliyyin wala naseer
42|32|Wamin ayatihi aljawari fee albahri kala‘lam
42|33|In yasha yuskini arreeha fayathlalna rawakida ‘ala thahrihi inna fee thalika laayatin likulli sabbarin shakoor
42|34|Aw yoobiqhunna bima kasaboo waya‘fu ‘an katheer
42|35|Waya‘lama allatheena yujadiloona fee ayatina ma lahum min mahees
42|36|Fama ooteetum min shayin famata‘u alhayati addunya wama ‘inda Allahi khayrun waabqa lillatheena amanoo wa‘ala rabbihim yatawakkaloon
42|37|Wallatheena yajtaniboona kabaira alithmi walfawahisha waitha ma ghadiboo hum yaghfiroon
42|38|Wallatheena istajaboo lirabbihim waaqamoo assalata waamruhum shoora baynahum wamimma razaqnahum yunfiqoon
42|39|Wallatheena itha asabahumu albaghyu hum yantasiroon
42|40|Wajazao sayyiatin sayyiatun mithluha faman ‘afa waaslaha faajruhu ‘ala Allahi innahu la yuhibbu aththalimeen
42|41|Walamani intasara ba‘da thulmihi faolaika ma ‘alayhim min sabeel
42|42|Innama assabeelu ‘ala allatheena yathlimoona annasa wayabghoona fee alardi bighayri alhaqqi olaika lahum ‘athabun aleem
42|43|Walaman sabara waghafara inna thalika lamin ‘azmi alomoor
42|44|Waman yudlili Allahu fama lahu min waliyyin min ba‘dihi watara aththalimeena lamma raawoo al‘athaba yaqooloona hal ila maraddin min sabeel
42|45|Watarahum yu‘radoona ‘alayha khashi‘eena mina aththulli yanthuroona min tarfin khafiyyin waqala allatheena amanoo inna alkhasireena allatheena khasiroo anfusahum waahleehim yawma alqiyamati ala inna aththalimeena fee ‘athabin muqeem
42|46|Wama kana lahum min awliyaa yansuroonahum min dooni Allahi waman yudlili Allahu fama lahu min sabeel
42|47|Istajeeboo lirabbikum min qabli an yatiya yawmun la maradda lahu mina Allahi ma lakum min maljain yawmaithin wama lakum min nakeer
42|48|Fain a‘radoo fama arsalnaka ‘alayhim hafeethan in ‘alayka illa albalaghu wainna itha athaqna alinsana minna rahmatan fariha biha wain tusibhum sayyiatun bima qaddamat aydeehim fainna alinsana kafoor
42|49|Lillahi mulku assamawati walardi yakhluqu ma yashao yahabu liman yashao inathan wayahabu liman yashao aththukoor
42|50|Aw yuzawwijuhum thukranan wainathan wayaj‘alu man yashao ‘aqeeman innahu ‘aleemun qadeer
42|51|Wama kana libasharin an yukallimahu Allahu illa wahyan aw min warai hijabin aw yursila rasoolan fayoohiya biithnihi ma yashao innahu ‘aliyyun hakeem
42|52|Wakathalika awhayna ilayka roohan min amrina ma kunta tadree ma alkitabu wala aleemanu walakin ja‘alnahu nooran nahdee bihi man nashao min ‘ibadina wainnaka latahdee ila siratin mustaqeem
42|53|Sirati Allahi allathee lahu ma fee assamawati wama fee alardi ala ila Allahi taseeru alomoor

43|2|Walkitabi almubeen

43|13|Litastawoo ‘ala thuhoorihi thumma tathkuroo ni‘mata rabbikum itha istawaytum ‘alayhi wataqooloo subhana allathee sakhkhara lana hatha wama kunna lahu muqrineen
43|14|Wainna ila rabbina lamunqaliboon

43|17|Waitha bushshira ahaduhum bima daraba lirrahmani mathalan thalla wajhuhu muswaddan wahuwa katheem
43|18|Awaman yunashshao fee alhilyati wahuwa fee alkhisami ghayru mubeen

43|25|Fantaqamna minhum fanthur kayfa kana ‘aqibatu almukaththibeen

43|30|Walamma jaahumu alhaqqu qaloo hatha sihrun wainna bihi kafiroon
43|31|Waqaloo lawla nuzzila hatha alquranu ‘ala rajulin mina alqaryatayni ‘atheem
43|32|Ahum yaqsimoona rahmata rabbika nahnu qasamna baynahum ma‘eeshatahum fee alhayati addunya warafa‘na ba‘dahum fawqa ba‘din darajatin liyattakhitha ba‘duhum ba‘dan sukhriyyan warahmatu rabbika khayrun mimma yajma‘oon
43|33|Walawla an yakoona annasu ommatan wahidatan laja‘alna liman yakfuru birrahmani libuyootihim suqufan min fiddatin wama‘arija ‘alayha yathharoon
43|34|Walibuyootihim abwaban wasururan ‘alayha yattakioon

45|1|Hameem
45|2|Tanzeelu alkitabi mina Allahi al‘azeezi alhakeem
45|3|Inna fee assamawati walardi laayatin lilmumineen

45|5|Wakhtilafi allayli wannahari wama anzala Allahu mina assamai min rizqin faahya bihi alarda ba‘da mawtiha watasreefi arriyahi ayatun liqawmin ya‘qiloon
45|6|Tilka ayatu Allahi natlooha ‘alayka bilhaqqi fabiayyi hadeethin ba‘da Allahi waayatihi yuminoon
45|7|Waylun likulli affakin atheem
45|8|Yasma‘u ayati Allahi tutla ‘alayhi thumma yusirru mustakbiran kaan lam yasma‘ha fabashshirhu bi‘athabin aleem
45|9|Waitha ‘alima min ayatina shayan ittakhathaha huzuwan olaika lahum ‘athabun muheen
45|10|Min waraihim jahannamu wala yughnee ‘anhum ma kasaboo shayan wala ma ittakhathoo min dooni Allahi awliyaa walahum ‘athabun ‘atheem
45|11|Hatha hudan wallatheena kafaroo biayati rabbihim lahum ‘athabun min rijzin aleem
45|12|Allahu allathee sakhkhara lakumu albahra litajriya alfulku feehi biamrihi walitabtaghoo min fadlihi wala‘allakum tashkuroon
45|13|Wasakhkhara lakum ma fee assamawati wama fee alardi jamee‘an minhu inna fee thalika laayatin liqawmin yatafakkaroon
45|14|Qul lillatheena amanoo yaghfiroo lillatheena la yarjoona ayyama Allahi liyajziya qawman bima kanoo yaksiboon
45|15|Man ‘amila salihan falinafsihi waman asaa fa‘alayha thumma ila rabbikum turja‘oon
45|16|Walaqad atayna banee israeela alkitaba walhukma wannubuwwata warazaqnahum mina attayyibati wafaddalnahum ‘ala al‘alameen
45|17|Waataynahum bayyinatin mina alamri fama ikhtalafoo illa min ba‘di ma jaahumu al‘ilmu baghyan baynahum inna rabbaka yaqdee baynahum yawma alqiyamati feema kanoo feehi yakhtalifoon
45|18|Thumma ja‘alnaka ‘ala sharee‘atin mina alamri fattabi‘ha wala tattabi‘ ahwaa allatheena la ya‘lamoon
45|19|Innahum lan yughnoo ‘anka mina Allahi shayan wainna aththalimeena ba‘duhum awliyao ba‘din wallahu waliyyu almuttaqeen
45|20|Hatha basairu linnasi wahudan warahmatun liqawmin yooqinoon
45|21|Am hasiba allatheena ijtarahoo assayyiati an naj‘alahum kallatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati sawaan mahyahum wamamatuhum saa ma yahkumoon
45|22|Wakhalaqa Allahu assamawati walarda bilhaqqi walitujza kullu nafsin bima kasabat wahum la yuthlamoon
45|23|Afaraayta mani ittakhatha ilahahu hawahu waadallahu Allahu ‘ala ‘ilmin wakhatama ‘ala sam‘ihi waqalbihi waja‘ala ‘ala basarihi ghishawatan faman yahdeehi min ba‘di Allahi afala tathakkaroon
45|24|Waqaloo ma hiya illa hayatuna addunya namootu wanahya wama yuhlikuna illa addahru wama lahum bithalika min ‘ilmin in hum illa yathunnoon
45|25|Waitha tutla ‘alayhim ayatuna bayyinatin ma kana hujjatahum illa an qaloo itoo biabaina in kuntum sadiqeen
45|26|Quli Allahu yuhyeekum thumma yumeetukum thumma yajma‘ukum ila yawmi alqiyamati la rayba feehi walakinna akthara annasi la ya‘lamoon
45|27|Walillahi mulku assamawati walardi wayawma taqoomu assa‘atu yawmaithin yakhsaru almubtiloon
45|28|Watara kulla ommatin jathiyatan kullu ommatin tud‘a ila kitabiha alyawma tujzawna ma kuntum ta‘maloon
45|29|Hatha kitabuna yantiqu ‘alaykum bilhaqqi inna kunna nastansikhu ma kuntum ta‘maloon
45|30|Faamma allatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati fayudkhiluhum rabbuhum fee rahmatihi thalika huwa alfawzu almubeen
45|31|Waamma allatheena kafaroo afalam takun ayatee tutla ‘alaykum fastakbartum wakuntum qawman mujrimeen
45|32|Waitha qeela inna wa‘da Allahi haqqun wassa‘atu la rayba feeha qultum ma nadree ma assa‘atu in nathunnu illa thannan wama nahnu bimustayqineen
45|33|Wabada lahum sayyiatu ma ‘amiloo wahaqa bihim ma kanoo bihi yastahzioon
45|34|Waqeela alyawma nansakum kama naseetum liqaa yawmikum hatha wamawakumu annaru wama lakum min nasireen
45|35|Thalikum biannakumu ittakhathtum ayati Allahi huzuwan wagharratkumu alhayatu addunya falyawma la yukhrajoona minha wala hum yusta‘taboon
45|36|Falillahi alhamdu rabbi assamawati warabbi alardi rabbi al‘alameen
45|37|Walahu alkibriyao fee assamawati walardi wahuwa al‘azeezu alhakeem
46|1|Hameem
46|2|Tanzeelu alkitabi mina Allahi al‘azeezi alhakeem
46|3|Ma khalaqna assamawati walarda wama baynahuma illa bilhaqqi waajalin musamman wallatheena kafaroo ‘amma onthiroo mu‘ridoon
46|4|Qul araaytum ma tad‘oona min dooni Allahi aroonee matha khalaqoo mina alardi am lahum shirkun fee assamawati eetoonee bikitabin min qabli hatha aw atharatin min ‘ilmin in kuntum sadiqeen
46|5|Waman adallu mimman yad‘oo min dooni Allahi man la yastajeebu lahu ila yawmi alqiyamati wahum ‘an du‘aihim ghafiloon
46|6|Waitha hushira annasu kanoo lahum a‘daan wakanoo bi‘ibadatihim kafireen
46|7|Waitha tutla ‘alayhim ayatuna bayyinatin qala allatheena kafaroo lilhaqqi lamma jaahum hatha sihrun mubeen
46|8|Am yaqooloona iftarahu qul ini iftaraytuhu fala tamlikoona lee mina Allahi shayan huwa a‘lamu bima tufeedoona feehi kafa bihi shaheedan baynee wabaynakum wahuwa alghafooru arraheem
46|9|Qul ma kuntu bid‘an mina arrusuli wama adree ma yuf‘alu bee wala bikum in attabi‘u illa ma yooha ilayya wama ana illa natheerun mubeen
46|10|Qul araaytum in kana min ‘indi Allahi wakafartum bihi washahida shahidun min banee israeela ‘ala mithlihi faamana wastakbartum inna Allaha la yahdee alqawma aththalimeen
46|11|Waqala allatheena kafaroo lillatheena amanoo law kana khayran ma sabaqoona ilayhi waith lam yahtadoo bihi fasayaqooloona hatha ifkun qadeem
46|12|Wamin qablihi kitabu moosa imaman warahmatan wahatha kitabun musaddiqun lisanan ‘arabiyyan liyunthira allatheena thalamoo wabushra lilmuhsineen
46|13|Inna allatheena qaloo rabbuna Allahu thumma istaqamoo fala khawfun ‘alayhim wala hum yahzanoon
46|14|Olaika ashabu aljannati khalideena feeha jazaan bima kanoo ya‘maloon
46|15|Wawassayna alinsana biwalidayhi ihsanan hamalathu ommuhu kurhan wawada‘athu kurhan wahamluhu wafisaluhu thalathoona shahran hatta itha balagha ashuddahu wabalagha arba‘eena sanatan qala rabbi awzi‘nee an ashkura ni‘mataka allatee an‘amta ‘alayya wa‘ala walidayya waan a‘mala salihan tardahu waaslih lee fee thurriyyatee innee tubtu ilayka wainnee mina almuslimeen
46|16|Olaika allatheena nataqabbalu ‘anhum ahsana ma ‘amiloo wanatajawazu ‘an sayyiatihim fee ashabi aljannati wa‘da assidqi allathee kanoo yoo‘adoon
46|17|Wallathee qala liwalidayhi offin lakuma ata‘idaninee an okhraja waqad khalati alquroonu min qablee wahuma yastagheethani Allaha waylaka amin inna wa‘da Allahi haqqun fayaqoolu ma hatha illa asateeru alawwaleen
46|18|Olaika allatheena haqqa ‘alayhimu alqawlu fee umamin qad khalat min qablihim mina aljinni walinsi innahum kanoo khasireen
46|19|Walikullin darajatun mimma ‘amiloo waliyuwaffiyahum a‘malahum wahum la yuthlamoon
46|20|Wayawma yu‘radu allatheena kafaroo ‘ala annari athhabtum tayyibatikum fee hayatikumu addunya wastamta‘tum biha falyawma tujzawna ‘athaba alhooni bima kuntum tastakbiroona fee alardi bighayri alhaqqi wabima kuntum tafsuqoon
46|21|Wathkur akha ‘adin ith anthara qawmahu bilahqafi waqad khalati annuthuru min bayni yadayhi wamin khalfihi alla ta‘budoo illa Allaha innee akhafu ‘alaykum ‘athaba yawmin ‘atheem
46|22|Qaloo ajitana litafikana ‘an alihatina fatina bima ta‘iduna in kunta mina assadiqeen
46|23|Qala innama al‘ilmu ‘inda Allahi waoballighukum ma orsiltu bihi walakinnee arakum qawman tajhaloon
46|24|Falamma raawhu ‘aridan mustaqbila awdiyatihim qaloo hatha ‘aridun mumtiruna bal huwa ma ista‘jaltum bihi reehun feeha ‘athabun aleem
46|25|Tudammiru kulla shayin biamri rabbiha faasbahoo la yura illa masakinuhum kathalika najzee alqawma almujrimeen
46|26|Walaqad makkannahum feema in makkannakum feehi waja‘alna lahum sam‘an waabsaran waafidatan fama aghna ‘anhum sam‘uhum wala absaruhum wala afidatuhum min shayin ith kanoo yajhadoona biayati Allahi wahaqa bihim ma kanoo bihi yastahzioon
46|27|Walaqad ahlakna ma hawlakum mina alqura wasarrafna alayati la‘allahum yarji‘oon
46|28|Falawla nasarahumu allatheena ittakhathoo min dooni Allahi qurbanan alihatan bal dalloo ‘anhum wathalika ifkuhum wama kanoo yaftaroon
46|29|Waith sarafna ilayka nafaran mina aljinni yastami‘oona alqurana falamma hadaroohu qaloo ansitoo falamma qudiya wallaw ila qawmihim munthireen
46|30|Qaloo ya qawmana inna sami‘na kitaban onzila min ba‘di moosa musaddiqan lima bayna yadayhi yahdee ila alhaqqi waila tareeqin mustaqeem
46|31|Ya qawmana ajeeboo da‘iya Allahi waaminoo bihi yaghfir lakum min thunoobikum wayujirkum min ‘athabin aleem
46|32|Waman la yujib da‘iya Allahi falaysa bimu‘jizin fee alardi walaysa lahu min doonihi awliyaa olaika fee dalalin mubeen
46|33|Awalam yaraw anna Allaha allathee khalaqa assamawati walarda walam ya‘ya bikhalqihinna biqadirin ‘ala an yuhyiya almawta bala innahu ‘ala kulli shayin qadeer
46|34|Wayawma yu‘radu allatheena kafaroo ‘ala annari alaysa hatha bilhaqqi qaloo bala warabbina qala fathooqoo al‘athaba bima kuntum takfuroon
46|35|Fasbir kama sabara oloo al‘azmi mina arrusuli wala tasta‘jil lahum kaannahum yawma yarawna ma yoo‘adoona lam yalbathoo illa sa‘atan min naharin balaghun fahal yuhlaku illa alqawmu alfasiqoon
47|1|Allatheena kafaroo wasaddoo ‘an sabeeli Allahi adalla a‘malahum
47|2|Wallatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati waamanoo bima nuzzila ‘ala muhammadin wahuwa alhaqqu min rabbihim kaffara ‘anhum sayyiatihim waaslaha balahum
47|3|Thalika bianna allatheena kafaroo ittaba‘oo albatila waanna allatheena amanoo ittaba‘oo alhaqqa min rabbihim kathalika yadribu Allahu linnasi amthalahum
47|4|Faitha laqeetumu allatheena kafaroo fadarba arriqabi hatta itha athkhantumoohum fashuddoo alwathaqa faimma mannan ba‘du waimma fidaan hatta tada‘a alharbu awzaraha thalika walaw yashao Allahu lantasara minhum walakin liyabluwa ba‘dakum biba‘din wallatheena qutiloo fee sabeeli Allahi falan yudilla a‘malahum
47|5|Sayahdeehim wayuslihu balahum
47|6|Wayudkhiluhumu aljannata ‘arrafaha lahum
47|7|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo in tansuroo Allaha yansurkum wayuthabbit aqdamakum
47|8|Wallatheena kafaroo fata‘san lahum waadalla a‘malahum
47|9|Thalika biannahum karihoo ma anzala Allahu faahbata a‘malahum
47|10|Afalam yaseeroo fee alardi fayanthuroo kayfa kana ‘aqibatu allatheena min qablihim dammara Allahu ‘alayhim walilkafireena amthaluha
47|11|Thalika bianna Allaha mawla allatheena amanoo waanna alkafireena la mawla lahum
47|12|Inna Allaha yudkhilu allatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati jannatin tajree min tahtiha alanharu wallatheena kafaroo yatamatta‘oona wayakuloona kama takulu alan‘amu wannaru mathwan lahum
47|13|Wakaayyin min qaryatin hiya ashaddu quwwatan min qaryatika allatee akhrajatka ahlaknahum fala nasira lahum
47|14|Afaman kana ‘ala bayyinatin min rabbihi kaman zuyyina lahu sooo ‘amalihi wattaba‘oo ahwaahum
47|15|Mathalu aljannati allatee wu‘ida almuttaqoona feeha anharun min main ghayri asinin waanharun min labanin lam yataghayyar ta‘muhu waanharun min khamrin laththatin lishsharibeena waanharun min ‘asalin musaffan walahum feeha min kulli aththamarati wamaghfiratun min rabbihim kaman huwa khalidun fee annari wasuqoo maan hameeman faqatta‘a am‘aahum
47|16|Waminhum man yastami‘u ilayka hatta itha kharajoo min ‘indika qaloo lillatheena ootoo al‘ilma matha qala anifan olaika allatheena taba‘a Allahu ‘ala quloobihim wattaba‘oo ahwaahum
47|17|Wallatheena ihtadaw zadahum hudan waatahum taqwahum
47|18|Fahal yanthuroona illa assa‘ata an tatiyahum baghtatan faqad jaa ashratuha faanna lahum itha jaathum thikrahum
47|19|Fa‘lam annahu la ilaha illa Allahu wastaghfir lithanbika walilmumineena walmuminati wallahu ya‘lamu mutaqallabakum wamathwakum
47|20|Wayaqoolu allatheena amanoo lawla nuzzilat sooratun faitha onzilat sooratun muhkamatun wathukira feeha alqitalu raayta allatheena fee quloobihim maradun yanthuroona ilayka nathara almaghshiyyi ‘alayhi mina almawti faawla lahum
47|21|Ta‘atun waqawlun ma‘roofun faitha ‘azama alamru falaw sadaqoo Allaha lakana khayran lahum
47|22|Fahal ‘asaytum in tawallaytum an tufsidoo fee alardi watuqatti‘oo arhamakum
47|23|Olaika allatheena la‘anahumu Allahu faasammahum waa‘ma absarahum
47|24|Afala yatadabbaroona alqurana am ‘ala quloobin aqfaluha

47|26|Thalika biannahum qaloo lillatheena karihoo ma nazzala Allahu sanutee‘ukum fee ba‘di alamri wallahu ya‘lamu israrahum
47|27|Fakayfa itha tawaffathumu almalaikatu yadriboona wujoohahum waadbarahum
47|28|Thalika biannahumu ittaba‘oo ma askhata Allaha wakarihoo ridwanahu faahbata a‘malahum
47|29|Am hasiba allatheena fee quloobihim maradun an lan yukhrija Allahu adghanahum
47|30|Walaw nashao laaraynakahum fala‘araftahum biseemahum walata‘rifannahum fee lahni alqawli wallahu ya‘lamu a‘malakum
47|31|Walanabluwannakum hatta na‘lama almujahideena minkum wassabireena wanabluwa akhbarakum
47|32|Inna allatheena kafaroo wasaddoo ‘an sabeeli Allahi washaqqoo arrasoola min ba‘di ma tabayyana lahumu alhuda lan yaduroo Allaha shayan wasayuhbitu a‘malahum
47|33|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo atee‘oo Allaha waatee‘oo arrasoola wala tubtiloo a‘malakum
47|34|Inna allatheena kafaroo wasaddoo ‘an sabeeli Allahi thumma matoo wahum kuffarun falan yaghfira Allahu lahum
47|35|Fala tahinoo watad‘oo ila assalmi waantumu ala‘lawna wallahu ma‘akum walan yatirakum a‘malakum
47|36|Innama alhayatu addunya la‘ibun walahwun wain tuminoo watattaqoo yutikum ojoorakum wala yasalkum amwalakum
47|37|In yasalkumooha fayuhfikum tabkhaloo wayukhrij adghanakum
47|38|Haantum haolai tud‘awna litunfiqoo fee sabeeli Allahi faminkum man yabkhalu waman yabkhal fainnama yabkhalu ‘an nafsihi wallahu alghaniyyu waantumu alfuqarao wain tatawallaw yastabdil qawman ghayrakum thumma la yakoonoo amthalakum
48|1|Inna fatahna laka fathan mubeena
48|2|Liyaghfira laka Allahu ma taqaddama min thanbika wama taakhkhara wayutimma ni‘matahu ‘alayka wayahdiyaka siratan mustaqeema
48|3|Wayansuraka Allahu nasran ‘azeeza
48|4|Huwa allathee anzala assakeenata fee quloobi almumineena liyazdadoo eemanan ma‘a eemanihim walillahi junoodu assamawati walardi wakana Allahu ‘aleeman hakeema
48|5|Liyudkhila almumineena walmuminati jannatin tajree min tahtiha alanharu khalideena feeha wayukaffira ‘anhum sayyiatihim wakana thalika ‘inda Allahi fawzan ‘atheema
48|6|Wayu‘aththiba almunafiqeena walmunafiqati walmushrikeena walmushrikati aththanneena billahi thanna assawi ‘alayhim dairatu assawi waghadiba Allahu ‘alayhim wala‘anahum waa‘adda lahum jahannama wasaat maseera
48|7|Walillahi junoodu assamawati walardi wakana Allahu ‘azeezan hakeema
48|8|Inna arsalnaka shahidan wamubashshiran wanatheera
48|9|Lituminoo billahi warasoolihi watu‘azziroohu watuwaqqiroohu watusabbihoohu bukratan waaseela
48|10|Inna allatheena yubayi‘oonaka innama yubayi‘oona Allaha yadu Allahi fawqa aydeehim faman nakatha fainnama yankuthu ‘ala nafsihi waman awfa bima ‘ahada ‘alayhu Allaha fasayuteehi ajran ‘atheema
48|11|Sayaqoolu laka almukhallafoona mina ala‘rabi shaghalatna amwaluna waahloona fastaghfir lana yaqooloona bialsinatihim ma laysa fee quloobihim qul faman yamliku lakum mina Allahi shayan in arada bikum darran aw arada bikum naf‘an bal kana Allahu bima ta‘maloona khabeera
48|12|Bal thanantum an lan yanqaliba arrasoolu walmuminoona ila ahleehim abadan wazuyyina thalika fee quloobikum wathanantum thanna assawi wakuntum qawman boora
48|13|Waman lam yumin billahi warasoolihi fainna a‘tadna lilkafireena sa‘eera
48|14|Walillahi mulku assamawati walardi yaghfiru liman yashao wayu‘aththibu man yashao wakana Allahu ghafooran raheema
48|15|Sayaqoolu almukhallafoona itha intalaqtum ila maghanima litakhuthooha tharoona nattabi‘kum yureedoona an yubaddiloo kalama Allahi qul lan tattabi‘oona kathalikum qala Allahu min qablu fasayaqooloona bal tahsudoonana bal kanoo la yafqahoona illa qaleela
48|16|Qul lilmukhallafeena mina ala‘rabi satud‘awna ila qawmin olee basin shadeedin tuqatiloonahum aw yuslimoona fain tutee‘oo yutikumu Allahu ajran hasanan wain tatawallaw kama tawallaytum min qablu yu‘aththibkum ‘athaban aleema
48|17|Laysa ‘ala ala‘ma harajun wala ‘ala ala‘raji harajun wala ‘ala almareedi harajun waman yuti‘i Allaha warasoolahu yudkhilhu jannatin tajree min tahtiha alanharu waman yatawalla yu‘aththibhu ‘athaban aleema
48|18|Laqad radiya Allahu ‘ani almumineena ith yubayi‘oonaka tahta ashshajarati fa‘alima ma fee quloobihim faanzala asakeenata ‘alayhim waathabahum fathan qareeba
48|19|Wamaghanima katheeratan yakhuthoonaha wakana Allahu ‘azeezan hakeema
48|20|Wa‘adakumu Allahu maghanima katheeratan takhuthoonaha fa‘ajjala lakum hathihi wakaffa aydiya annasi ‘ankum walitakoona ayatan lilmumineena wayahdiyakum siratan mustaqeema
48|21|Waokhra lam taqdiroo ‘alayha qad ahata Allahu biha wakana Allahu ‘ala kulli shayin qadeera
48|22|Walaw qatalakumu allatheena kafaroo lawallawoo aladbara thumma la yajidoona waliyyan wala naseera
48|23|Sunnata Allahi allatee qad khalat min qablu walan tajida lisunnati Allahi tabdeela
48|24|Wahuwa allathee kaffa aydiyahum ‘ankum waaydiyakum ‘anhum bibatni makkata min ba‘di an athfarakum ‘alayhim wakana Allahu bima ta‘maloona baseera
48|25|Humu allatheena kafaroo wasaddookum ‘ani almasjidi alharami walhadya ma‘koofan an yablugha mahillahu walawla rijalun muminoona wanisaon muminatun lam ta‘lamoohum an tataoohum fatuseebakum minhum ma‘arratun bighayri ‘ilmin liyudkhila Allahu fee rahmatihi man yashao law tazayyaloo la‘aththabna allatheena kafaroo minhum ‘athaban aleema
48|26|Ith ja‘ala allatheena kafaroo fee quloobihimu alhamiyyata hamiyyata aljahiliyyati faanzala Allahu sakeenatahu ‘ala rasoolihi wa‘ala almumineena waalzamahum kalimata attaqwa wakanoo ahaqqa biha waahlaha wakana Allahu bikulli shayin ‘aleema
48|27|Laqad sadaqa Allahu rasoolahu arruya bilhaqqi latadkhulunna almasjida alharama in shaa Allahu amineena muhalliqeena ruoosakum wamuqassireena la takhafoona fa‘alima ma lam ta‘lamoo faja‘ala min dooni thalika fathan qareeba
48|28|Huwa allathee arsala rasoolahu bilhuda wadeeni alhaqqi liyuthhirahu ‘ala addeeni kullihi wakafa billahi shaheeda
48|29|Muhammadun rasoolu Allahi wallatheena ma‘ahu ashiddao ‘ala alkuffari ruhamao baynahum tarahum rukka‘an sujjadan yabtaghoona fadlan mina Allahi waridwanan seemahum fee wujoohihim min athari assujoodi thalika mathaluhum fee attawrati wamathaluhum fee alinjeeli kazar‘in akhraja shatahu faazarahu fastaghlatha fastawa ‘ala sooqihi yu‘jibu azzurra‘a liyagheetha bihimu alkuffara wa‘ada Allahu allatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati minhum maghfiratan waajran ‘atheema
49|1|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo la tuqaddimoo bayna yadayi Allahi warasoolihi wattaqoo Allaha inna Allaha samee‘un ‘aleem
49|2|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo la tarfa‘oo aswatakum fawqa sawti annabiyyi wala tajharoo lahu bilqawli kajahri ba‘dikum liba‘din an tahbata a‘malukum waantum la tash‘uroon
49|3|Inna allatheena yaghuddoona aswatahum ‘inda rasooli Allahi olaika allatheena imtahana Allahu quloobahum littaqwa lahum maghfiratun waajrun ‘atheem
49|4|Inna allatheena yunadoonaka min warai alhujurati aktharuhum la ya‘qiloon
49|5|Walaw annahum sabaroo hatta takhruja ilayhim lakana khayran lahum wallahu ghafoorun raheem
49|6|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo in jaakum fasiqun binabain fatabayyanoo an tuseeboo qawman bijahalatin fatusbihoo ‘ala ma fa‘altum nadimeen
49|7|Wa‘lamoo anna feekum rasoola Allahi law yutee‘ukum fee katheerin mina alamri la‘anittum walakinna Allaha habbaba ilaykumu aleemana wazayyanahu fee quloobikum wakarraha ilaykumu alkufra walfusooqa wal‘isyana olaika humu arrashidoon
49|8|Fadlan mina Allahi wani‘matan wallahu ‘aleemun hakeem
49|9|Wain taifatani mina almumineena iqtataloo faaslihoo baynahuma fain baghat ihdahuma ‘ala alokhra faqatiloo allatee tabghee hatta tafeea ila amri Allahi fain faat faaslihoo baynahuma bil‘adli waaqsitoo inna Allaha yuhibbu almuqsiteen
49|10|Innama almuminoona ikhwatun faaslihoo bayna akhawaykum wattaqoo Allaha la‘allakum turhamoon
49|11|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo la yaskhar qawmun min qawmin ‘asa an yakoonoo khayran minhum wala nisaon min nisain ‘asa an yakunna khayran minhunna wala talmizoo anfusakum wala tanabazoo bilalqabi bisa alismu alfusooqu ba‘da aleemani waman lam yatub faolaika humu aththalimoon
49|12|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo ijtaniboo katheeran mina aththanni inna ba‘da aththanni ithmun wala tajassasoo wala yaghtab ba‘dukum ba‘dan ayuhibbu ahadukum an yakula lahma akheehi maytan fakarihtumoohu wattaqoo Allaha inna Allaha tawwabun raheem
49|13|Ya ayyuha annasu inna khalaqnakum min thakarin waontha waja‘alnakum shu‘ooban waqabaila lita‘arafoo inna akramakum ‘inda Allahi atqakum inna Allaha ‘aleemun khabeer
49|14|Qalati ala‘rabu amanna qul lam tuminoo walakin qooloo aslamna walamma yadkhuli aleemanu fee quloobikum wain tutee‘oo Allaha warasoolahu la yalitkum min a‘malikum shayan inna Allaha ghafoorun raheem
49|15|Innama almuminoona allatheena amanoo billahi warasoolihi thumma lam yartaboo wajahadoo biamwalihim waanfusihim fee sabeeli Allahi olaika humu assadiqoon
49|16|Qul atu‘allimoona Allaha bideenikum wallahu ya‘lamu ma fee assamawati wama fee alardi wallahu bikulli shayin ‘aleem
