56|96|Fasabbih bismi rabbika al‘atheem
57|1|Sabbaha lillahi ma fee assamawati walardi wahuwa al‘azeezu alhakeem
57|2|Lahu mulku assamawati walardi yuhyee wayumeetu wahuwa ‘ala kulli shayin qadeer
57|3|Huwa alawwalu walakhiru waththahiru walbatinu wahuwa bikulli shayin ‘aleem
57|4|Huwa allathee khalaqa assamawati walarda fee sittati ayyamin thumma istawa ‘ala al‘arshi ya‘lamu ma yaliju fee alardi wama yakhruju minha wama yanzilu mina assamai wama ya‘ruju feeha wahuwa ma‘akum ayna ma kuntum wallahu bima ta‘maloona baseer
57|5|Lahu mulku assamawati walardi waila Allahi turja‘u alomoor
57|6|Yooliju allayla fee annahari wayooliju annahara fee allayi wahuwa ‘aleemun bithati assudoor
57|7|Aminoo billahi warasoolihi waanfiqoo mimma ja‘alakum mustakhlafeena feehi fallatheena amanoo minkum waanfaqoo lahum ajrun kabeer
57|8|Wama lakum la tuminoona billahi warrasoolu yad‘ookum lituminoo birabbikum waqad akhatha meethaqakum in kuntum mumineen
57|9|Huwa allathee yunazzilu ‘ala ‘abdihi ayatin bayyinatin liyukhrijakum mina aththulumati ila annoori wainna Allaha bikum laraoofun raheem
57|10|Wama lakum alla tunfiqoo fee sabeeli Allahi walillahi meerathu assamawati walardi la yastawee minkum man anfaqa min qabli alfathi waqatala olaika a‘thamu darajatan mina allatheena anfaqoo min ba‘du waqataloo wakullan wa‘ada Allahu alhusna wallahu bima ta‘maloona khabeer
57|11|Man tha allathee yuqridu Allaha qardan hasanan fayuda‘ifahu lahu walahu ajrun kareem
57|12|Yawma tara almumineena walmuminati yas‘a nooruhum bayna aydeehim wabiaymanihim bushrakumu alyawma jannatun tajree min tahtiha alanharu khalideena feeha thalika huwa alfawzu al‘atheem
57|13|Yawma yaqoolu almunafiqoona walmunafiqatu lillatheena amanoo onthuroona naqtabis min noorikum qeela irji‘oo waraakum faltamisoo nooran faduriba baynahum bisoorin lahu babun batinuhu feehi arrahmatu wathahiruhu min qibalihi al‘athab
57|14|Yunadoonahum alam nakun ma‘akum qaloo bala walakinnakum fatantum anfusakum watarabbastum wartabtum wagharratkumu alamaniyyu hatta jaa amru Allahi wagharrakum billahi algharoor
57|15|Falyawma la yukhathu minkum fidyatun wala mina allatheena kafaroo mawakumu annaru hiya mawlakum wabisa almaseer
57|16|Alam yani lillatheena amanoo an takhsha‘a quloobuhum lithikri Allahi wama nazala mina alhaqqi wala yakoonoo kallatheena ootoo alkitaba min qablu fatala ‘alayhimu alamadu faqasat quloobuhum wakatheerun minhum fasiqoon
57|17|I‘lamoo anna Allaha yuhyee alarda ba‘da mawtiha qad bayyanna lakumu alayati la‘allakum ta‘qiloon
57|18|Inna almussaddiqeena walmussaddiqati waaqradoo Allaha qardan hasanan yuda‘afu lahum walahum ajrun kareem
57|19|Wallatheena amanoo billahi warusulihi olaika humu assiddeeqoona washshuhadao ‘inda rabbihim lahum ajruhum wanooruhum wallatheena kafaroo wakaththaboo biayatina olaika ashabu aljaheem
57|20|I‘lamoo annama alhayatu addunya la‘ibun walahwun wazeenatun watafakhurun baynakum watakathurun fee alamwali walawladi kamathali ghaythin a‘jaba alkuffara nabatuhu thumma yaheeju fatarahu musfarran thumma yakoonu hutaman wafee alakhirati ‘athabun shadeedun wamaghfiratun mina Allahi waridwanun wama alhayatu addunya illa mata‘u alghuroor
57|21|Sabiqoo ila maghfiratin min rabbikum wajannatin ‘arduha ka‘ardi assamai walardi o‘iddat lillatheena amanoo billahi warusulihi thalika fadlu Allahi yuteehi man yashao wallahu thoo alfadli al‘atheem
57|22|Ma asaba min museebatin fee alardi wala fee anfusikum illa fee kitabin min qabli an nabraaha inna thalika ‘ala Allahi yaseer
57|23|Likayla tasaw ‘ala ma fatakum wala tafrahoo bima atakum wallahu la yuhibbu kulla mukhtalin fakhoor
57|24|Allatheena yabkhaloona wayamuroona annasa bilbukhli waman yatawalla fainna Allaha huwa alghaniyyu alhameed
57|25|Laqad arsalna rusulana bilbayyinati waanzalna ma‘ahumu alkitaba walmeezana liyaqooma annasu bilqisti waanzalna alhadeeda feehi basun shadeedun wamanafi‘u linnasi waliya‘lama Allahu man yansuruhu warusulahu bilghaybi inna Allaha qawiyyun ‘azeez
57|26|Walaqad arsalna noohan waibraheema waja‘alna fee thurriyyatihima annubuwwata walkitaba faminhum muhtadin wakatheerun minhum fasiqoon
57|27|Thumma qaffayna ‘ala atharihim birusulina waqaffayna bi‘eesa ibni maryama waataynahu alinjeela waja‘alna fee quloobi allatheena ittaba‘oohu rafatan warahmatan warahbaniyyatan ibtada‘ooha ma katabnaha ‘alayhim illa ibtighaa ridwani Allahi fama ra‘awha haqqa ri‘ayatiha faatayna allatheena amanoo minhum ajrahum wakatheerun minhum fasiqoon
57|28|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo ittaqoo Allaha waaminoo birasoolihi yutikum kiflayni min rahmatihi wayaj‘al lakum nooran tamshoona bihi wayaghfir lakum wallahu ghafoorun raheem
57|29|Lialla ya‘lama ahlu alkitabi alla yaqdiroona ‘ala shayin min fadli Allahi waanna alfadla biyadi Allahi yuteehi man yashao wallahu thoo alfadli al‘atheem
58|1|Qad sami‘a Allahu qawla allatee tujadiluka fee zawjiha watashtakee ila Allahi wallahu yasma‘u tahawurakuma inna Allaha samee‘un baseer
58|2|Allatheena yuthahiroona minkum min nisaihim ma hunna ommahatihim in ommahatuhum illa allaee waladnahum wainnahum layaqooloona munkaran mina alqawli wazooran wainna Allaha la‘afuwwun ghafoor
58|3|Wallatheena yuthahiroona min nisaihim thumma ya‘oodoona lima qaloo fatahreeru raqabatin min qabli an yatamassa thalikum too‘athoona bihi wallahu bima ta‘maloona khabeer
58|4|Faman lam yajid fasiyamu shahrayni mutatabi‘ayni min qabli an yatamassa faman lam yastati‘ fait‘amu sitteena miskeenan thalika lituminoo billahi warasoolihi watilka hudoodu Allahi walilkafireena ‘athabun aleem
58|5|Inna allatheena yuhaddoona Allaha warasoolahu kubitoo kama kubita allatheena min qablihim waqad anzalna ayatin bayyinatin walilkafireena ‘athabun muheen
58|6|Yawma yab‘athuhumu Allahu jamee‘an fayunabbiohum bima ‘amiloo ahsahu Allahu wanasoohu wallahu ‘ala kulli shayin shaheed
58|7|Alam tara anna Allaha ya‘lamu ma fee assamawati wama fee alardi ma yakoonu min najwa thalathatin illa huwa rabi‘uhum wala khamsatin illa huwa sadisuhum wala adna min thalika wala akthara illa huwa ma‘ahum ayna ma kanoo thumma yunabbiohum bima ‘amiloo yawma alqiyamati inna Allaha bikulli shayin ‘aleem
58|8|Alam tara ila allatheena nuhoo ‘ani annajwa thumma ya‘oodoona lima nuhoo ‘anhu wayatanajawna bilithmi wal‘udwani wama‘siyati arrasooli waitha jaooka hayyawka bima lam yuhayyika bihi Allahu wayaqooloona fee anfusihim lawla yu‘aththibuna Allahu bima naqoolu hasbuhum jahannamu yaslawnaha fabisa almaseer
58|9|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo itha tanajaytum fala tatanajaw bilithmi wal‘udwani wama‘siyati arrasooli watanajaw bilbirri wattaqwa wattaqoo Allaha allathee ilayhi tuhsharoon
58|10|Innama annajwa mina ashshaytani liyahzuna allatheena amanoo walaysa bidarrihim shayan illa biithni Allahi wa‘ala Allahi falyatawakkali almuminoon
58|11|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo itha qeela lakum tafassahoo fee almajalisi fafsahoo yafsahi Allahu lakum waitha qeela onshuzoo fanshuzoo yarfa‘i Allahu allatheena amanoo minkum wallatheena ootoo al‘ilma darajatin wallahu bima ta‘maloona khabeer
58|12|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo itha najaytumu arrasoola faqaddimoo bayna yaday najwakum sadaqatan thalika khayrun lakum waatharu fain lam tajidoo fainna Allaha ghafoorun raheem
58|13|Aashfaqtum an tuqaddimoo bayna yaday najwakum sadaqatin faith lam taf‘aloo wataba Allahu ‘alaykum faaqeemoo assalata waatoo azzakata waatee‘oo Allaha warasoolahu wallahu khabeerun bima ta‘maloon
58|14|Alam tara ila allatheena tawallaw qawman ghadiba Allahu ‘alayhim ma hum minkum wala minhum wayahlifoona ‘ala alkathibi wahum ya‘lamoon
58|15|a‘adda Allahu lahum ‘athaban shadeedan innahum saa ma kanoo ya‘maloon
58|16|Ittakhathoo aymanahum junnatan fasaddoo ‘an sabeeli Allahi falahum ‘athabun muheen
58|17|Lan tughniya ‘anhum amwaluhum wala awladuhum mina Allahi shayan olaika ashabu annari hum feeha khalidoon
58|18|Yawma yab‘athuhumu Allahu jamee‘an fayahlifoona lahu kama yahlifoona lakum wayahsaboona annahum ‘ala shayin ala innahum humu alkathiboon
58|19|Istahwatha ‘alayhimu ashshaytanu faansahum thikra Allahi olaika hizbu ashshaytani ala inna hizba ashshaytani humu alkhasiroon
58|20|Inna allatheena yuhaddoona Allaha warasoolahu olaika fee alathalleen
58|21|Kataba Allahu laaghlibanna ana warusulee inna Allaha qawiyyun ‘azeez
58|22|La tajidu qawman yuminoona billahi walyawmi alakhiri yuwaddoona man hadda Allaha warasoolahu walaw kanoo abaahum aw abnaahum aw ikhwanahum aw ‘asheeratahum olaika kataba fee quloobihimu aleemana waayyadahum biroohin minhu wayudkhiluhum jannatin tajree min tahtiha alanharu khalideena feeha radiya Allahu ‘anhum waradoo ‘anhu olaika hizbu Allahi ala inna hizba Allahi humu almuflihoon
59|1|Sabbaha lillahi ma fee assamawati wama fee alardi wahuwa al‘azeezu alhakeem
59|2|Huwa allathee akhraja allatheena kafaroo min ahli alkitabi min diyarihim liawwali alhashri ma thanantum an yakhrujoo wathannoo annahum mani‘atuhum husoonuhum mina Allahi faatahumu Allahu min haythu lam yahtasiboo waqathafa fee quloobihimu arru‘ba yukhriboona buyootahum biaydeehim waaydee almumineena fa‘tabiroo ya olee alabsar
59|3|Walawla an kataba Allahu ‘alayhimu aljalaa la‘aththabahum fee addunya walahum fee alakhirati ‘athabu annar
59|4|Thalika biannahum shaqqoo Allaha warasoolahu waman yushaqqi Allaha fainna Allaha shadeedu al‘iqab
59|5|Ma qata‘tum min leenatin aw taraktumooha qaimatan ‘ala osooliha fabiithni Allahi waliyukhziya alfasiqeen
59|6|Wama afaa Allahu ‘ala rasoolihi minhum fama awjaftum ‘alayhi min khaylin wala rikabin walakinna Allaha yusallitu rusulahu ‘ala man yashao wallahu ‘ala kulli shayin qadeer
59|7|Ma afaa Allahu ‘ala rasoolihi min ahli alqura falillahi walirrasooli walithee alqurba walyatama walmasakeeni wabni assabeeli kay la yakoona doolatan bayna alaghniyai minkum wama atakumu arrasoolu fakhuthoohu wama nahakum ‘anhu fantahoo wattaqoo Allaha inna Allaha shadeedu al‘iqab
59|8|Lilfuqarai almuhajireena allatheena okhrijoo min diyarihim waamwalihim yabtaghoona fadlan mina Allahi waridwanan wayansuroona Allaha warasoolahu olaika humu assadiqoon
59|9|Wallatheena tabawwaoo addara waleemana min qablihim yuhibboona man hajara ilayhim wala yajidoona fee sudoorihim hajatan mimma ootoo wayuthiroona ‘ala anfusihim walaw kana bihim khasasatun waman yooqa shuhha nafsihi faolaika humu almuflihoon
59|10|Wallatheena jaoo min ba‘dihim yaqooloona rabbana ighfir lana waliikhwanina allatheena sabaqoona bileemani wala taj‘al fee quloobina ghillan lillatheena amanoo rabbana innaka raoofun raheem
59|11|Alam tara ila allatheena nafaqoo yaqooloona liikhwanihimu allatheena kafaroo min ahli alkitabi lain okhrijtum lanakhrujanna ma‘akum wala nutee‘u feekum ahadan abadan wain qootiltum lanansurannakum wallahu yashhadu innahum lakathiboon
59|12|Lain okhrijoo la yakhrujoona ma‘ahum walain qootiloo la yansuroonahum walain nasaroohum layuwallunna aladbara thumma la yunsaroon
59|13|Laantum ashaddu rahbatan fee sudoorihim mina Allahi thalika biannahum qawmun la yafqahoon
59|14|La yuqatiloonakum jamee‘an illa fee quran muhassanatin aw min warai judurin basuhum baynahum shadeedun tahsabuhum jamee‘an waquloobuhum shatta thalika biannahum qawmun la ya‘qiloon
59|15|Kamathali allatheena min qablihim qareeban thaqoo wabala amrihim walahum ‘athabun aleem
59|16|Kamathali ashshaytani ith qala lilinsani okfur falamma kafara qala innee bareeon minka innee akhafu Allaha rabba al‘alameen
59|17|Fakana ‘aqibatahuma annahuma fee annari khalidayni feeha wathalika jazao aththalimeen
59|18|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo ittaqoo Allaha waltanthur nafsun ma qaddamat lighadin wattaqoo Allaha inna Allaha khabeerun bima ta‘maloon
59|19|Wala takoonoo kallatheena nasoo Allaha faansahum anfusahum olaika humu alfasiqoon
59|20|La yastawee ashabu annari waashabu aljannati ashabu aljannati humu alfaizoon
59|21|Law anzalna hatha alqurana ‘ala jabalin laraaytahu khashi‘an mutasaddi‘an min khashyati Allahi watilka alamthalu nadribuha linnasi la‘allahum yatafakkaroon
59|22|Huwa Allahu allathee la ilaha illa huwa ‘alimu alghaybi washshahadati huwa arrahmanu arraheem
59|23|Huwa Allahu allathee la ilaha illa huwa almaliku alquddoosu assalamu almuminu almuhayminu al‘azeezu aljabbaru almutakabbiru subhana Allahi ‘amma yushrikoon
59|24|Huwa Allahu alkhaliqu albario almusawwiru lahu alasmao alhusna yusabbihu lahu ma fee assamawati walardi wahuwa al‘azeezu alhakeem
60|1|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo la tattakhithoo ‘aduwwee wa‘aduwwakum awliyaa tulqoona ilayhim bilmawaddati waqad kafaroo bima jaakum mina alhaqqi yukhrijoona arrasoola waiyyakum an tuminoo billahi rabbikum in kuntum kharajtum jihadan fee sabeelee wabtighaa mardatee tusirroona ilayhim bilmawaddati waana a‘lamu bima akhfaytum wama a‘lantum waman yaf‘alhu minkum faqad dalla sawaa assabeel
60|2|In yathqafookum yakoonoo lakam a‘daan wayabsutoo ilaykum aydiyahum waalsinatahum bissooi wawaddoo law takfuroon
60|3|Lan tanfa‘akum arhamukum wala awladukum yawma alqiyamati yafsilu baynakum wallahu bima ta‘maloona baseer
60|4|Qad kanat lakum oswatun hasanatun fee ibraheema wallatheena ma‘ahu ith qaloo liqawmihim inna buraao minkum wamimma ta‘budoona min dooni Allahi kafarna bikum wabada baynana wabaynakumu al‘adawatu walbaghdao abadan hatta tuminoo billahi wahdahu illa qawla ibraheema liabeehi laastaghfiranna laka wama amliku laka mina Allahi min shayin rabbana ‘alayka tawakkalna wailayka anabna wailayka almaseer
60|5|Rabbana la taj‘alna fitnatan lillatheena kafaroo waghfir lana rabbana innaka anta al‘azeezu alhakeem
60|6|Laqad kana lakum feehim oswatun hasanatun liman kana yarjoo Allaha walyawma alakhira waman yatawalla fainna Allaha huwa alghanniyyu alhameed
60|7|‘asa Allahu an yaj‘ala baynakum wabayna allatheena ‘adaytum minhum mawaddatan wallahu qadeerun wallahu ghafoorun raheem
60|8|La yanhakumu Allahu ‘ani allatheena lam yuqatilookum fee addeeni walam yukhrijookum min diyarikum an tabarroohum watuqsitoo ilayhim inna Allaha yuhibbu almuqsiteen
60|9|Innama yanhakumu Allahu ‘ani allatheena qatalookum fee addeeni waakhrajookum min diyarikum wathaharoo ‘ala ikhrajikum an tawallawhum waman yatawallahum faolaika humu aththalimoon
60|10|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo itha jaakumu almuminatu muhajiratin famtahinoohunna Allahu a‘lamu bieemanihinna fain ‘alimtumoohunna muminatin fala tarji‘oohunna ila alkuffari la hunna hillun lahum wala hum yahilloona lahunna waatoohum ma anfaqoo wala junaha ‘alaykum an tankihoohunna itha ataytumoohunna ojoorahunna wala tumsikoo bi‘isami alkawafiri wasaloo ma anfaqtum walyasaloo ma anfaqoo thalikum hukmu Allahi yahkumu baynakum wallahu ‘aleemun hakeem
60|11|Wain fatakum shayon min azwajikum ila alkuffari fa‘aqabtum faatoo allatheena thahabat azwajuhum mithla ma anfaqoo wattaqoo Allaha allathee antum bihi muminoon
60|12|Ya ayyuha annabiyyu itha jaaka almuminatu yubayi‘naka ‘ala an la yushrikna billahi shayan wala yasriqna wala yazneena wala yaqtulna awladahunna wala yateena bibuhtanin yaftareenahu bayna aydeehinna waarjulihinna wala ya‘seenaka fee ma‘roofin fabayi‘hunna wastaghfir lahunna Allaha inna Allaha ghafoorun raheem
60|13|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo la tatawallaw qawman ghadiba Allahu ‘alayhim qad yaisoo mina alakhirati kama yaisa alkuffaru min ashabi alquboor
61|1|Sabbaha lillahi ma fee assamawati wama fee alardi wahuwa al‘azeezu alhakeem
61|2|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo lima taqooloona ma la taf‘aloon
61|3|Kabura maqtan ‘inda Allahi an taqooloo ma la taf‘aloon
61|4|Inna Allaha yuhibbu allatheena yuqatiloona fee sabeelihi saffan kaannahum bunyanun marsoos
61|5|Waith qala moosa liqawmihi ya qawmi lima tuthoonanee waqad ta‘lamoona annee rasoolu Allahi ilaykum falamma zaghoo azagha Allahu quloobahum wallahu la yahdee alqawma alfasiqeen
61|6|Waith qala ‘eesa ibnu maryama ya banee israeela innee rasoolu Allahi ilaykum musaddiqan lima bayna yadayya mina attawrati wamubashshiran birasoolin yatee min ba‘dee ismuhu ahmadu falamma jaahum bilbayyinati qaloo hatha sihrun mubeen
61|7|Waman athlamu mimmani iftara ‘ala Allahi alkathiba wahuwa yud‘a ila alislami wallahu la yahdee alqawma aththalimeen
61|8|Yureedoona liyutfioo noora Allahi biafwahihim wallahu mutimmu noorihi walaw kariha alkafiroon
61|9|Huwa allathee arsala rasoolahu bilhuda wadeeni alhaqqi liyuthhirahu ‘ala addeeni kullihi walaw kariha almushrikoon
61|10|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo hal adullukum ‘ala tijaratin tunjeekum min ‘athabin aleem
61|11|Tuminoona billahi warasoolihi watujahidoona fee sabeeli Allahi biamwalikum waanfusikum thalikum khayrun lakum in kuntum ta‘lamoon
61|12|Yaghfir lakum thunoobakum wayudkhilkum jannatin tajree min tahtiha alanharu wamasakina tayyibatan fee jannati ‘adnin thalika alfawzu al‘atheem
61|13|Waokhra tuhibboonaha nasrun mina Allahi wafathun qarreebun wabashshiri almumineen
61|14|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo koonoo ansara Allahi kama qala ‘eesa ibnu maryama lilhawariyyeena man ansaree ila Allahi qala alhawariyyoona nahnu ansaru Allahi faamanat taifatun min banee israeela wakafarat taifatun faayyadna allatheena amanoo ‘ala ‘aduwwihim faasbahoo thahireen
62|1|Yusabbihu lillahi ma fee assamawati wama fee alardi almaliki alquddoosi al‘azeezi alhakeem
62|2|Huwa allathee ba‘atha fee alommiyyeena rasoolan minhum yatloo ‘alayhim ayatihi wayuzakkeehim wayu‘allimuhumu alkitaba walhikmata wain kanoo min qablu lafee dalalin mubeen
62|3|Waakhareena minhum lamma yalhaqoo bihim wahuwa al‘azeezu alhakeem
62|4|Thalika fadlu Allahi yuteehi man yashao wallahu thoo alfadli al‘atheem
62|5|Mathalu allatheena hummiloo attawrata thumma lam yahmilooha kamathali alhimari yahmilu asfaran bisa mathalu alqawmi allatheena kaththaboo biayati Allahi wallahu la yahdee alqawma aththalimeen
62|6|Qul ya ayyuha allatheena hadoo in za‘amtum annakum awliyao lillahi min dooni annasi fatamannawoo almawta in kuntum sadiqeen
62|7|Wala yatamannawnahu abadan bima qaddamat aydeehim wallahu ‘aleemun biththalimeen
62|8|Qul inna almawta allathee tafirroona minhu fainnahu mulaqeekum thumma turaddoona ila ‘alimi alghaybi washshahadati fayunabbiokum bima kuntum ta‘maloon
62|9|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo itha noodiya lissalati min yawmi aljumu‘ati fas‘aw ila thikri Allahi watharoo albay‘a thalikum khayrun lakum in kuntum ta‘lamoon
62|10|Faitha qudiyati assalatu fantashiroo fee alardi wabtaghoo min fadli Allahi wathkuroo Allaha katheeran la‘allakum tuflihoon
62|11|Waitha raaw tijaratan aw lahwan infaddoo ilayha watarakooka qaiman qul ma ‘inda Allahi khayrun mina allahwi wamina attijarati wallahu khayru arraziqeen
63|1|Itha jaaka almunafiqoona qaloo nashhadu innaka larasoolu Allahi wallahu ya‘lamu innaka larasooluhu wallahu yashhadu inna almunafiqeena lakathiboon
63|2|Ittakhathoo aymanahum junnatan fasaddoo ‘an sabeeli Allahi innahum saa ma kanoo ya‘maloon
63|3|Thalika biannahum amanoo thumma kafaroo fatubi‘a ‘ala quloobihim fahum la yafqahoon
63|4|Waitha raaytahum tu‘jibuka ajsamuhum wain yaqooloo tasma‘ liqawlihim kaannahum khushubun musannadatun yahsaboona kulla sayhatin ‘alayhim humu al‘aduwwu fahtharhum qatalahumu Allahu anna yufakoon
63|5|Waitha qeela lahum ta‘alaw yastaghfir lakum rasoolu Allahi lawwaw ruoosahum waraaytahum yasuddoona wahum mustakbiroon
63|6|Sawaon ‘alayhim astaghfarta lahum am lam tastaghfir lahum lan yaghfira Allahu lahum inna Allaha la yahdee alqawma alfasiqeen
63|7|Humu allatheena yaqooloona la tunfiqoo ‘ala man ‘inda rasooli Allahi hatta yanfaddoo walillahi khazainu assamawati walardi walakinna almunafiqeena la yafqahoon
63|8|Yaqooloona lain raja‘na ila almadeenati layukhrijanna ala‘azzu minha alathalla walillahi al‘izzatu walirasoolihi walilmumineena walakinna almunafiqeena la ya‘lamoon
63|9|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo la tulhikum amwalukum wala awladukum ‘an thikri Allahi waman yaf‘al thalika faolaika humu alkhasiroon
63|10|Waanfiqoo min ma razaqnakum min qabli an yatiya ahadakumu almawtu fayaqoola rabbi lawla akhkhartanee ila ajalin qareebin faassaddaqa waakun mina assaliheen
63|11|Walan yuakhkhira Allahu nafsan itha jaa ajaluha wallahu khabeerun bima ta‘maloon
64|1|Yusabbihu lillahi ma fee assamawati wama fee alardi lahu almulku walahu alhamdu wahuwa ‘ala kulli shayin qadeer
64|2|Huwa allathee khalaqakum faminkum kafirun waminkum muminun wallahu bima ta‘maloona baseer
64|3|Khalaqa assamawati walarda bilhaqqi wasawwarakum faahsana suwarakum wailayhi almaseer
64|4|Ya‘lamu ma fee assamawati walardi waya‘lamu ma tusirroona wama tu‘linoona wallahu ‘aleemun bithati assudoor
64|5|Alam yatikum nabao allatheena kafaroo min qablu fathaqoo wabala amrihim walahum ‘athabun aleem
64|6|Thalika biannahu kanat tateehim rusuluhum bilbayyinati faqaloo abasharun yahdoonana fakafaroo watawallaw wastaghna Allahu wallahu ghaniyyun hameed
64|7|Za‘ama allatheena kafaroo an lan yub‘athoo qul bala warabbee latub‘athunna thumma latunabbaonna bima ‘amiltum wathalika ‘ala Allahi yaseer
64|8|Faaminoo billahi warasoolihi wannoori allathee anzalna wallahu bima ta‘maloona khabeer
64|9|Yawma yajma‘ukum liyawmi aljam‘i thalika yawmu attaghabuni waman yumin billahi waya‘mal salihan yukaffir ‘anhu sayyiatihi wayudkhilhu jannatin tajree min tahtiha alanharu khalideena feeha abadan thalika alfawzu al‘atheem
64|10|Wallatheena kafaroo wakaththaboo biayatina olaika ashabu annari khalideena feeha wabisa almaseer
64|11|Ma asaba min museebatin illa biithni Allahi waman yumin billahi yahdi qalbahu wallahu bikulli shayin ‘aleem
64|12|Waatee‘oo Allaha waatee‘oo arrasoola fain tawallaytum fainnama ‘ala rasoolina albalaghu almubeen
64|13|Allahu la ilaha illa huwa wa‘ala Allahi falyatawakkali almuminoon
64|14|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo inna min azwajikum waawladikum ‘aduwwan lakum fahtharoohum wain ta‘foo watasfahoo wataghfiroo fainna Allaha ghafoorun raheem
64|15|Innama amwalukum waawladukum fitnatun wallahu ‘indahu ajrun ‘atheem
64|16|Fattaqoo Allaha ma istata‘tum wasma‘oo waatee‘oo waanfiqoo khayran lianfusikum waman yooqa shuhha nafsihi faolaika humu almuflihoon
64|17|In tuqridoo Allaha qardan hasanan yuda‘ifhu lakum wayaghfir lakum wallahu shakoorun haleem
64|18|‘alimu alghaybi washshahadati al‘azeezu alhakeem
65|1|Ya ayyuha annabiyyu itha tallaqtumu annisaa fatalliqoohunna li‘iddatihinna waahsoo al‘iddata wattaqoo Allaha rabbakum la tukhrijoohunna min buyootihinna wala yakhrujna illa an yateena bifahishatin mubayyinatin watilka hudoodu Allahi waman yata‘adda hudooda Allahi faqad thalama nafsahu la tadree la‘alla Allaha yuhdithu ba‘da thalika amra
65|2|Faitha balaghna ajalahunna faamsikoohunna bima‘roofin aw fariqoohunna bima‘roofin waashhidoo thaway ‘adlin minkum waaqeemoo ashshahadata lillahi thalikum yoo‘athu bihi man kana yuminu billahi walyawmi alakhiri waman yattaqi Allaha yaj‘al lahu makhraja
65|3|Wayarzuqhu min haythu la yahtasibu waman yatawakkal ‘ala Allahi fahuwa hasbuhu inna Allaha balighu amrihi qad ja‘ala Allahu likulli shayin qadra
65|4|Wallaee yaisna mina almaheedi min nisaikum ini irtabtum fa‘iddatuhunna thalathatu ashhurin wallaee lam yahidna waolatu alahmali ajaluhunna an yada‘na hamlahunna waman yattaqi Allaha yaj‘al lahu min amrihi yusra
65|5|Thalika amru Allahi anzalahu ilaykum waman yattaqi Allaha yukaffir ‘anhu sayyiatihi wayu‘thim lahu ajra
65|6|Askinoohunna min haythu sakantum min wujdikum wala tudarroohunna litudayyiqoo ‘alayhinna wain kunna olati hamlin faanfiqoo ‘alayhinna hatta yada‘na hamlahunna fain arda‘na lakum faatoohunna ojoorahunna watamiroo baynakum bima‘roofin wain ta‘asartum fasaturdi‘u lahu okhra
65|7|Liyunfiq thoo sa‘atin min sa‘atihi waman qudira ‘alayhi rizquhu falyunfiq mimma atahu Allahu la yukallifu Allahu nafsan illa ma ataha sayaj‘alu Allahu ba‘da ‘usrin yusra
65|8|Wakaayyin min qaryatin ‘atat ‘an amri rabbiha warusulihi fahasabnaha hisaban shadeedan wa‘aththabnaha ‘athaban nukra
65|9|Fathaqat wabala amriha wakana ‘aqibatu amriha khusra
65|10|a‘adda Allahu lahum ‘athaban shadeedan fattaqoo Allaha ya olee alalbabi allatheena amanoo qad anzala Allahu ilaykum thikra
65|11|Rasoolan yatloo ‘alaykum ayati Allahi mubayyinatin liyukhrija allatheena amanoo wa‘amiloo assalihati mina aththulumati ila annoori waman yumin billahi waya‘mal salihan yudkhilhu jannatin tajree min tahtiha alanharu khalideena feeha abadan qad ahsana Allahu lahu rizqa
65|12|Allahu allathee khalaqa sab‘a samawatin wamina alardi mithlahunna yatanazzalu alamru baynahunna lita‘lamoo anna Allaha ‘ala kulli shayin qadeerun waanna Allaha qad ahata bikulli shayin ‘ilma
66|1|Ya ayyuha annabiyyu lima tuharrimu ma ahalla Allahu laka tabtaghee mardata azwajika wallahu ghafoorun raheem
66|2|Qad farada Allahu lakum tahillata aymanikum wallahu mawlakum wahuwa al‘aleemu alhakeem
66|3|Waith asarra annabiyyu ila ba‘di azwajihi hadeethan falamma nabbaat bihi waathharahu Allahu ‘alayhi ‘arrafa ba‘dahu waa‘rada ‘an ba‘din falamma nabbaaha bihi qalat man anbaaka hatha qala nabbaaniya al‘aleemu alkhabeer
66|4|In tatooba ila Allahi faqad saghat quloobukuma wain tathahara ‘alayhi fainna Allaha huwa mawlahu wajibreelu wasalihu almumineena walmalaikatu ba‘da thalika thaheer
66|5|‘asa rabbuhu in tallaqakunna an yubdilahu azwajan khayran minkunna muslimatin muminatin qanitatin taibatin ‘abidatin saihatin thayyibatin waabkara
66|6|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo qoo anfusakum waahleekum naran waqooduha annasu walhijaratu ‘alayha malaikatun ghilathun shidadun la ya‘soona Allaha ma amarahum wayaf‘aloona ma yumaroon
66|7|Ya ayyuha allatheena kafaroo la ta‘tathiroo alyawma innama tujzawna ma kuntum ta‘maloon
66|8|Ya ayyuha allatheena amanoo tooboo ila Allahi tawbatan nasoohan ‘asa rabbukum an yukaffira ‘ankum sayyiatikum wayudkhilakum jannatin tajree min tahtiha alanharu yawma la yukhzee Allahu annabiyya wallatheena amanoo ma‘ahu nooruhum yas‘a bayna aydeehim wabiaymanihim yaqooloona rabbana atmim lana noorana waghfir lana innaka ‘ala kulli shayin qadeer
66|9|Ya ayyuha annabiyyu jahidi alkuffara walmunafiqeena waghluth ‘alayhim wamawahum jahannamu wabisa almaseer
66|10|Daraba Allahu mathalan lillatheena kafaroo imraata noohin wamraata lootin kanata tahta ‘abdayni min ‘ibadina salihayni fakhanatahuma falam yughniya ‘anhuma mina Allahi shayan waqeela odkhula annara ma‘a addakhileen
66|11|Wadaraba Allahu mathalan lillatheena amanoo imraata fir‘awna ith qalat rabbi ibni lee ‘indaka baytan fee aljannati wanajjinee min fir‘awna wa‘amalihi wanajjinee mina alqawmi aththalimeen
66|12|Wamaryama ibnata ‘imrana allatee ahsanat farjaha fanafakhna feehi min roohina wasaddaqat bikalimati rabbiha wakutubihi wakanat mina alqaniteen
67|1|Tabaraka allathee biyadihi almulku wahuwa ‘ala kulli shayin qadeer
67|2|Allathee khalaqa almawta walhayata liyabluwakum ayyukum ahsanu ‘amalan wahuwa al‘azeezu alghafoor
67|3|Allathee khalaqa sab‘a samawatin tibaqan ma tara fee khalqi arrahmani min tafawutin farji‘i albasara hal tara min futoor
67|4|Thumma irji‘i albasara karratayni yanqalib ilayka albasaru khasian wahuwa haseer
67|5|Walaqad zayyanna assamaa addunya bimasabeeha waja‘alnaha rujooman lishshayateeni waa‘tadna lahum ‘athaba assa‘eer
67|6|Walillatheena kafaroo birabbihim ‘athabu jahannama wabisa almaseer
67|7|Itha olqoo feeha sami‘oo laha shaheeqan wahiya tafoor
67|8|Takadu tamayyazu mina alghaythi kullama olqiya feeha fawjun saalahum khazanatuha alam yatikum natheer
67|9|Qaloo bala qad jaana natheerun fakaththabna waqulna ma nazzala Allahu min shayin in antum illa fee dalalin kabeer
67|10|Waqaloo law kunna nasma‘u aw na‘qilu ma kunna fee ashabi assa‘eer
67|11|Fa‘tarafoo bithanbihim fasuhqan liashabi assa‘eer
67|12|Inna allatheena yakhshawna rabbahum bilghaybi lahum maghfiratun waajrun kabeer
67|13|Waasirroo qawlakum awi ijharoo bihi innahu ‘aleemun bithati assudoor
67|14|Ala ya‘lamu man khalaqa wahuwa allateefu alkhabeer
67|15|Huwa allathee ja‘ala lakumu alarda thaloolan famshoo fee manakibiha wakuloo min rizqihi wailayhi annushoor
67|16|Aamintum man fee assamai an yakhsifa bikumu alarda faitha hiya tamoor
67|17|Am amintum man fee assamai an yursila ‘alaykum hasiban fasata‘lamoona kayfa natheer
67|18|Walaqad kaththaba allatheena min qablihim fakayfa kana nakeer
67|19|Awalam yaraw ila attayri fawqahum saffatin wayaqbidna ma yumsikuhunna illa arrahmanu innahu bikulli shayin baseer
67|20|Amman hatha allathee huwa jundun lakum yansurukum min dooni arrahmani ini alkafiroona illa fee ghuroor
67|21|Amman hatha allathee yarzuqukum in amsaka rizqahu bal lajjoo fee ‘utuwwin wanufoor
67|22|Afaman yamshee mukibban ‘ala wajhihi ahda amman yamshee sawiyyan ‘ala siratin mustaqeem
67|23|Qul huwa allathee anshaakum waja‘ala lakumu assam‘a walabsara walafidata qaleelan ma tashkuroon
67|24|Qul huwa allathee tharaakum fee alardi wailayhi tuhsharoon
67|25|Wayaqooloona mata hatha alwa‘du in kuntum sadiqeen
67|26|Qul innama al‘ilmu ‘inda Allahi wainnama ana natheerun mubeen
67|27|Falamma raawhu zulfatan seeat wujoohu allatheena kafaroo waqeela hatha allathee kuntum bihi tadda‘oon
67|28|Qul araaytum in ahlakaniya Allahu waman ma‘iya aw rahimana faman yujeeru alkafireena min ‘athabin aleem
67|29|Qul huwa arrahmanu amanna bihi wa‘alayhi tawakkalna fasata‘lamoona man huwa fee dalalin mubeen
67|30|Qul araaytum in asbaha maokum ghawran faman yateekum bimain ma‘een
68|1|Noon walqalami wama yasturoon
68|2|Ma anta bini‘mati rabbika bimajnoon
68|3|Wainna laka laajran ghayra mamnoon
68|4|Wainnaka la‘ala khuluqin ‘atheem
68|5|Fasatubsiru wayubsiroon
68|6|Biayyikumu almaftoon
68|7|Inna rabbaka huwa a‘lamu biman dalla ‘an sabeelihi wahuwa a‘lamu bilmuhtadeen
68|8|Fala tuti‘i almukaththibeen
68|9|Waddoo law tudhinu fayudhinoon
68|10|Wala tuti‘ kulla hallafin maheen
68|11|Hammazin mashshain binameem
68|12|Manna‘in lilkhayri mu‘tadin atheem
68|13|‘utullin ba‘da thalika zaneem
68|14|An kana tha malin wabaneen
68|15|Itha tutla ‘alayhi ayatuna qala asateeru alawwaleen
68|16|Sanasimuhu ‘ala alkhurtoom
68|17|Inna balawnahum kama balawna ashaba aljannati ith aqsamoo layasrimunnaha musbiheen
68|18|Wala yastathnoon
68|19|Fatafa ‘alayha taifun min rabbika wahum naimoon
68|20|Faasbahat kassareem
68|21|Fatanadaw musbiheen
68|22|Ani ighdoo ‘ala harthikum in kuntum sarimeen
68|23|Fantalaqoo wahum yatakhafatoon
68|24|An la yadkhulannaha alyawma ‘alaykum miskeen
68|25|Waghadaw ‘ala hardin qadireen
68|26|Falamma raawha qaloo inna ladalloon
68|27|Bal nahnu mahroomoon
68|28|Qala awsatuhum alam aqul lakum lawla tusabbihoon
68|29|Qaloo subhana rabbina inna kunna thalimeen
68|30|Faaqbala ba‘duhum ‘ala ba‘din yatalawamoon
68|31|Qaloo ya waylana inna kunna tagheen
68|32|‘asa rabbuna an yubdilana khayran minha inna ila rabbina raghiboon
68|33|Kathalika al‘athabu wala‘athabu alakhirati akbaru law kanoo ya‘lamoon
68|34|Inna lilmuttaqeena ‘inda rabbihim jannati anna‘eem
68|35|Afanaj‘alu almuslimeena kalmujrimeen
68|36|Ma lakum kayfa tahkumoon
68|37|Am lakum kitabun feehi tadrusoon
68|38|Inna lakum feehi lama takhayyaroon
68|39|Am lakum aymanun ‘alayna balighatun ila yawmi alqiyamati inna lakum lama tahkumoon
68|40|Salhum ayyuhum bithalika za‘eem
68|41|Am lahum shurakao falyatoo bishurakaihim in kanoo sadiqeen
68|42|Yawma yukshafu ‘an saqin wayud‘awna ila assujoodi fala yastatee‘oon
68|43|Khashi‘atan absaruhum tarhaquhum thillatun waqad kanoo yud‘awna ila assujoodi wahum salimoon
68|44|Fatharnee waman yukaththibu bihatha alhadeethi sanastadrijuhum min haythu la ya‘lamoon
68|45|Waomlee lahum inna kaydee mateen
68|46|Am tasaluhum ajran fahum min maghramin muthqaloon
68|47|Am ‘indahumu alghaybu fahum yaktuboon
68|48|Fasbir lihukmi rabbika wala takun kasahibi alhooti ith nada wahuwa makthoom
68|49|Lawla an tadarakahu ni‘matun min rabbihi lanubitha bil‘arai wahuwa mathmoom
68|50|Fajtabahu rabbuhu faja‘alahu mina assaliheen
68|51|Wain yakadu allatheena kafaroo layuzliqoonaka biabsarihim lamma sami‘oo aththikra wayaqooloona innahu lamajnoon
68|52|Wama huwa illa thikrun lil‘alameen
69|1|Alhaqqa
69|2|Ma alhaqqa
69|3|Wama adraka ma alhaqqa
69|4|Kaththabat thamoodu wa‘adun bilqari‘a
69|5|Faamma thamoodu faohlikoo bittaghiya
69|6|Waamma ‘adun faohlikoo bireehin sarsarin ‘atiya
69|7|Sakhkharaha ‘alayhim sab‘a layalin wathamaniyata ayyamin husooman fatara alqawma feeha sar‘a kaannahum a‘jazu nakhlin khawiya
